+type EffectivePlayerView = Readonly<{
+  albumSlug: string;
+  album: AlbumPlayerBundle["album"];
+  tracks: PlayerTrack[];

+function resolveAccessIdentityKey(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
+    isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
+    userId: string | null | undefined;
+    sessionId: string | null | undefined;
+  }>,
+): string {
+  if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
+  if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
+
+  return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
+}
+
+function resolveEffectivePlayerView(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
+    stableView: StableView | null;
+    isBrowsingAlbum: boolean;
+  }>,
+): EffectivePlayerView {
+  const { bundle, stableView, isBrowsingAlbum } = params;
+  const shouldUseStableView = Boolean(
+    isBrowsingAlbum &&
+    stableView?.album &&
+    (!bundle.album || bundle.tracks.length === 0),
+  );
+
+  if (shouldUseStableView && stableView) {
+    return {
+      albumSlug: stableView.albumSlug,
+      album: stableView.album,
+      tracks: stableView.tracks,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    albumSlug: bundle.albumSlug,
+    album: bundle.album,
+    tracks: bundle.tracks,
+  };
+}

+function accessStatesEqual(
+  left: AccessState | null,
+  right: AccessState | null,
+): boolean {
+  if (!left || !right) return left === right;
+  return JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right);
+}
+
+function mergeAccessState(
+  previous: AccessState | null,
+  next: AccessState | null,
+): AccessState | null {
+  return accessStatesEqual(previous, next) ? previous : next;
+}
+
+function isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code: string): boolean {
+  return (
+    code === "AUTH_REQUIRED" ||
+    code === "CAP_REACHED" ||
+    code === "ANON_CAP_REACHED"
+  );
+}

+function hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(
+  player: PlayerApi,
+  pendingInAlbum: boolean,
+): boolean {
+  return (
+    player.playRequestPending ||
+    player.intent === "play" ||
+    player.status === "loading" ||
+    player.status === "playing" ||
+    player.status === "paused" ||
+    pendingInAlbum
+  );
+}
+
+function reconcilePlayerWithAccessDecision(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    player: PlayerApi;
+    decision: AccessState;
+    recordingIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
+    albumKey: string | null;
+  }>,
+): void {
+  const { player, decision, recordingIds, albumKey } = params;
+
+  if (decision.allowed) {
+    if (player.lastError) player.clearError();
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const currentInAlbum = Boolean(
+    player.current?.recordingId && recordingIds.has(player.current.recordingId),
+  );
+  const pendingInAlbum = Boolean(
+    player.pendingRecordingId && recordingIds.has(player.pendingRecordingId),
+  );
+  const queueIsAlbum = Boolean(albumKey && player.queueContextId === albumKey);
+  const hasIntent = hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(player, pendingInAlbum);
+  const shouldBlockAlbum =
+    currentInAlbum || pendingInAlbum || (queueIsAlbum && hasIntent);
+
+  const code = typeof decision.code === "string" ? decision.code.trim() : "";
+  if (!shouldBlockAlbum || (isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code) && !hasIntent)) return;
+
+  try {
+    player.pause();
+  } catch {
+    // ignore
+  }
+
+  if (player.status === "loading") {
+    player.setStatusExternal("idle");
+  }
+}
+
+function accessFailureFallback(catalogueId: string): AccessState {
+  return {
+    forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
+    allowed: true,
+    embargoed: false,
+    releaseAt: null,
+    code: "ACCESS_CHECK_ERROR",
+    reason: "Access check failed (client).",
+    corr: null,
+    shareTokenAccess: null,
+    sharePlaybackContext: null,
+    sharePlaybackScopeId: null,
+  };
+}
+
+function resolveAccessForAlbum(
+  catalogueId: string | null,
+  access: AccessState | null,
+): AccessState | null {
+  if (!catalogueId) return null;
+  if (access?.forCatalogueId !== catalogueId) return null;
+  return access;
+}

+function isPlayerPlayingish(player: PlayerApi): boolean {
+  return (
+    player.status === "playing" ||
+    player.status === "loading" ||
+    player.playRequestPending
+  );
+}

+type PlaybackAccessUiState = Readonly<{
+  accessKnown: boolean;
+  isPlaybackGate: boolean;
+  canPlay: boolean;
+  canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function derivePlaybackAccessUiState(
+  tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[],
+  accessForAlbum: AccessState | null,
+): PlaybackAccessUiState {
+  const accessKnown = accessForAlbum !== null;
+  const hasPlayableTracks = tracks.length > 0;
+  const isPlaybackGate = Boolean(
+    hasPlayableTracks &&
+    accessKnown &&
+    accessForAlbum?.allowed === false &&
+    isPlaybackGateCode(accessForAlbum.code),
+  );
+  const canPlay = Boolean(
+    hasPlayableTracks && accessKnown && accessForAlbum?.allowed !== false,
+  );
+
+  return {
+    accessKnown,
+    isPlaybackGate,
+    canPlay,
+    canAttemptPlay: canPlay || isPlaybackGate,
+  };
+}
+
+type EmbargoUiState = Readonly<{
+  releaseAtMs: number;
+  showEmbargo: boolean;
+  showShareTokenEmbargoAccess: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function deriveEmbargoUiState(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    embargo: NonNullable<AlbumPlayerBundle["album"]>["embargo"];
+    accessForAlbum: AccessState | null;
+    shareToken: string | null;
+  }>,
+): EmbargoUiState {
+  const releaseAtMs = params.embargo?.releaseAt
+    ? Date.parse(params.embargo.releaseAt)
+    : Number.NaN;
+  const accessResolved = params.accessForAlbum !== null;
+  const accessAllowsEmbargoBypass = params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true;
+  const showEmbargo = Boolean(
+    params.embargo?.embargoed &&
+    Number.isFinite(releaseAtMs) &&
+    !accessAllowsEmbargoBypass &&
+    (!params.shareToken || accessResolved),
+  );
+  const showShareTokenEmbargoAccess = Boolean(
+    params.embargo?.embargoed &&
+    params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true &&
+    params.accessForAlbum.shareTokenAccess,
+  );
+
+  return { releaseAtMs, showEmbargo, showShareTokenEmbargoAccess };
+}

+function isAlbumPlaybackActive(
+  albumKey: string | null,
+  queueContextId: string | undefined,
+): boolean {
+  return Boolean(albumKey && queueContextId === albumKey);
+}

+type AlbumTransportState = Readonly<{
+  albumIdx: number;
+  prevDisabled: boolean;
+  nextDisabled: boolean;
+}>;
+
+function deriveAlbumTransportState(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    currentRecordingId: string;
+    tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[];
+    transportLock: boolean;
+  }>,
+): AlbumTransportState {
+  const albumIdx = params.currentRecordingId
+    ? params.tracks.findIndex(
+        (track) => track.recordingId === params.currentRecordingId,
+      )
+    : -1;
+
+  if (albumIdx < 0) {
+    return { albumIdx, prevDisabled: true, nextDisabled: true };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    albumIdx,
+    prevDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === 0,
+    nextDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === params.tracks.length - 1,
+  };
+}
+
+function isCoarsePointerDevice(): boolean {
+  if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
+
+  try {
+    return window.matchMedia?.("(pointer: coarse)").matches ?? false;
+  } catch {
+    return "ontouchstart" in window;
+  }
+}
+
+function trackRowColors(
+  canPlay: boolean,
+  isCurrent: boolean,
+): Readonly<{ titleColor: string; subColor: string }> {
+  if (!canPlay) {
+    return {
+      titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.38)",
+      subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.32)",
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (isCurrent) {
+    return {
+      titleColor: "var(--accent)",
+      subColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 70%, rgba(255,255,255,0.70))",
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
+    subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.70)",
+  };
+}
+
+function trackRowRadius(index: number, trackCount: number): string {
+  if (index === 0) return "14px 14px 0 0";
+  if (index === trackCount - 1) return "0 0 14px 14px";
+  return "0";
+}
+
+type TrackRowView = Readonly<{
+  isCurrent: boolean;
+  shimmerTitle: boolean;
+  isNowPlaying: boolean;
+  titleColor: string;
+  subColor: string;
+  restingBackground: string;
+  rowRadius: string;
+  dataReason: string;
+  cursor: "pointer" | "default";
+  opacity: number;
+}>;
+
+function deriveTrackRowView(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    track: PlayerTrack;
+    index: number;
+    trackCount: number;
+    currentRecordingId: string | undefined;
+    pendingRecordingId: string | undefined;
+    playerStatus: PlayerApi["status"];
+    playerIntent: PlayerApi["intent"];
+    loadingReason: PlayerApi["loadingReason"];
+    canPlay: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    isSelected: boolean;
+  }>,
+): TrackRowView {
+  const isCurrent = params.currentRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
+  const isPending = params.pendingRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
+  const shimmerTitle =
+    params.playerStatus === "loading" && isPending && !isCurrent;
+  const isNowPlaying = Boolean(
+    isCurrent &&
+    (params.playerStatus === "playing" ||
+      params.playerStatus === "loading" ||
+      params.playerIntent === "play"),
+  );
+  const { titleColor, subColor } = trackRowColors(params.canPlay, isCurrent);
+  const baseBackground = params.isSelected
+    ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)"
+    : "transparent";
+  const restingBackground =
+    isCurrent && !params.isSelected ? "transparent" : baseBackground;
+  const dataReason =
+    isCurrent && params.playerStatus === "loading"
+      ? (params.loadingReason ?? "")
+      : "";
+
+  return {
+    isCurrent,
+    shimmerTitle,
+    isNowPlaying,
+    titleColor,
+    subColor,
+    restingBackground,
+    rowRadius: trackRowRadius(params.index, params.trackCount),
+    dataReason,
+    cursor: params.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default",
+    opacity: params.canPlay ? 1 : 0.75,
+  };
+}
+
+function trackPlayLabel(track: PlayerTrack, index: number): string {

+  const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;
+  return `Play ${subject}`;

+}

+function TransportControls(
+  props: Readonly<{
+    prevDisabled: boolean;
+    nextDisabled: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    canPlay: boolean;
+    playLock: boolean;
+    playingThisAlbum: boolean;
+    onDownload: () => void;
+    onPrevious: () => void;
+    onTogglePlay: () => void;
+    onNext: () => void;
+    onShare: () => void;
+  }>,
+) {
+  const playLabel = props.playingThisAlbum ? "Pause" : "Play";
+  const playClick =
+    props.canAttemptPlay && !props.playLock ? props.onTogglePlay : undefined;
+  const playCursor = props.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default";

+function TrackIndexCell(
+  props: Readonly<{
+    index: number;
+    track: PlayerTrack;
+    isNowPlaying: boolean;
+    canAttemptPlay: boolean;
+    isCurrent: boolean;
+    subColor: string;
+    onInlinePlay: (track: PlayerTrack) => void;
+  }>,
+) {
+  let content: React.ReactNode = (
+    <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
+  );
+
+  if (props.isNowPlaying) {
+    content = <NowPlayingPip />;
+  } else if (props.canAttemptPlay && !props.isCurrent) {
+    content = (
+      <span className="afTrackIndexSwap">
+        <span className="afTrackIndexNumber">{props.index + 1}</span>
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          className="afTrackInlinePlay"
+          aria-label={trackPlayLabel(props.track, props.index)}
+          title="Play"
+          onClick={(event) => {
+            event.preventDefault();
+            event.stopPropagation();
+            props.onInlinePlay(props.track);
+          }}
+        >
+          <TrackInlinePlayIcon />
+        </button>
+      </span>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      style={{
+        fontSize: 12,
+        opacity: 0.9,
+        display: "flex",
+        alignItems: "center",
+        gap: 6,
+        color: props.subColor,
+        paddingLeft: 12,
+        justifyContent: "flex-start",
+        position: "relative",
+        zIndex: 1,
+        pointerEvents: "none",
+      }}
+    >
+      {content}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

-export function PlayerStateProvider(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
+function resolvePlaybackAccessIdentityKey(
+  params: Readonly<{
+    clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
+    isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
+    userId: string | null | undefined;
+    sessionId: string | null | undefined;
+  }>,
+): string {
+  if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
+  if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
+
+  return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
+}
+
+function markCurrentTrackLoading(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  loadingReason: "attach",
+): PlayerState {
+  const current = state.current;
+  if (!current) return state;
+
+  return {
+    ...state,
+    status: "loading",
+    loadingReason,
+    positionMs: 0,
+    intent: "play",
+    intentAtMs: Date.now(),
+    pendingRecordingId: current.recordingId,
+    selectedRecordingId: current.recordingId,
+  };
+}
+
+function markNextTrackLoading(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  track: PlayerTrack,
+  loadingReason: "token" | "attach",
+): PlayerState {
+  const hydratedTrack = hydrateTrack(track, state.durationByRecordingId);
+
+  return {
+    ...state,
+    current: hydratedTrack,
+    status: "loading",
+    loadingReason,
+    positionMs: 0,
+    intent: "play",
+    intentAtMs: Date.now(),
+    pendingRecordingId: hydratedTrack.recordingId,
+    selectedRecordingId: hydratedTrack.recordingId,
+  };
+}
+
+function transitionForwardState(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  nextTrackLoadingReason: "token" | "attach",
+  terminalIntent: "pause" | null,
+): PlayerState {
+  const current = state.current;
+  if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return state;
+
+  if (state.repeat === "one") {
+    return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
+  }
+
+  const index = state.queue.findIndex(
+    (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
+  );
+  const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
+  const nextTrack = state.queue[currentIndex + 1];
+
+  if (nextTrack) {
+    return markNextTrackLoading(state, nextTrack, nextTrackLoadingReason);
+  }
+
+  if (state.repeat === "all") {
+    const firstTrack = state.queue[0];
+    if (firstTrack) {
+      return markNextTrackLoading(state, firstTrack, nextTrackLoadingReason);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ...state,
+    status: "paused",
+    intent: terminalIntent,
+    intentAtMs: terminalIntent === "pause" ? Date.now() : undefined,
+    positionMs: 0,
+    pendingSeekMs: 0,
+    seeking: true,
+    seekNonce: state.seekNonce + 1,
+    loadingReason: undefined,
+    pendingRecordingId: undefined,
+  };
+}
+
+function transitionPreviousState(state: PlayerState): PlayerState {
+  const current = state.current;
+  if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return state;
+
+  if (state.status === "playing" && state.positionMs > 3000) {
+    return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
+  }
+
+  const index = state.queue.findIndex(
+    (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
+  );
+  const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
+  const previousTrack = state.queue[currentIndex - 1];
+
+  if (previousTrack) {
+    return markNextTrackLoading(state, previousTrack, "token");
+  }
+
+  if (state.repeat === "all") {
+    const lastTrack = state.queue.at(-1);
+    if (lastTrack) {
+      return markNextTrackLoading(state, lastTrack, "token");
+    }
+  }
+
+  return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
+}
+
+type QueueContextStatePatch = Partial<
+  Pick<
+    PlayerState,
+    | "queueContextId"
+    | "queueContextSlug"
+    | "queueContextTitle"
+    | "queueContextArtist"
+    | "queueContextArtworkUrl"
+    | "queueSharePlaybackContext"
+    | "queueSharePlaybackScopeId"
+  >
+>;
+
+function normalizedQueueShareValue(
+  value: string | null | undefined,
+): string | null {
+  if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
+  return value.trim() || null;
+}
+
+function buildQueueContextStatePatch(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  opts: QueueContext | undefined,
+): QueueContextStatePatch {
+  const patch: QueueContextStatePatch = {};
+  const contextId = typeof opts?.contextId === "string" ? opts.contextId : "";
+  const contextSlug =
+    typeof opts?.contextSlug === "string" ? opts.contextSlug.trim() : "";
+  const contextTitle =
+    typeof opts?.contextTitle === "string" ? opts.contextTitle.trim() : "";
+  const contextArtist =
+    typeof opts?.contextArtist === "string" ? opts.contextArtist.trim() : "";
+
+  if (contextId.length > 0) patch.queueContextId = contextId;
+  if (contextSlug) patch.queueContextSlug = contextSlug;
+  if (contextTitle) patch.queueContextTitle = contextTitle;
+  if (contextArtist) patch.queueContextArtist = contextArtist;
+
+  patch.queueContextArtworkUrl =
+    opts?.artworkUrl !== undefined
+      ? (opts.artworkUrl ?? null)
+      : (state.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null);
+
+  const optionBag = opts ?? {};
+
+  if (Object.hasOwn(optionBag, "sharePlaybackContext")) {
+    patch.queueSharePlaybackContext = normalizedQueueShareValue(
+      opts?.sharePlaybackContext,
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (Object.hasOwn(optionBag, "sharePlaybackScopeId")) {
+    patch.queueSharePlaybackScopeId = normalizedQueueShareValue(
+      opts?.sharePlaybackScopeId,
+    );
+  }
+
+  return patch;
+}
+
+function findRequestedInitialCurrent(
+  queue: readonly PlayerTrack[],
+  opts: QueueContext | undefined,
+): PlayerTrack | null {
+  const recordingId =
+    typeof opts?.initialCurrentRecordingId === "string"
+      ? opts.initialCurrentRecordingId.trim()
+      : "";
+
+  if (!recordingId) return null;
+
+  return (
+    queue.find((track) => track.recordingId === recordingId) ?? null
+  );
+}
+
+type QueueSelectionState = Pick<
+  PlayerState,
+  "positionMs" | "selectedRecordingId" | "pendingRecordingId"
+>;
+
+function resolveQueueSelectionState(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  nextCurrent: PlayerTrack | undefined,
+  requestedInitialCurrent: PlayerTrack | null,
+): QueueSelectionState {
+  if (requestedInitialCurrent) {
+    return {
+      positionMs: 0,
+      selectedRecordingId: requestedInitialCurrent.recordingId,
+      pendingRecordingId: undefined,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    positionMs: state.current ? state.positionMs : 0,
+    selectedRecordingId:
+      state.selectedRecordingId ?? nextCurrent?.recordingId,
+    pendingRecordingId: state.pendingRecordingId,
+  };
+}
+
+function applyQueueState(
+  state: PlayerState,
+  tracks: PlayerTrack[],
+  opts: QueueContext | undefined,
+): PlayerState {
+  const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
+    state.durationByRecordingId,
+    tracks,
+  );

+    state.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
+    tracks,
+  );
+  const hydratedQueue = hydrateTracks(tracks, nextDurationByRecordingId);

+    hydratedQueue,
+    opts,
+  );
+  const nextCurrentRaw =
+    requestedInitialCurrent ?? state.current ?? hydratedQueue[0];
+  const nextCurrent = nextCurrentRaw
+    ? hydrateTrack(nextCurrentRaw, nextDurationByRecordingId)
+    : undefined;
+
+  return {
+    ...state,
+    ...buildQueueContextStatePatch(state, opts),
+    durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
+    assetDurationByPlaybackId: nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId,
+    queue: hydratedQueue,
+    current: nextCurrent,
+    ...resolveQueueSelectionState(
+      state,
+      nextCurrent,
+      requestedInitialCurrent,
+    ),
+  };

    4 | import React from "react";

   10 | export default function PlayerHost({
   11 |   children,
   12 | }: {
   13 |   children: React.ReactNode;
   14 | }) {
   15 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   16 |     if (
   17 |       typeof window === "undefined" ||
   18 |       process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_NAV_DEBUG !== "1"
   19 |     ) {
   20 |       return;
   21 |     }
   22 | 
   23 |     const tag = "[NAV]";
   24 |     const origPush = window.history.pushState.bind(window.history);
   25 |     const origReplace = window.history.replaceState.bind(window.history);
   26 | 
   27 |     const log = (
   28 |       kind: "pushState" | "replaceState",
   29 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   30 |     ) => {
   31 |       try {
   32 |         const href = String(url ?? "");
   33 |         const from = window.location.pathname;
   34 | 
   35 |         const toPath = href.startsWith("http")
   36 |           ? new URL(href).pathname
   37 |           : (href.split("?")[0] || "").trim();
   38 | 
   39 |         if (
   40 |           (from === "/portal" && toPath === "/journal") ||
   41 |           href.includes("/journal") ||
   42 |           href.includes("/portal") ||
   43 |           // legacy (avoid breaking old in-flight links/history)
   44 |           href.includes("/posts") ||
   45 |           href.includes("/extras")
   46 |         ) {
   47 |           console.log(tag, kind, { from, href }, new Error().stack);
   48 |         }
   49 |       } catch {
   50 |         // ignore
   51 |       }
   52 |     };
   53 | 
   54 |     window.history.pushState = function (
   55 |       data: unknown,
   56 |       unused: string,
   57 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   58 |     ): void {
   59 |       log("pushState", url);
   60 |       origPush(data, unused, url);
   61 |     };
   62 | 
   63 |     window.history.replaceState = function (
   64 |       data: unknown,
   65 |       unused: string,
   66 |       url?: string | URL | null,
   67 |     ): void {
   68 |       log("replaceState", url);
   69 |       origReplace(data, unused, url);
   70 |     };
   71 | 
   72 |     const onPop = () => {
   73 |       console.log(tag, "popstate", { href: window.location.href });
   74 |     };
   75 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   76 | 
   77 |     return () => {
   78 |       window.history.pushState = origPush;
   79 |       window.history.replaceState = origReplace;
   80 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
   81 |     };
   82 |   }, []);
   83 | 
   84 |   return (
   85 |     <PlayerStateProvider>
   86 |       <AudioEngine />
   87 |       <StageInlineHost />
   88 |       <TrackTitleSync fallbackLeaf="God Defend" mode="track" />
   89 |       {children}
   90 |     </PlayerStateProvider>
   91 |   );
   92 | }

   79 | function baseJson<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
   80 |   body: T,
   81 |   opts: { correlationId: string; status?: number; anonId?: string },
   82 | ) {
   83 |   const res = NextResponse.json(body, { status: opts.status ?? 200 });
   84 |   // ✅ micro-cache to absorb double-invokes / fast remounts
   85 |   // safe because access state doesn't change multiple times per second.
   86 |   res.headers.set(

  131 | async function resolveAnonymousAccess(

  133 |   context: AccessRequestContext,
  134 | ): Promise<NextResponse> {

  214 |         sharePlaybackContext,
  215 |         sharePlaybackScopeId: sharePlaybackContext ? albumScopeId : null,
  216 |       },
  217 |       { correlationId },
  218 |     );
  219 |   }
  220 | 
  221 |   const distinctCompleted = await countAnonDistinctCompletedTracks({
  222 |     anonId,
  223 |     sinceDays: ANON_PLAYBACK_POLICY.windowDays,
  224 |   });
  225 | 
  226 |   const cap = {
  227 |     used: distinctCompleted,
  228 |     max: ANON_PLAYBACK_POLICY.distinctTrackCap,
  229 |     windowDays: ANON_PLAYBACK_POLICY.windowDays,
  230 |   };
  231 | 
  232 |   if (
  233 |     hasReachedAnonPlaybackCap({
  234 |       distinctCompletedTracks: distinctCompleted,
  235 |     })
  236 |   ) {
  237 |     return anonJsonWithId(
  238 |       anonId,
  239 |       {
  240 |         ok: true,
  241 |         allowed: false,
  242 |         embargoed: false,
  243 |         releaseAt,
  244 |         code: "ANON_CAP_REACHED",
  245 |         action: "login" as const,
  246 |         reason: "Enter your email address to continue listening.",
  247 |         correlationId,
  248 |         redeemed: null,
  249 |         cap,
  250 |       },
  251 |       { correlationId },
  252 |     );
  253 |   }
  254 | 
  255 |   return anonJsonWithId(
  256 |     anonId,
  257 |     {
  258 |       ok: true,
  259 |       allowed: true,
  260 |       embargoed: false,
  261 |       releaseAt,
  262 |       code: null,
  263 |       action: null,
  264 |       reason: null,
  265 |       correlationId,
  266 |       redeemed: null,
  267 |       cap,
  268 |     },
  269 |     { correlationId },
  270 |   );
  271 | }

  273 | async function resolveAdminDebugOverride(
  274 |   memberId: string,
  275 |   correlationId: string,
  276 | ): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  277 |   const debug = await readAdminDebugCookie();
  278 |   if (!debug) return null;
  279 | 
  280 |   const isAdmin = (
  281 |     await checkAccess(
  282 |       memberId,
  283 |       { kind: "global", required: [ENTITLEMENTS.ADMIN] },
  284 |       { log: false },
  285 |     )
  286 |   ).allowed;
  287 | 
  288 |   if (!isAdmin) return null;
  289 | 
  290 |   const force = String(debug.force ?? "none");
  291 | 
  292 |   switch (force) {
  293 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
  294 |       return baseJson(
  295 |         {
  296 |           ok: true,
  297 |           allowed: false,
  298 |           embargoed: false,
  299 |           releaseAt: null,
  300 |           code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  301 |           action: "login",
  302 |           reason: "Sign in required",
  303 |           correlationId,
  304 |           redeemed: null,
  305 |         },
  306 |         { correlationId },
  307 |       );
  308 | 
  309 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
  310 |       return baseJson(
  311 |         {
  312 |           ok: true,
  313 |           allowed: false,
  314 |           embargoed: false,
  315 |           releaseAt: null,
  316 |           code: "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED",
  317 |           action: "subscribe",
  318 |           reason: "Entitlement required",
  319 |           correlationId,
  320 |           redeemed: null,
  321 |         },
  322 |         { correlationId },
  323 |       );
  324 | 
  325 |     case "ANON_CAP_REACHED":
  326 |       return baseJson(
  327 |         {
  328 |           ok: true,
  329 |           allowed: false,
  330 |           embargoed: false,
  331 |           releaseAt: null,
  332 |           code: "ANON_CAP_REACHED",
  333 |           action: "login",
  334 |           reason: "Anon cap reached",
  335 |           correlationId,
  336 |           redeemed: null,
  337 |         },
  338 |         { correlationId },
  339 |       );
  340 | 
  341 |     case "EMBARGOED":
  342 |       return baseJson(
  343 |         {
  344 |           ok: true,
  345 |           allowed: false,
  346 |           embargoed: true,
  347 |           releaseAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  348 |           code: "EMBARGOED",
  349 |           action: "wait",
  350 |           reason: "Embargoed",
  351 |           correlationId,
  352 |           redeemed: null,
  353 |         },
  354 |         { correlationId },
  355 |       );
  356 | 
  357 |     default:
  358 |       return null;
  359 |   }
  360 | }

  362 | async function resolveMemberShareState(params: {
  363 |   memberId: string;
  364 |   albumScopeId: string;
  365 |   shareToken: string;
  366 | }): Promise<MemberShareState> {
  367 |   if (!params.shareToken) {
  368 |     return {
  369 |       redeemed: null,
  370 |       shareTokenAllowsAccess: false,
  371 |       shareTokenAccess: null,
  372 |       sharePlaybackContext: null,
  373 |     };
  374 |   }
  375 | 
  376 |   const redemption = await redeemShareTokenForMember({
  377 |     token: params.shareToken,
  378 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  379 |     expectedScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  380 |     resourceKind: "album",
  381 |     resourceId: params.albumScopeId,
  382 |     action: "redeem",
  383 |   });
  384 | 
  385 |   if (!redemption.ok) {
  386 |     return {
  387 |       redeemed: { ok: false, code: redemption.code },

  389 |       shareTokenAccess: null,
  390 |       sharePlaybackContext: null,
  391 |     };

  399 |         memberId: params.memberId,
  400 |         anonId: null,
  401 |       })
  402 |     : null;
  403 | 
  404 |   return {
  405 |     redeemed: { ok: true },
  406 |     shareTokenAllowsAccess: true,
  407 |     shareTokenAccess: redemption.shareTokenAccess,
  408 |     sharePlaybackContext,
  409 |   };

  412 | async function resolveMemberEmbargoGate(params: {
  413 |   memberId: string;
  414 |   albumScopeId: string;
  415 |   policy: AlbumPolicy | null;
  416 |   embargoed: boolean;
  417 |   releaseAt: string | null;
  418 |   shareTokenAllowsAccess: boolean;
  419 |   correlationId: string;
  420 |   redeemed: RedeemedState;
  421 | }): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  422 |   if (!params.embargoed || params.shareTokenAllowsAccess) {
  423 |     return null;
  424 |   }
  425 | 
  426 |   const shareGrant = await checkAccess(
  427 |     params.memberId,
  428 |     {
  429 |       kind: "album",
  430 |       albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  431 |       required: [ENTITLEMENTS.ALBUM_SHARE_GRANT],
  432 |     },
  433 |     {
  434 |       log: true,
  435 |       action: ACCESS_ACTIONS.ACCESS_CHECK,
  436 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  437 |     },
  438 |   );
  439 | 
  440 |   if (shareGrant.allowed) {
  441 |     return null;
  442 |   }
  443 | 
  444 |   const { policy } = params;
  445 | 
  446 |   if (policy?.earlyAccessEnabled && policy.earlyAccessTiers.length > 0) {
  447 |     const keys = await listCurrentEntitlementKeys(params.memberId);
  448 |     const keySet = new Set(keys);
  449 |     const allowedTierKeys = policy.earlyAccessTiers.map(
  450 |       (tier) => `tier_${tier}`,
  451 |     );
  452 | 
  453 |     if (allowedTierKeys.some((key) => keySet.has(key))) {
  454 |       return null;
  455 |     }
  456 | 
  457 |     return baseJson(
  458 |       {
  459 |         ok: true,
  460 |         allowed: false,
  461 |         embargoed: true,
  462 |         releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  463 |         code: "EMBARGO",
  464 |         action: "subscribe" satisfies Action,
  465 |         reason: "This album is not released yet. Upgrade for early access.",
  466 |         correlationId: params.correlationId,
  467 |         redeemed: params.redeemed,
  468 |       },
  469 |       { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  470 |     );
  471 |   }
  472 | 
  473 |   return baseJson(
  474 |     {
  475 |       ok: true,
  476 |       allowed: false,
  477 |       embargoed: true,
  478 |       releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  479 |       code: "EMBARGO",
  480 |       action: "wait" satisfies Action,
  481 |       reason: "This album is not released yet.",
  482 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  483 |       redeemed: params.redeemed,
  484 |     },
  485 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  486 |   );
  487 | }
  488 | 
  489 | async function resolveMemberTierGate(params: {
  490 |   memberId: string;
  491 |   policy: AlbumPolicy | null;
  492 |   releaseAt: string | null;
  493 |   correlationId: string;
  494 |   redeemed: RedeemedState;
  495 | }): Promise<NextResponse | null> {
  496 |   const minTier = params.policy?.minTierForPlayback;
  497 | 
  498 |   if (!minTier) {
  499 |     return null;
  500 |   }
  501 | 
  502 |   const keys = await listCurrentEntitlementKeys(params.memberId);
  503 |   const keySet = new Set(keys);
  504 |   const requiredTierKeys = tierAtOrAbove(minTier);
  505 | 
  506 |   if (requiredTierKeys.some((key) => keySet.has(key))) {
  507 |     return null;
  508 |   }
  509 | 
  510 |   return baseJson(
  511 |     {
  512 |       ok: true,
  513 |       allowed: false,
  514 |       embargoed: false,
  515 |       releaseAt: params.releaseAt,
  516 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  517 |       action: "subscribe" satisfies Action,
  518 |       reason: `This album requires ${minTier} tier or higher.`,
  519 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  520 |       redeemed: params.redeemed,
  521 |     },
  522 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  523 |   );
  524 | }
  525 | 
  526 | async function resolveMemberAccess(params: {
  527 |   memberId: string;
  528 |   albumId: string;
  529 |   albumScopeId: string;
  530 |   correlationId: string;
  531 |   shareState: MemberShareState;
  532 | }): Promise<NextResponse> {
  533 |   const policy = await getAlbumPolicyByAlbumId(params.albumId);
  534 |   const releaseAt = policy?.releaseAt ?? null;
  535 |   const embargoed = isEmbargoed(policy);
  536 | 
  537 |   const embargoResponse = await resolveMemberEmbargoGate({
  538 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  539 |     albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  540 |     policy,
  541 |     embargoed,
  542 |     releaseAt,
  543 |     shareTokenAllowsAccess: params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess,
  544 |     correlationId: params.correlationId,
  545 |     redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  546 |   });
  547 | 
  548 |   if (embargoResponse) {
  549 |     return embargoResponse;
  550 |   }
  551 | 
  552 |   const tierResponse = await resolveMemberTierGate({
  553 |     memberId: params.memberId,
  554 |     policy,
  555 |     releaseAt,
  556 |     correlationId: params.correlationId,
  557 |     redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  558 |   });
  559 | 
  560 |   if (tierResponse) {
  561 |     return tierResponse;
  562 |   }
  563 | 
  564 |   const decision = await checkAccess(
  565 |     params.memberId,
  566 |     {
  567 |       kind: "album",
  568 |       albumScopeId: params.albumScopeId,
  569 |       required: [ENTITLEMENTS.PLAY_ALBUM],
  570 |     },
  571 |     {
  572 |       log: true,
  573 |       action: ACCESS_ACTIONS.ACCESS_CHECK,
  574 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  575 |     },
  576 |   );
  577 | 
  578 |   const allowed = Boolean(
  579 |     decision.allowed || params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess,
  580 |   );
  581 | 
  582 |   return baseJson(
  583 |     {
  584 |       ok: true,
  585 |       allowed,
  586 |       embargoed: embargoed && !allowed,
  587 |       releaseAt,
  588 |       code: allowed ? null : "NO_ENTITLEMENT",
  589 |       action: allowed ? null : ("subscribe" satisfies Action),
  590 |       reason: allowed ? null : "reason" in decision ? decision.reason : null,
  591 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  592 |       redeemed: params.shareState.redeemed,
  593 |       shareTokenAccess:
  594 |         allowed && params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess
  595 |           ? params.shareState.shareTokenAccess
  596 |           : null,
  597 |       sharePlaybackContext:
  598 |         allowed && params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess
  599 |           ? params.shareState.sharePlaybackContext
  600 |           : null,
  601 |       sharePlaybackScopeId:
  602 |         allowed &&
  603 |         params.shareState.shareTokenAllowsAccess &&
  604 |         params.shareState.sharePlaybackContext
  605 |           ? params.albumScopeId
  606 |           : null,
  607 |     },
  608 |     { correlationId: params.correlationId },
  609 |   );
  610 | }

  612 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  613 |   const correlationId = newCorrelationId();
  614 |   const { userId } = await auth();
  615 | 
  616 |   const url = new URL(req.url);
  617 |   const albumId = normalizeAlbumId(
  618 |     (url.searchParams.get("albumId") ?? "").trim(),
  619 |   );
  620 |   const shareToken = (
  621 |     url.searchParams.get("st") ??
  622 |     url.searchParams.get("share") ??
  623 |     ""
  624 |   ).trim();
  625 | 
  626 |   if (!albumId) {
  627 |     return baseJson(
  628 |       {
  629 |         ok: true,
  630 |         allowed: false,
  631 |         embargoed: false,
  632 |         releaseAt: null,
  633 |         code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  634 |         action: null,
  635 |         reason: "Missing albumId",
  636 |         correlationId,
  637 |         redeemed: null,
  638 |       },
  639 |       { correlationId },
  640 |     );
  641 |   }
  642 | 
  643 |   const albumScopeId = `alb:${albumId}`;
  644 | 
  645 |   if (!userId) {
  646 |     return resolveAnonymousAccess(req, {
  647 |       albumId,
  648 |       albumScopeId,
  649 |       shareToken,
  650 |       correlationId,
  651 |     });
  652 |   }
  653 | 
  654 |   const memberId = await getMemberIdByClerkUserId(userId);
  655 | 
  656 |   if (!memberId) {
  657 |     const anonForAuthed = shareToken ? ensureAnonId(req).anonId : undefined;
  658 | 
  659 |     return baseJson(
  660 |       {
  661 |         ok: true,
  662 |         allowed: false,
  663 |         embargoed: false,
  664 |         releaseAt: null,
  665 |         code: "PROVISIONING",
  666 |         action: "wait" satisfies Action,
  667 |         reason: "Member profile is still being created",
  668 |         correlationId,
  669 |         redeemed: null,
  670 |       },
  671 |       { correlationId, anonId: anonForAuthed },
  672 |     );
  673 |   }
  674 | 
  675 |   const debugResponse = await resolveAdminDebugOverride(
  676 |     memberId,
  677 |     correlationId,
  678 |   );
  679 | 
  680 |   if (debugResponse) {
  681 |     return debugResponse;
  682 |   }
  683 | 
  684 |   const shareState = await resolveMemberShareState({
  685 |     memberId,
  686 |     albumScopeId,
  687 |     shareToken,
  688 |   });
  689 | 
  690 |   return resolveMemberAccess({
  691 |     memberId,
  692 |     albumId,
  693 |     albumScopeId,
  694 |     correlationId,
  695 |     shareState,
  696 |   });
  697 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/MiniPlayerHost.tsx =====
    1 | "use client";
    2 | 
    3 | import React from "react";
    4 | import { usePlayer } from "@/app/home/player/PlayerState";
    5 | import MiniPlayer from "./player/MiniPlayer";
    6 | 
    7 | export default function MiniPlayerHost(props: { onExpand: () => void }) {
    8 |   const { onExpand } = props;
    9 |   const p = usePlayer();
   10 | 
   11 |   const intent = p.intent;
   12 |   const status = p.status;
   13 |   const playRequestPending = p.playRequestPending;
   14 |   const current = p.current;
   15 |   const queueLen = p.queue.length;
   16 | 
   17 |   const [miniActive, setMiniActive] = React.useState(() => {
   18 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
   19 |     return window.sessionStorage.getItem("af:miniActive") === "1";
   20 |   });
   21 | 
   22 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   23 |     const shouldActivate =
   24 |       playRequestPending ||
   25 |       intent === "play" ||
   26 |       status === "playing" ||
   27 |       status === "paused" ||
   28 |       Boolean(current) ||
   29 |       queueLen > 0;
   30 | 
   31 |     if (!miniActive && shouldActivate) {
   32 |       setMiniActive(true);
   33 |       try {
   34 |         window.sessionStorage.setItem("af:miniActive", "1");
   35 |       } catch {}
   36 |     }
   37 |   }, [miniActive, intent, status, playRequestPending, current, queueLen]);
   38 | 
   39 |   if (!miniActive) return null;
   40 | 
   41 |   return (
   42 |     <MiniPlayer
   43 |       onExpand={onExpand}
   44 |       artworkUrl={p.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null}
   45 |     />
   46 |   );
   47 | }

  168 | 
  169 |   return (
  170 |     (isAppleHandheld || isTouchCapableIpadDesktopUa) && /AppleWebKit/i.test(ua)
  171 |   );
  172 | }
  173 | 
  174 | function shouldUseStaticM4a(): boolean {
  175 |   return isAppleMobileWebKit();
  176 | }
  177 | 
  178 | function bufferedAheadSeconds(media: HTMLMediaElement): number {
  179 |   try {
  180 |     const currentTime = Math.max(0, media.currentTime);
  181 | 
  182 |     for (let index = 0; index < media.buffered.length; index += 1) {
  183 |       const start = media.buffered.start(index);
  184 |       const end = media.buffered.end(index);
  185 | 
  186 |       if (currentTime >= start && currentTime <= end) {
  187 |         return Math.max(0, end - currentTime);
  188 |       }
  189 |     }
  190 | 
  191 |     if (media.buffered.length > 0) {
  192 |       return Math.max(
  193 |         0,
  194 |         media.buffered.end(media.buffered.length - 1) - currentTime,
  195 |       );
  196 |     }
  197 |   } catch {}
  198 | 
  199 |   return 0;
  200 | }
  201 | 
  202 | function readFiniteMediaDurationMs(media: HTMLMediaElement): number {
  203 |   const durationSec = media.duration;
  204 | 
  205 |   return Number.isFinite(durationSec) && durationSec > 0
  206 |     ? Math.round(durationSec * 1000)
  207 |     : 0;
  208 | }
  209 | 
  210 | function isStaticM4aStandbyBuffered(media: HTMLMediaElement): boolean {
  211 |   return (
  212 |     media.readyState >= HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_FUTURE_DATA &&
  213 |     bufferedAheadSeconds(media) >= STATIC_M4A_STANDBY_MIN_BUFFER_AHEAD_SEC
  214 |   );
  215 | }

  217 | function newPlaybackSessionId(): string {
  218 |   if (
  219 |     typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
  220 |     typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
  221 |   ) {
  222 |     return crypto.randomUUID();
  223 |   }
  224 | 
  225 |   throw new Error("Unable to create secure playback session id");
  226 | }

  228 | function hasMediaSession(): boolean {
  229 |   return (
  230 |     typeof navigator !== "undefined" &&
  231 |     "mediaSession" in navigator &&
  232 |     typeof navigator.mediaSession !== "undefined"
  233 |   );
  234 | }

  236 | type MediaSessionArtwork = {
  237 |   src: string;
  238 |   sizes?: string;
  239 |   type?: string;
  240 | };
  241 | 
  242 | function getMediaSessionArtwork(
  243 |   rawArtworkUrl: string | null | undefined,
  244 | ): MediaSessionArtwork[] {
  245 |   const artworkUrl = (rawArtworkUrl ?? "").trim();
  246 | 
  247 |   if (!artworkUrl) return [];
  248 | 
  249 |   let sourceUrl: URL;
  250 | 
  251 |   try {
  252 |     sourceUrl = new URL(artworkUrl);
  253 |   } catch {
  254 |     return [];
  255 |   }
  256 | 
  257 |   // The album artwork currently comes from Sanity's CDN. Supplying several
  258 |   // correctly described JPEG variants gives iOS a clean, native-friendly
  259 |   // choice for Now Playing and the lock screen.
  260 |   if (sourceUrl.hostname !== "cdn.sanity.io") {
  261 |     return [{ src: sourceUrl.toString() }];
  262 |   }
  263 | 
  264 |   const sizes = [96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 1024];
  265 | 
  266 |   return sizes.map((size) => {
  267 |     const url = new URL(sourceUrl.toString());
  268 | 
  269 |     url.searchParams.set("w", String(size));
  270 |     url.searchParams.set("h", String(size));
  271 |     url.searchParams.set("fit", "crop");
  272 |     url.searchParams.set("crop", "center");
  273 |     url.searchParams.set("fm", "jpg");
  274 |     url.searchParams.set("q", "85");
  275 |     url.searchParams.set("cs", "srgb");
  276 | 
  277 |     return {
  278 |       src: url.toString(),
  279 |       sizes: `${size}x${size}`,
  280 |       type: "image/jpeg",
  281 |     };
  282 |   });
  283 | }

  285 | function audioDebugEnabled(): boolean {
  286 |   return process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_AUDIO_DEBUG === "1";
  287 | }
  288 | 
  289 | function audioDebugVerboseEnabled(): boolean {
  290 |   return process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_AUDIO_DEBUG_VERBOSE === "1";
  291 | }
  292 | 
  293 | function shouldSendAudioDebugEvent(event: string): boolean {
  294 |   if (audioDebugVerboseEnabled()) return true;
  295 | 
  296 |   return (
  297 |     event.includes("failed") ||
  298 |     event.includes("fatal") ||
  299 |     event.includes("rejected") ||
  300 |     event.includes("error") ||
  301 |     event.includes("missing") ||
  302 |     event.includes("unsupported") ||
  303 |     event === "album-session-failed" ||
  304 |     event === "standby-not-ready-fallback-state-advance" ||
  305 |     event === "standby-promote-failed-fallback-state-advance"
  306 |   );
  307 | }
  308 | 
  309 | const audioDebugSessionId =
  310 |   typeof crypto !== "undefined" && typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
  311 |     ? crypto.randomUUID()
  312 |     : `debug-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
  313 | 
  314 | const AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY = "af:audio-debug:v1";
  315 | const AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT = 240;
  316 | 
  317 | const audioDebugMemoryBuffer: AudioDebugEvent[] = [];
  318 | let audioDebugFlushTimer: number | null = null;
  319 | let audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  320 | 
  321 | function isStoredAudioDebugEvent(value: unknown): value is AudioDebugEvent {
  322 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  323 | 
  324 |   const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  325 | 
  326 |   return (
  327 |     typeof candidate.t === "number" &&
  328 |     Number.isFinite(candidate.t) &&
  329 |     typeof candidate.event === "string"
  330 |   );
  331 | }
  332 | 
  333 | function readPersistedAudioDebugEvents(): AudioDebugEvent[] | null {
  334 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  335 | 
  336 |   try {
  337 |     const raw = window.sessionStorage.getItem(AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY);
  338 | 
  339 |     if (!raw?.trim()) return [];
  340 | 
  341 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
  342 | 
  343 |     if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
  344 | 
  345 |     return parsed
  346 |       .filter(isStoredAudioDebugEvent)
  347 |       .slice(-AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT);
  348 |   } catch {
  349 |     return null;
  350 |   }
  351 | }
  352 | 
  353 | function writePersistedAudioDebugEvents(events: AudioDebugEvent[]): boolean {
  354 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
  355 | 
  356 |   try {
  357 |     window.sessionStorage.setItem(
  358 |       AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_KEY,
  359 |       JSON.stringify(events.slice(-AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT)),
  360 |     );
  361 |     return true;
  362 |   } catch {
  363 |     return false;
  364 |   }
  365 | }
  366 | 
  367 | function appendAudioDebugEvent(event: AudioDebugEvent): void {
  368 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  369 | 
  370 |   if (
  371 |     persisted !== null &&
  372 |     writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([...persisted, event])
  373 |   ) {
  374 |     return;
  375 |   }
  376 | 
  377 |   audioDebugMemoryBuffer.push(event);
  378 | 
  379 |   if (audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length > AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT) {
  380 |     audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(
  381 |       0,
  382 |       audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length - AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  383 |     );
  384 |   }
  385 | }
  386 | 
  387 | function takeAudioDebugEvents(): AudioDebugEvent[] {
  388 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  389 | 
  390 |   if (persisted !== null) {
  391 |     if (writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([])) {
  392 |       return persisted;
  393 |     }
  394 | 
  395 |     return persisted;
  396 |   }
  397 | 
  398 |   return audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(0, audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length);
  399 | }
  400 | 
  401 | function restoreAudioDebugEvents(events: AudioDebugEvent[]): void {
  402 |   if (!events.length) return;
  403 | 
  404 |   const persisted = readPersistedAudioDebugEvents();
  405 | 
  406 |   if (
  407 |     persisted !== null &&
  408 |     writePersistedAudioDebugEvents([...events, ...persisted])
  409 |   ) {
  410 |     return;
  411 |   }
  412 | 
  413 |   audioDebugMemoryBuffer.unshift(...events);
  414 | 
  415 |   if (audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length > AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT) {
  416 |     audioDebugMemoryBuffer.splice(
  417 |       AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  418 |       audioDebugMemoryBuffer.length - AUDIO_DEBUG_STORAGE_LIMIT,
  419 |     );
  420 |   }
  421 | }
  422 | 
  423 | function flushAudioDebugSoon(force = false): void {
  424 |   if (!audioDebugEnabled()) return;
  425 | 
  426 |   const flush = () => {
  427 |     audioDebugFlushTimer = null;
  428 | 
  429 |     if (audioDebugFlushInFlight) return;
  430 | 
  431 |     const events = takeAudioDebugEvents();
  432 |     if (!events.length) return;
  433 | 
  434 |     audioDebugFlushInFlight = true;
  435 | 
  436 |     const restore = () => {
  437 |       restoreAudioDebugEvents(events);
  438 |     };
  439 | 
  440 |     try {
  441 |       void fetch("/api/playback/debug", {
  442 |         method: "POST",
  443 |         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  444 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  445 |           sessionId: audioDebugSessionId,
  446 |           href: window.location.href,
  447 |           events,
  448 |         }),
  449 |         keepalive: true,
  450 |       })
  451 |         .then((response) => {
  452 |           if (!response.ok) {
  453 |             throw new Error(`Audio debug request failed (${response.status})`);
  454 |           }
  455 |         })
  456 |         .catch(restore)
  457 |         .finally(() => {
  458 |           audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  459 | 
  460 |           if (readPersistedAudioDebugEvents()?.length) {
  461 |             flushAudioDebugSoon();
  462 |           }
  463 |         });
  464 |     } catch {
  465 |       restore();
  466 |       audioDebugFlushInFlight = false;
  467 |     }
  468 |   };
  469 | 
  470 |   if (force) {
  471 |     if (audioDebugFlushTimer != null) {
  472 |       window.clearTimeout(audioDebugFlushTimer);
  473 |       audioDebugFlushTimer = null;
  474 |     }
  475 | 
  476 |     flush();
  477 |     return;
  478 |   }
  479 | 
  480 |   if (audioDebugFlushTimer != null) return;
  481 | 
  482 |   audioDebugFlushTimer = window.setTimeout(flush, 5000);
  483 | }
  484 | 
  485 | function sendAudioDebug(payload: {
  486 |   event: string;
  487 |   albumId?: string | null;
  488 |   recordingId?: string | null;
  489 |   playbackId?: string | null;
  490 |   source?: string | null;
  491 |   detail?: string | null;
  492 | }): void {
  493 |   if (!audioDebugEnabled()) return;
  494 |   if (!shouldSendAudioDebugEvent(payload.event)) return;
  495 | 
  496 |   const event: AudioDebugEvent = {
  497 |     t: Math.floor(performance.now()),
  498 |     ...payload,
  499 |   };
  500 | 
  501 |   appendAudioDebugEvent(event);
  502 | 
  503 |   if (audioDebugVerboseEnabled()) {
  504 |     try {
  505 |       console.info("[audio-debug]", {
  506 |         sessionId: audioDebugSessionId,
  507 |         ...event,
  508 |       });
  509 |     } catch {}
  510 |   }
  511 | 
  512 |   // Batch diagnostics after the playback-critical call stack has completed.
  513 |   // Immediate per-event fetches can perturb timing-sensitive mobile playback.
  514 |   flushAudioDebugSoon();
  515 | }
  516 | 
  517 | function setMediaSessionPositionStateSafe(args: {
  518 |   durationSec: number;
  519 |   positionSec: number;
  520 |   playbackRate?: number;
  521 | }): void {
  522 |   if (!hasMediaSession()) return;
  523 |   if (typeof navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState !== "function") return;
  524 | 
  525 |   const duration = Number.isFinite(args.durationSec)
  526 |     ? Math.max(0, args.durationSec)
  527 |     : 0;
  528 |   const position = Number.isFinite(args.positionSec)
  529 |     ? Math.max(0, Math.min(args.positionSec, duration || args.positionSec))
  530 |     : 0;
  531 |   const playbackRate =
  532 |     typeof args.playbackRate === "number" &&
  533 |     Number.isFinite(args.playbackRate) &&
  534 |     args.playbackRate > 0
  535 |       ? args.playbackRate
  536 |       : 1;
  537 | 
  538 |   try {
  539 |     navigator.mediaSession.setPositionState({
  540 |       duration,
  541 |       position,
  542 |       playbackRate,
  543 |     });
  544 |   } catch {}
  545 | }
  546 | 
  547 | function otherDeck(deckId: DeckId): DeckId {
  548 |   return deckId === "a" ? "b" : "a";
  549 | }
  550 | 
  551 | function normalizeAlbumId(raw: string | null | undefined): string {
  552 |   let s = (raw ?? "").trim();
  553 |   while (s.startsWith("alb:")) s = s.slice(4);
  554 |   return s.trim();
  555 | }

  557 | type SharePlaybackAttribution = {
  558 |   context: string;
  559 |   scopeId: string;
  560 | };
  561 | 
  562 | function readQueueSharePlaybackAttribution(queue: {
  563 |   queueSharePlaybackContext?: string | null;
  564 |   queueSharePlaybackScopeId?: string | null;
  565 | }): SharePlaybackAttribution | null {
  566 |   const context = queue.queueSharePlaybackContext?.trim() ?? "";
  567 |   const scopeId = queue.queueSharePlaybackScopeId?.trim() ?? "";
  568 | 
  569 |   if (!context.startsWith("stpc1.") || context.length > 900) {
  570 |     return null;
  571 |   }
  572 | 
  573 |   if (!/^alb:[^\s]+$/i.test(scopeId)) {
  574 |     return null;
  575 |   }
  576 | 
  577 |   return { context, scopeId };
  578 | }

  802 |   }, [clearGate]);
  803 | 
  804 |   const reportPlaybackGate = React.useCallback(
  805 |     (payload: GatePayload, corrFromHeader: string | null) => {
  806 |       const domain: GateDomain = (payload.domain ?? "playback") as GateDomain;
  807 | 
  808 |       const decision = gateResultFromPayload({
  809 |         payload: {
  810 |           ...payload,
  811 |           domain,
  812 |           correlationId: payload.correlationId ?? corrFromHeader ?? null,
  813 |         },
  814 |         attempt: { verb: "play", domain: "playback" },
  815 |         intent: inferIntentForGate(),
  816 |       });
  817 | 
  818 |       if (!decision.ok) {
  819 |         engineBlockedRef.current = true;
  820 |         lastPlaybackGateRef.current = payload;
  821 | 
  822 |         reportGate({
  823 |           code: decision.reason.code,
  824 |           action: decision.reason.action,
  825 |           domain: decision.reason.domain,
  826 |           correlationId: decision.reason.correlationId ?? null,
  827 |           message: decision.reason.message,
  828 |           uiMode: decision.uiMode,
  829 |         });
  830 |         return;
  831 |       }
  832 | 
  833 |       if (domain === "playback") clearPlaybackGate();
  834 |       else clearGate({ domain });
  835 |     },
  836 |     [clearGate, clearPlaybackGate, inferIntentForGate, reportGate],
  837 |   );
  838 | 
  839 |   const resurfacePlaybackGate = React.useCallback(() => {
  840 |     const lastGate = lastPlaybackGateRef.current;
  841 | 
  842 |     if (lastGate) {
  843 |       reportPlaybackGate(lastGate, lastGate.correlationId ?? null);
  844 |       return;
  845 |     }
  846 | 
  847 |     reportPlaybackGate(
  848 |       {
  849 |         domain: "playback",
  850 |         code: "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
  851 |         action: "login",
  852 |         message: "Enter your email address to continue listening.",
  853 |         correlationId: null,
  854 |       },
  855 |       null,
  856 |     );
  857 |   }, [reportPlaybackGate]);

  873 |   const stopDeck = React.useCallback(
  874 |     (deckId: DeckId, opts?: { destroyHls?: boolean; clearSrc?: boolean }) => {
  875 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
  876 |       if (!a) return;
  877 | 
  878 |       try {
  879 |         a.pause();
  880 |       } catch {}
  881 | 
  882 |       if (opts?.destroyHls !== false && hlsByDeckRef.current[deckId]) {
  883 |         try {
  884 |           hlsByDeckRef.current[deckId]?.destroy();
  885 |         } catch {}
  886 |         hlsByDeckRef.current[deckId] = null;
  887 |       }
  888 | 
  889 |       if (opts?.clearSrc !== false) {
  890 |         try {
  891 |           a.removeAttribute("src");
  892 |         } catch {}
  893 |         try {
  894 |           a.load();
  895 |         } catch {}
  896 |         metaByDeckRef.current[deckId] = null;
  897 |       }
  898 |     },
  899 |     [getAudio],
  900 |   );
  901 | 
  902 |   const hardStopAll = React.useCallback(() => {
  903 |     try {
  904 |       tokenAbortRef.current?.abort();
  905 |     } catch {}
  906 |     tokenAbortRef.current = null;
  907 | 
  908 |     stopDeck("a");
  909 |     stopDeck("b");
  910 | 
  911 |     standbyRef.current = null;
  912 |     telemetrySessionIdRef.current = null;
  913 |     telemetryShareAttributionRef.current = null;
  914 |     playIntentRef.current = false;
  915 |   }, [stopDeck]);
  916 | 
  917 |   const cacheAlbumSessionTokens = React.useCallback(
  918 |     (args: {
  919 |       albumId: string;
  920 |       st: string | null;
  921 |       expiresAt: string | number;
  922 |       tracks: AlbumSessionToken[];
  923 |       sampleSession?: {
  924 |         id: string;
  925 |         expiresAt: string | number;
  926 |       } | null;
  927 |     }): boolean => {
  928 |       const expiresAtMs =
  929 |         typeof args.expiresAt === "number"
  930 |           ? args.expiresAt * 1000
  931 |           : Date.parse(String(args.expiresAt));
  932 | 
  933 |       if (!Number.isFinite(expiresAtMs)) return false;
  934 | 
  935 |       const byPlaybackId = new Map<
  936 |         string,
  937 |         { token: string; expiresAtMs: number }
  938 |       >();
  939 | 
  940 |       for (const t of args.tracks) {
  941 |         const playbackId = (t.playbackId ?? "").trim();
  942 |         const token = (t.token ?? "").trim();
  943 | 
  944 |         const trackExpiresAtMs =
  945 |           typeof t.expiresAt === "number"
  946 |             ? t.expiresAt * 1000
  947 |             : Date.parse(String(t.expiresAt));
  948 | 
  949 |         if (!playbackId || !token || !Number.isFinite(trackExpiresAtMs)) {
  950 |           continue;
  951 |         }
  952 | 
  953 |         const entry = { token, expiresAtMs: trackExpiresAtMs };
  954 |         byPlaybackId.set(playbackId, entry);
  955 |         tokenCacheRef.current.set(playbackId, entry);
  956 |       }
  957 | 
  958 |       if (byPlaybackId.size === 0) return false;
  959 | 
  960 |       albumSessionCacheRef.current.set(albumSessionKey(args.albumId, args.st), {
  961 |         albumId: args.albumId,
  962 |         st: args.st,
  963 |         expiresAtMs,
  964 |         byPlaybackId,
  965 |       });
  966 | 
  967 |       if (args.sampleSession) {
  968 |         const sampleExpiresAtMs =
  969 |           typeof args.sampleSession.expiresAt === "number"
  970 |             ? args.sampleSession.expiresAt * 1000
  971 |             : Date.parse(String(args.sampleSession.expiresAt));
  972 | 
  973 |         if (Number.isFinite(sampleExpiresAtMs)) {
  974 |           anonSampleSessionRef.current = {
  975 |             id: args.sampleSession.id,
  976 |             expiresAtMs: sampleExpiresAtMs,
  977 |           };
  978 |         }
  979 |       }
  980 | 
  981 |       return true;
  982 |     },
  983 |     [albumSessionKey],
  984 |   );
  985 | 
  986 |   const getCachedTokenForPlaybackId = React.useCallback(
  987 |     (playbackId: string): { token: string; expiresAtMs: number } | null => {
  988 |       const direct = tokenCacheRef.current.get(playbackId);
  989 |       if (direct && Date.now() < direct.expiresAtMs - 5000) return direct;
  990 |       return null;
  991 |     },
  992 |     [],
  993 |   );

  995 |   const prefetchAlbumSession = React.useCallback(
  996 |     async (args: {
  997 |       albumId: string | null | undefined;
  998 |       st?: string | null;
  999 |       startPlaybackId?: string | null;
 1000 |       signal?: AbortSignal;
 1001 |       surfaceGate?: boolean;
 1002 |     }): Promise<boolean> => {
 1003 |       const albumId = normalizeAlbumId(args.albumId);
 1004 |       if (!albumId) return false;
 1005 | 
 1006 |       const st = args.st ?? getShareTokenFromLocation();
 1007 |       const startPlaybackId = args.startPlaybackId?.trim() || null;
 1008 | 
 1009 |       const key = albumSessionKey(albumId, st);
 1010 | 
 1011 |       const cached = albumSessionCacheRef.current.get(key);
 1012 |       if (cached && Date.now() < cached.expiresAtMs - 5000) return true;
 1013 | 
 1014 |       const existing = albumSessionInFlightRef.current.get(key);
 1015 |       if (existing) return existing;
 1016 | 
 1017 |       const promise = (async () => {
 1018 |         try {
 1019 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1020 |             event: "album-session-requested",
 1021 |             albumId,
 1022 |             source: "AudioEngine",
 1023 |           });
 1024 | 
 1025 |           const res = await fetch("/api/mux/album-session", {
 1026 |             method: "POST",
 1027 |             headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 1028 |             body: JSON.stringify({
 1029 |               albumId,
 1030 |               ...(st ? { st } : {}),
 1031 |               ...(startPlaybackId ? { startPlaybackId } : {}),
 1032 |             }),
 1033 |             signal: args.signal,
 1034 |           });
 1035 | 
 1036 |           let data: AlbumSessionResponse | null = null;
 1037 |           try {
 1038 |             data = (await res.json()) as AlbumSessionResponse;
 1039 |           } catch {
 1040 |             data = null;
 1041 |           }
 1042 | 
 1043 |           if (!res.ok || !data || !("ok" in data) || data.ok !== true) {
 1044 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1045 |               event: "album-session-failed",
 1046 |               albumId,
 1047 |               source: "AudioEngine",
 1048 |               detail: `status=${res.status}`,
 1049 |             });
 1050 | 
 1051 |             const gatePayload =
 1052 |               data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false
 1053 |                 ? (data.gate ?? null)
 1054 |                 : null;
 1055 | 
 1056 |             if (args.surfaceGate && gatePayload) {
 1057 |               reportPlaybackGate(
 1058 |                 {
 1059 |                   domain: gatePayload.domain ?? "playback",
 1060 |                   code:
 1061 |                     normalizeGateCodeRaw(gatePayload.code) ??
 1062 |                     "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
 1063 |                   action: gatePayload.action ?? "login",
 1064 |                   message:
 1065 |                     gatePayload.message ??
 1066 |                     "Enter your email address to continue listening.",
 1067 |                   correlationId:
 1068 |                     gatePayload.correlationId ??
 1069 |                     res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ??
 1070 |                     null,
 1071 |                   reason: gatePayload.reason,
 1072 |                 },
 1073 |                 res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null,
 1074 |               );
 1075 |             }
 1076 | 
 1077 |             return false;
 1078 |           }
 1079 | 
 1080 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1081 |             event: "album-session-received",
 1082 |             albumId: data.albumId || albumId,
 1083 |             source: "AudioEngine",
 1084 |             detail: `tracks=${data.tracks.length}`,
 1085 |           });
 1086 | 
 1087 |           return cacheAlbumSessionTokens({
 1088 |             albumId: data.albumId || albumId,
 1089 |             st,
 1090 |             expiresAt: data.expiresAt,
 1091 |             tracks: data.tracks,
 1092 |             sampleSession:
 1093 |               data.mode === "sample" && data.sampleSession
 1094 |                 ? data.sampleSession
 1095 |                 : null,
 1096 |           });
 1097 |         } catch {
 1098 |           return false;
 1099 |         }
 1100 |       })().finally(() => {
 1101 |         albumSessionInFlightRef.current.delete(key);
 1102 |       });
 1103 | 
 1104 |       albumSessionInFlightRef.current.set(key, promise);
 1105 |       return promise;
 1106 |     },
 1107 |     [
 1108 |       albumSessionKey,
 1109 |       cacheAlbumSessionTokens,
 1110 |       getShareTokenFromLocation,
 1111 |       reportPlaybackGate,
 1112 |     ],
 1113 |   );

 1115 |   const prefetchCurrentQueueAlbumSession = React.useCallback(
 1116 |     async (signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<boolean> => {
 1117 |       const s = pRef.current;
 1118 |       const albumId = normalizeAlbumId(s.queueContextId);
 1119 |       if (!albumId) return false;
 1120 | 
 1121 |       return prefetchAlbumSession({
 1122 |         albumId,
 1123 |         st: getShareTokenFromLocation(),
 1124 |         startPlaybackId: s.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 1125 |         signal,
 1126 |       });
 1127 |     },
 1128 |     [getShareTokenFromLocation, prefetchAlbumSession],
 1129 |   );

 1131 |   const fetchSingleToken = React.useCallback(
 1132 |     async (args: {
 1133 |       playbackId: string;
 1134 |       track: PlayerTrack;
 1135 |       signal: AbortSignal;
 1136 |     }): Promise<{ token: string; expiresAtMs: number } | null> => {
 1137 |       const s = pRef.current;
 1138 |       const st = getShareTokenFromLocation();
 1139 | 
 1140 |       const res = await fetch("/api/mux/token", {
 1141 |         method: "POST",
 1142 |         headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 1143 |         body: JSON.stringify({
 1144 |           playbackId: args.playbackId,
 1145 |           recordingId: args.track.recordingId,
 1146 |           albumId: s.queueContextId,
 1147 |           albumSlug: s.queueContextSlug,
 1148 |           durationMs:
 1149 |             args.track.durationMs ??
 1150 |             s.durationByRecordingId?.[args.track.recordingId],
 1151 |           ...(st ? { st } : {}),
 1152 |         }),
 1153 |         signal: args.signal,
 1154 |       });
 1155 | 
 1156 |       const corr = res.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null;
 1157 | 
 1158 |       let data: TokenResponse | null = null;
 1159 |       try {
 1160 |         data = (await res.json()) as TokenResponse;
 1161 |       } catch {
 1162 |         data = null;
 1163 |       }
 1164 | 
 1165 |       if (!res.ok || !data || !("ok" in data) || data.ok !== true) {
 1166 |         const gatePayloadRaw =
 1167 |           data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false
 1168 |             ? (data.gate ?? null)
 1169 |             : null;
 1170 | 
 1171 |         const msg =
 1172 |           gatePayloadRaw?.message?.trim() ||
 1173 |           (data && "ok" in data && data.ok === false ? data.error : "") ||
 1174 |           `Token error (${res.status})`;
 1175 | 
 1176 |         blockedNonceRef.current.set(args.playbackId, s.reloadNonce);
 1177 |         playIntentRef.current = false;
 1178 | 
 1179 |         if (gatePayloadRaw) {
 1180 |           const rawCode =
 1181 |             normalizeGateCodeRaw(gatePayloadRaw.code) ?? "INVALID_REQUEST";
 1182 |           const action: GateAction = gatePayloadRaw.action ?? "wait";
 1183 |           const payload: GatePayload = {
 1184 |             domain: (gatePayloadRaw.domain ?? "playback") as GateDomain,
 1185 |             code: rawCode,
 1186 |             action,
 1187 |             message: gatePayloadRaw.message ?? msg,
 1188 |             correlationId: gatePayloadRaw.correlationId ?? corr ?? null,
 1189 |             reason: gatePayloadRaw.reason,
 1190 |           };
 1191 |           reportPlaybackGate(payload, corr);
 1192 |         } else {
 1193 |           clearPlaybackGate();
 1194 |         }
 1195 | 
 1196 |         mediaSurface.setStatus("blocked");
 1197 |         return null;
 1198 |       }
 1199 | 
 1200 |       const expiresAtMs =
 1201 |         typeof data.expiresAt === "number"
 1202 |           ? data.expiresAt * 1000
 1203 |           : Date.parse(String(data.expiresAt));
 1204 | 
 1205 |       if (!Number.isFinite(expiresAtMs)) return null;
 1206 | 
 1207 |       const token = { token: data.token, expiresAtMs };
 1208 |       tokenCacheRef.current.set(args.playbackId, token);
 1209 |       blockedNonceRef.current.delete(args.playbackId);
 1210 |       clearPlaybackGate();
 1211 | 
 1212 |       return token;
 1213 |     },
 1214 |     [clearPlaybackGate, getShareTokenFromLocation, reportPlaybackGate],
 1215 |   );
 1216 | 
 1217 |   const ensureTokenForTrack = React.useCallback(
 1218 |     async (args: {

 1267 |   const attachTrackToDeck = React.useCallback(
 1268 |     async (args: {
 1269 |       deckId: DeckId;
 1270 |       track: PlayerTrack;
 1271 |       token: string;
 1272 |       seq: number;
 1273 |       reason: "active" | "standby";
 1274 |     }): Promise<boolean> => {
 1275 |       const a = getAudio(args.deckId);
 1276 |       if (!a) return false;
 1277 | 
 1278 |       const playbackId = (args.track.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 1279 |       const recordingId = args.track.recordingId;
 1280 |       if (!playbackId || !recordingId) return false;
 1281 | 
 1282 |       const attachKey = `${playbackId}:${pRef.current.reloadNonce}`;
 1283 |       const useStaticM4a = shouldUseStaticM4a();
 1284 |       const srcUrl = useStaticM4a
 1285 |         ? muxSignedStaticAudioUrl(playbackId, args.token)
 1286 |         : muxSignedHlsUrl(playbackId, args.token);
 1287 |       const useNativeHls = !useStaticM4a && shouldUseNativeHls(a);
 1288 |       const hlsPath = useStaticM4a
 1289 |         ? "static-m4a"
 1290 |         : useNativeHls
 1291 |           ? "native"
 1292 |           : "hlsjs";
 1293 | 
 1294 |       sendAudioDebug({
 1295 |         event: "transport-path-selected",
 1296 |         albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1297 |         recordingId,
 1298 |         playbackId,
 1299 |         source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1300 |         detail: JSON.stringify({
 1301 |           epochMs: Date.now(),
 1302 |           reason: args.reason,
 1303 |           path: hlsPath,
 1304 |           nativeHlsCanPlay: a.canPlayType("application/vnd.apple.mpegurl"),
 1305 |           alternateNativeHlsCanPlay: a.canPlayType("application/x-mpegURL"),
 1306 |           hlsJsLoaded: hlsConstructorCache !== null,
 1307 |           hlsJsSupported: getKnownHlsJsSupport(),
 1308 |           userAgent:
 1309 |             typeof navigator === "undefined" ? null : navigator.userAgent,
 1310 |         }),
 1311 |       });
 1312 | 
 1313 |       sendAudioDebug({
 1314 |         event:
 1315 |           args.reason === "standby"
 1316 |             ? "standby-attach-start"
 1317 |             : "active-attach-start",
 1318 |         albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1319 |         recordingId,
 1320 |         playbackId,
 1321 |         source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1322 |         detail: `seq=${args.seq}`,
 1323 |       });
 1324 | 
 1325 |       stopDeck(args.deckId);
 1326 | 
 1327 |       if (args.seq !== loadSeq.current && args.reason === "active") {
 1328 |         return false;
 1329 |       }
 1330 | 
 1331 |       a.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
 1332 |       a.preload =
 1333 |         args.reason === "standby" || useStaticM4a ? "auto" : "metadata";
 1334 |       a.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pRef.current.volume));
 1335 |       a.muted = pRef.current.muted;
 1336 | 
 1337 |       metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1338 |         deckId: args.deckId,
 1339 |         recordingId,
 1340 |         playbackId,
 1341 |         attachKey,
 1342 |         prepared: false,
 1343 |         hlsPath,
 1344 |       };
 1345 | 
 1346 |       if (useStaticM4a) {
 1347 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1348 |           event:
 1349 |             args.reason === "standby"
 1350 |               ? "standby-static-m4a-attach"
 1351 |               : "active-static-m4a-attach",
 1352 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1353 |           recordingId,
 1354 |           playbackId,
 1355 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1356 |         });
 1357 | 
 1358 |         return new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
 1359 |           let settled = false;
 1360 |           let timeoutId: number | null = null;
 1361 | 
 1362 |           const snapshot = () =>
 1363 |             JSON.stringify({
 1364 |               readyState: a.readyState,
 1365 |               networkState: a.networkState,
 1366 |               currentTimeSec: Number(a.currentTime.toFixed(3)),
 1367 |               bufferedAheadSec: Number(bufferedAheadSeconds(a).toFixed(3)),
 1368 |             });
 1369 | 
 1370 |           const cleanup = () => {
 1371 |             a.removeEventListener("loadedmetadata", onMaybeReady);
 1372 |             a.removeEventListener("canplay", onMaybeReady);
 1373 |             a.removeEventListener("canplaythrough", onMaybeReady);
 1374 |             a.removeEventListener("progress", onMaybeReady);
 1375 |             a.removeEventListener("error", onError);
 1376 | 
 1377 |             if (timeoutId != null) {
 1378 |               window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
 1379 |               timeoutId = null;
 1380 |             }
 1381 |           };
 1382 | 
 1383 |           const finish = (ok: boolean) => {
 1384 |             if (settled) return;
 1385 | 
 1386 |             settled = true;
 1387 |             cleanup();
 1388 | 
 1389 |             const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId];
 1390 | 
 1391 |             if (ok && meta?.attachKey === attachKey) {
 1392 |               metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1393 |                 ...meta,
 1394 |                 prepared: true,
 1395 |               };
 1396 |             }
 1397 | 
 1398 |             resolve(ok);
 1399 |           };
 1400 | 
 1401 |           const onMaybeReady = () => {
 1402 |             const ready =
 1403 |               args.reason === "standby"
 1404 |                 ? isStaticM4aStandbyBuffered(a)
 1405 |                 : a.readyState >= HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_METADATA;
 1406 | 
 1407 |             if (!ready) return;
 1408 | 
 1409 |             if (args.reason === "standby") {
 1410 |               sendAudioDebug({
 1411 |                 event: "standby-static-m4a-buffered",
 1412 |                 albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1413 |                 recordingId,
 1414 |                 playbackId,
 1415 |                 source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1416 |                 detail: snapshot(),
 1417 |               });
 1418 |             }
 1419 | 
 1420 |             finish(true);
 1421 |           };
 1422 | 
 1423 |           const onError = () => {
 1424 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1425 |               event:
 1426 |                 args.reason === "standby"
 1427 |                   ? "standby-static-m4a-error"
 1428 |                   : "active-static-m4a-error",
 1429 |               albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1430 |               recordingId,
 1431 |               playbackId,
 1432 |               source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1433 |               detail: snapshot(),
 1434 |             });
 1435 | 
 1436 |             finish(false);
 1437 |           };
 1438 | 
 1439 |           a.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", onMaybeReady);
 1440 |           a.addEventListener("canplay", onMaybeReady);
 1441 |           a.addEventListener("canplaythrough", onMaybeReady);
 1442 |           a.addEventListener("progress", onMaybeReady);
 1443 |           a.addEventListener("error", onError);
 1444 | 
 1445 |           try {
 1446 |             a.src = srcUrl;
 1447 |             a.load();
 1448 |             onMaybeReady();
 1449 |           } catch {
 1450 |             onError();
 1451 |             return;
 1452 |           }
 1453 | 
 1454 |           timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
 1455 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1456 |               event:
 1457 |                 args.reason === "standby"
 1458 |                   ? "standby-static-m4a-buffer-timeout"
 1459 |                   : "active-static-m4a-timeout",
 1460 |               albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1461 |               recordingId,
 1462 |               playbackId,
 1463 |               source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1464 |               detail: snapshot(),
 1465 |             });
 1466 | 
 1467 |             finish(false);
 1468 |           }, STATIC_M4A_STANDBY_TIMEOUT_MS);
 1469 |         });
 1470 |       }
 1471 | 
 1472 |       if (useNativeHls) {
 1473 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1474 |           event:
 1475 |             args.reason === "standby"
 1476 |               ? "standby-native-hls-safari"
 1477 |               : "active-native-hls-safari",
 1478 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1479 |           recordingId,
 1480 |           playbackId,
 1481 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1482 |         });
 1483 | 
 1484 |         return new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
 1485 |           let settled = false;
 1486 | 
 1487 |           const cleanup = () => {
 1488 |             a.removeEventListener("loadedmetadata", onReady);
 1489 |             a.removeEventListener("canplay", onReady);
 1490 |             a.removeEventListener("error", onError);
 1491 |           };
 1492 | 
 1493 |           const finish = (ok: boolean) => {
 1494 |             if (settled) return;
 1495 |             settled = true;
 1496 |             cleanup();
 1497 |             const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId];
 1498 |             if (ok && meta?.attachKey === attachKey) {
 1499 |               metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1500 |                 ...meta,
 1501 |                 prepared: true,
 1502 |               };
 1503 |             }
 1504 |             resolve(ok);
 1505 |           };
 1506 | 
 1507 |           const onReady = () => finish(true);
 1508 |           const onError = () => finish(false);
 1509 | 
 1510 |           a.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", onReady);
 1511 |           a.addEventListener("canplay", onReady);
 1512 |           a.addEventListener("error", onError);
 1513 | 
 1514 |           try {
 1515 |             a.src = srcUrl;
 1516 |             a.load();
 1517 |           } catch {
 1518 |             finish(false);
 1519 |           }
 1520 | 
 1521 |           window.setTimeout(() => finish(true), 2500);
 1522 |         });
 1523 |       }
 1524 | 
 1525 |       let HlsJs: HlsJsConstructor;
 1526 | 
 1527 |       try {
 1528 |         HlsJs = await loadHlsConstructor();
 1529 |       } catch {
 1530 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1531 |           event: "hls-load-failed",
 1532 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1533 |           recordingId,
 1534 |           playbackId,
 1535 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1536 |         });

 1538 |         return false;
 1539 |       }

 1542 |         return false;
 1543 |       }
