 1545 |       if (!HlsJs.isSupported()) {
 1546 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1547 |           event: "hls-unsupported",
 1548 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1549 |           recordingId,
 1550 |           playbackId,
 1551 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1552 |         });
 1553 | 
 1554 |         return false;
 1555 |       }
 1556 | 
 1557 |       sendAudioDebug({
 1558 |         event:
 1559 |           args.reason === "standby" ? "standby-hlsjs-attach" : "hlsjs-attach",
 1560 |         albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1561 |         recordingId,
 1562 |         playbackId,
 1563 |         source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 1564 |       });
 1565 | 
 1566 |       return new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
 1567 |         let settled = false;
 1568 | 
 1569 |         const hls = new HlsJs({
 1570 |           enableWorker: true,
 1571 |           lowLatencyMode: false,
 1572 |           backBufferLength: 30,
 1573 |         });
 1574 | 
 1575 |         hlsByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = hls;
 1576 | 
 1577 |         const finish = (ok: boolean) => {
 1578 |           if (settled) return;
 1579 |           settled = true;
 1580 | 
 1581 |           const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId];
 1582 |           if (ok && meta?.attachKey === attachKey) {
 1583 |             metaByDeckRef.current[args.deckId] = {
 1584 |               ...meta,
 1585 |               prepared: true,
 1586 |             };
 1587 |           }
 1588 | 
 1589 |           resolve(ok);
 1590 |         };
 1591 | 
 1592 |         hls.on(HlsJs.Events.ERROR, (_event, err) => {
 1593 |           if (!err?.fatal) return;
 1594 | 
 1595 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1596 |             event:
 1597 |               args.reason === "standby"
 1598 |                 ? "standby-hls-fatal"
 1599 |                 : "active-hls-fatal",
 1600 |             albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1601 |             recordingId,
 1602 |             playbackId,
 1603 |             source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}.hls`,
 1604 |             detail: `${err.type ?? "unknown"}:${err.details ?? "error"}`,
 1605 |           });
 1606 | 
 1607 |           finish(false);
 1608 | 
 1609 |           if (args.reason === "active") {
 1610 |             reportLocalPlaybackErrorAsGate(
 1611 |               "INVALID_REQUEST",
 1612 |               `HLS fatal: ${err.details ?? "error"}`,
 1613 |             );
 1614 |           }
 1615 |         });
 1616 | 
 1617 |         hls.once(HlsJs.Events.MANIFEST_PARSED, () => {
 1618 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1619 |             event:
 1620 |               args.reason === "standby"
 1621 |                 ? "standby-manifest-parsed"
 1622 |                 : "hls-manifest-parsed",
 1623 |             albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1624 |             recordingId,
 1625 |             playbackId,
 1626 |             source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}.hls`,
 1627 |           });
 1628 | 
 1629 |           finish(true);
 1630 |         });

 1633 |           hls.attachMedia(a);
 1634 |           hls.loadSource(srcUrl);
 1635 |         } catch {
 1636 |           finish(false);
 1637 |         }
 1638 | 
 1639 |         window.setTimeout(() => finish(false), 10_000);
 1640 |       });
 1641 |     },

 1645 |   const playDeck = React.useCallback(
 1646 |     async (deckId: DeckId, reason: "active" | "promote"): Promise<boolean> => {
 1647 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 1648 |       const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];
 1649 |       if (!a || !meta) return false;
 1650 | 
 1651 |       try {
 1652 |         await a.play();
 1653 | 
 1654 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1655 |           event:
 1656 |             reason === "promote"
 1657 |               ? "standby-promote-play-resolved"
 1658 |               : "attach-play-resolved",
 1659 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1660 |           recordingId: meta.recordingId,
 1661 |           playbackId: meta.playbackId,
 1662 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 1663 |         });
 1664 | 
 1665 |         return true;
 1666 |       } catch (err: unknown) {
 1667 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1668 |           event:
 1669 |             reason === "promote"
 1670 |               ? "standby-promote-play-rejected"
 1671 |               : "attach-play-rejected",
 1672 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1673 |           recordingId: meta.recordingId,
 1674 |           playbackId: meta.playbackId,

 1676 |           detail:
 1677 |             err instanceof Error ? `${err.name}: ${err.message}` : "unknown",
 1678 |         });
 1679 | 
 1680 |         return false;
 1681 |       }

 1686 |   const playTargetFromUserGesture = React.useCallback(
 1687 |     (detail: TargetedPlayIntentDetail): void => {
 1688 |       if (!isAppleMobileWebKit()) return;
 1689 |       if (engineBlockedRef.current) return;
 1690 | 
 1691 |       const track = detail.track;
 1692 |       const albumId = (detail.albumId ?? "").trim();
 1693 |       const playbackId = (track?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 1694 |       const recordingId = (track?.recordingId ?? "").trim();
 1695 | 
 1696 |       if (!track || !albumId || !playbackId || !recordingId) {
 1697 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1698 |           event: "apple-target-play-missing-context",
 1699 |           albumId: albumId || pRef.current.queueContextId || null,
 1700 |           recordingId: recordingId || null,
 1701 |           playbackId: playbackId || null,
 1702 |           source: "AudioEngine",
 1703 |         });
 1704 |         return;
 1705 |       }
 1706 | 
 1707 |       const activeDeck = activeDeckRef.current;
 1708 |       const audio = getAudio(activeDeck);
 1709 |

 1711 | 
 1712 |       const existingMeta = metaByDeckRef.current[activeDeck];
 1713 | 
 1714 |       playIntentRef.current = true;
 1715 | 
 1716 |       if (
 1717 |         existingMeta?.recordingId === recordingId &&
 1718 |         existingMeta.playbackId === playbackId &&
 1719 |         Boolean(audio.currentSrc || audio.src)
 1720 |       ) {
 1721 |         void audio.play().then(
 1722 |           () => {
 1723 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1724 |               event: "apple-target-resume-resolved",
 1725 |               albumId,
 1726 |               recordingId,
 1727 |               playbackId,
 1728 |               source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeck}`,
 1729 |             });
 1730 |           },
 1731 |           (error: unknown) => {
 1732 |             sendAudioDebug({
 1733 |               event: "apple-target-resume-rejected",
 1734 |               albumId,
 1735 |               recordingId,
 1736 |               playbackId,
 1737 |               source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeck}`,
 1738 |               detail:
 1739 |                 error instanceof Error
 1740 |                   ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}`
 1741 |                   : "unknown",
 1742 |             });
 1743 |           },
 1744 |         );
 1745 | 
 1746 |         return;
 1747 |       }
 1748 | 
 1749 |       const sourceUrl = new URL("/api/mux/token", window.location.origin);
 1750 |       sourceUrl.searchParams.set("format", "audio-m4a");
 1751 |       sourceUrl.searchParams.set("playbackId", playbackId);
 1752 |       sourceUrl.searchParams.set("albumId", albumId);
 1753 | 
 1754 |       const durationMs =
 1755 |         track.durationMs ??
 1756 |         pRef.current.durationByRecordingId[recordingId] ??
 1757 |         0;
 1758 | 
 1759 |       if (Number.isFinite(durationMs) && durationMs > 0) {
 1760 |         sourceUrl.searchParams.set(
 1761 |           "durationMs",
 1762 |           String(Math.round(durationMs)),
 1763 |         );
 1764 |       }
 1765 | 
 1766 |       const shareToken = getShareTokenFromLocation();
 1767 | 
 1768 |       if (shareToken) {
 1769 |         sourceUrl.searchParams.set("st", shareToken);
 1770 |       }
 1771 | 
 1772 |       const attachKey = `${playbackId}:${pRef.current.reloadNonce}`;
 1773 | 
 1774 |       loadSeq.current += 1;
 1775 |       standbyRef.current = null;
 1776 | 
 1777 |       stopDeck(activeDeck);
 1778 | 
 1779 |       audio.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
 1780 |       audio.preload = "auto";
 1781 |       audio.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pRef.current.volume));
 1782 |       audio.muted = pRef.current.muted;
 1783 | 
 1784 |       metaByDeckRef.current[activeDeck] = {
 1785 |         deckId: activeDeck,
 1786 |         recordingId,
 1787 |         playbackId,
 1788 |         attachKey,
 1789 |         prepared: false,
 1790 |         hlsPath: "static-m4a",
 1791 |       };
 1792 | 
 1793 |       telemetrySessionIdRef.current = newPlaybackSessionId();
 1794 |       telemetryShareAttributionRef.current = readQueueSharePlaybackAttribution(
 1795 |         pRef.current,
 1796 |       );
 1797 | 
 1798 |       mediaSurface.setTrack(recordingId);
 1799 |       mediaSurface.setStatus("loading");
 1800 | 
 1801 |       sendAudioDebug({
 1802 |         event: "apple-target-play-start",
 1803 |         albumId,
 1804 |         recordingId,
 1805 |         playbackId,
 1806 |         source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeck}`,
 1807 |         detail: "same-origin-token-redirect",
 1808 |       });
 1809 | 
 1810 |       try {
 1811 |         audio.src = sourceUrl.toString();
 1812 |         audio.load();
 1813 |       } catch (error: unknown) {
 1814 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1815 |           event: "apple-target-source-attach-rejected",
 1816 |           albumId,
 1817 |           recordingId,
 1818 |           playbackId,
 1819 |           source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeck}`,
 1820 |           detail:
 1821 |             error instanceof Error
 1822 |               ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}`
 1823 |               : "unknown",
 1824 |         });
 1825 |         return;
 1826 |       }
 1827 | 
 1828 |       // This invocation deliberately remains in the original click/touch call
 1829 |       // stack. The media request may resolve asynchronously, but play() itself
 1830 |       // has already received WebKit's user activation.
 1831 |       void audio.play().then(
 1832 |         () => {
 1833 |           sendAudioDebug({
 1834 |             event: "apple-target-play-resolved",
 1835 |             albumId,
 1836 |             recordingId,
 1837 |             playbackId,
 1838 |             source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeck}`,
 1839 |           });
 1840 |         },

 1878 |   const prepareStandbyForTrack = React.useCallback(
 1879 |     async (track: PlayerTrack): Promise<boolean> => {
 1880 |       const playbackId = (track.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 1881 |       if (!track.recordingId || !playbackId) return false;
 1882 | 
 1883 |       const currentStandby = standbyRef.current;
 1884 |       if (
 1885 |         currentStandby?.recordingId === track.recordingId &&
 1886 |         currentStandby.playbackId === playbackId
 1887 |       ) {
 1888 |         return true;
 1889 |       }
 1890 | 
 1891 |       const deckId = otherDeck(activeDeckRef.current);
 1892 |       const existing = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];
 1893 | 
 1894 |       if (
 1895 |         existing?.recordingId === track.recordingId &&
 1896 |         existing.playbackId === playbackId &&
 1897 |         existing.prepared
 1898 |       ) {
 1899 |         standbyRef.current = {
 1900 |           deckId,
 1901 |           recordingId: track.recordingId,
 1902 |           playbackId,
 1903 |           attachKey: existing.attachKey,
 1904 |         };
 1905 |         return true;
 1906 |       }
 1907 | 
 1908 |       const ac = new AbortController();
 1909 |       const token = await ensureTokenForTrack({
 1910 |         track,
 1911 |         signal: ac.signal,
 1912 |         surfaceGate: false,
 1913 |       });
 1914 | 
 1915 |       if (!token) return false;
 1916 | 
 1917 |       const seq = loadSeq.current;
 1918 | 
 1919 |       const ok = await attachTrackToDeck({
 1920 |         deckId,
 1921 |         track,
 1922 |         token: token.token,
 1923 |         seq,
 1924 |         reason: "standby",
 1925 |       });
 1926 | 
 1927 |       const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];
 1928 | 
 1929 |       if (!ok || !meta) {
 1930 |         sendAudioDebug({
 1931 |           event: "standby-prepare-failed",
 1932 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1933 |           recordingId: track.recordingId,
 1934 |           playbackId,
 1935 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 1936 |         });
 1937 |         return false;
 1938 |       }
 1939 | 
 1940 |       standbyRef.current = {
 1941 |         deckId,
 1942 |         recordingId: track.recordingId,
 1943 |         playbackId,
 1944 |         attachKey: meta.attachKey,
 1945 |       };
 1946 | 
 1947 |       sendAudioDebug({
 1948 |         event: "standby-prepared",
 1949 |         albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 1950 |         recordingId: track.recordingId,
 1951 |         playbackId,
 1952 |         source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 1953 |       });
 1954 | 
 1955 |       return true;
 1956 |     },
 1957 |     [attachTrackToDeck, ensureTokenForTrack],
 1958 |   );

 1960 |   const getNextTrack = React.useCallback((): PlayerTrack | null => {
 1961 |     const s = pRef.current;
 1962 |     const cur = s.current;
 1963 |     if (!cur) return null;
 1964 | 
 1965 |     const idx = s.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId);
 1966 | 
 1967 |     if (s.repeat === "one") return cur;
 1968 | 
 1969 |     if (idx >= 0 && idx + 1 < s.queue.length) {
 1970 |       return s.queue[idx + 1] ?? null;
 1971 |     }
 1972 | 
 1973 |     if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
 1974 |       return s.queue[0] ?? null;
 1975 |     }
 1976 | 
 1977 |     return null;
 1978 |   }, []);

 1980 |   const promoteStandby = React.useCallback(
 1981 |     async (nextTrack: PlayerTrack): Promise<boolean> => {
 1982 |       const playbackId = (nextTrack.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 1983 |       if (!playbackId) return false;
 1984 |

 1988 |         !prepared ||

 1990 |         prepared.playbackId !== playbackId
 1991 |       ) {

 1993 |           event: "standby-promote-missing-prepared-deck",

 1995 |           recordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
 1996 |           playbackId,
 1997 |           source: "AudioEngine",
 1998 |         });

 2001 |       }

 2005 |

 2009 |         recordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,

 2060 |   const attachActiveTrack = React.useCallback(async () => {
 2061 |     const s = pRef.current;
 2062 |     const track = s.current;
 2063 |     const playbackId = (track?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2064 | 
 2065 |     if (!track || !playbackId) return;
 2066 |     if (engineBlockedRef.current) return;
 2067 | 
 2068 |     const activeDeck = activeDeckRef.current;

 2070 |

 2072 |       activeMeta?.recordingId === track.recordingId &&
 2073 |       activeMeta.playbackId === playbackId
 2074 |     ) {

 2079 |           pRef.current.clearIntent();
 2080 |         }
 2081 |       }

 2083 |     }

 2086 |     if (blockedAt === s.reloadNonce) {

 2088 |       hardStopAll();
 2089 |       mediaSurface.setStatus("blocked");
 2090 |       resurfacePlaybackGate();
 2091 |       return;
 2092 |     }

 2094 |     const seq = ++loadSeq.current;
 2095 | 
 2096 |     standbyRef.current = null;
 2097 |     telemetrySessionIdRef.current = newPlaybackSessionId();
 2098 |     telemetryShareAttributionRef.current = readQueueSharePlaybackAttribution(s);

 2100 |     mediaSurface.setTrack(track.recordingId);
 2101 |     mediaSurface.setStatus("loading");
 2102 |     pRef.current.setStatusExternal("loading");

 2105 |     pRef.current.setLoadingReasonExternal(
 2106 |       cachedBeforeAttach ? "attach" : "token",

 2116 |         track,
 2117 |         signal: ac.signal,
 2118 |         surfaceGate: true,
 2119 |       }));

 2122 |     if (seq !== loadSeq.current) return;
 2123 | 
 2124 |     const ok = await attachTrackToDeck({
 2125 |       deckId: activeDeck,
 2126 |       track,
 2127 |       token: token.token,
 2128 |       seq,
 2129 |       reason: "active",
 2130 |     });
 2131 | 
 2132 |     if (!ok || seq !== loadSeq.current) return;
 2133 | 
 2134 |     if (
 2135 |       s.intent === "play" ||
 2136 |       playIntentRef.current ||
 2137 |       s.status === "loading"
 2138 |     ) {
 2139 |       const played = await playDeck(activeDeck, "active");
 2140 |       if (played) {
 2141 |         playIntentRef.current = false;
 2142 |         pRef.current.clearIntent();
 2143 |       } else {
 2144 |         playIntentRef.current = true;
 2145 |       }
 2146 |     }
 2147 |   }, [
 2148 |     attachTrackToDeck,
 2149 |     ensureTokenForTrack,
 2150 |     getCachedTokenForPlaybackId,

 2337 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 2338 |     const clearAudioFeatures = () => {
 2339 |       audioSurface.set({
 2340 |         rms: 0,
 2341 |         bass: 0,
 2342 |         mid: 0,
 2343 |         treble: 0,
 2344 |         centroid: 0,
 2345 |         energy: 0,
 2346 |       });
 2347 |     };
 2348 | 
 2349 |     const isActive = p.status === "playing" || p.status === "loading";
 2350 | 
 2351 |     if (!audioAnalysisReady || !isActive) {
 2352 |       clearAudioFeatures();
 2353 |       return;
 2354 |     }
 2355 | 
 2356 |     let raf: number | null = null;
 2357 | 
 2358 |     const tick = () => {
 2359 |       const analyser = analyserRef.current;
 2360 |       const freq = freqDataRef.current;
 2361 |       const time = timeDataRef.current;
 2362 | 
 2363 |       if (!analyser || !freq || !time) {
 2364 |         clearAudioFeatures();
 2365 |         return;
 2366 |       }
 2367 | 
 2368 |       analyser.getByteFrequencyData(freq);
 2369 |       analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(time);
 2370 | 
 2371 |       let sum = 0;
 2372 |       for (let i = 0; i < time.length; i++) {
 2373 |         const v = (time[i]! - 128) / 128;
 2374 |         sum += v * v;
 2375 |       }
 2376 |       const rms = Math.sqrt(sum / time.length);
 2377 | 
 2378 |       const n = freq.length;
 2379 |       const bassEnd = Math.floor(n * 0.08);
 2380 |       const midEnd = Math.floor(n * 0.35);
 2381 | 
 2382 |       let bass = 0;
 2383 |       let mid = 0;
 2384 |       let treble = 0;
 2385 | 
 2386 |       for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 2387 |         const v = freq[i]! / 255;
 2388 |         if (i < bassEnd) bass += v;
 2389 |         else if (i < midEnd) mid += v;
 2390 |         else treble += v;
 2391 |       }
 2392 | 
 2393 |       bass /= bassEnd || 1;
 2394 |       mid /= midEnd - bassEnd || 1;
 2395 |       treble /= n - midEnd || 1;
 2396 | 
 2397 |       let weighted = 0;
 2398 |       let total = 0;
 2399 | 
 2400 |       for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 2401 |         const v = freq[i]! / 255;
 2402 |         weighted += i * v;
 2403 |         total += v;
 2404 |       }
 2405 | 
 2406 |       const centroid = total > 0 ? weighted / total / n : 0;
 2407 | 
 2408 |       audioSurface.set({
 2409 |         rms,
 2410 |         bass,
 2411 |         mid,
 2412 |         treble,
 2413 |         centroid,
 2414 |         energy: Math.min(1, rms * 2),
 2415 |       });
 2416 | 
 2417 |       raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 2418 |     };
 2419 | 
 2420 |     raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 2421 | 
 2422 |     return () => {
 2423 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
 2424 |     };
 2425 |   }, [audioAnalysisReady, p.status]);

 2632 |     const reportTelemetryPlay = (params: {
 2633 |       recordingId: string;
 2634 |       playbackId: string;
 2635 |       progressMs: number;
 2636 |       durationMs: number | null;
 2637 |     }) => {
 2638 |       const { recordingId, playbackId, progressMs, durationMs } = params;
 2639 |       if (!recordingId || !playbackId) return;
 2640 | 
 2641 |       const sessionKey = `${recordingId}:${playbackId}`;
 2642 |       const sentKey = `${sessionKey}:play`;
 2643 | 
 2644 |       if (telemetryPlaySentRef.current.has(sentKey)) return;
 2645 | 
 2646 |       const prevProgress =
 2647 |         telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.get(sessionKey);
 2648 |       telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, progressMs);
 2649 | 
 2650 |       if (prevProgress == null) {
 2651 |         telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, 0);
 2652 |         return;
 2653 |       }
 2654 | 
 2655 |       const deltaMs = progressMs - prevProgress;
 2656 | 
 2657 |       if (deltaMs <= 0 || deltaMs > 5_000) {
 2658 |         telemetryPlayLastProgressMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, progressMs);
 2659 |         return;
 2660 |       }
 2661 | 
 2662 |       const accumulatedMs =
 2663 |         (telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.get(sessionKey) ?? 0) + deltaMs;
 2664 | 
 2665 |       telemetryPlayAccumulatedMsRef.current.set(sessionKey, accumulatedMs);
 2666 | 
 2667 |       if (accumulatedMs < TELEMETRY_PLAY_THRESHOLD_MS) return;
 2668 | 
 2669 |       telemetryPlaySentRef.current.add(sentKey);

 2826 |         a.currentTime = Math.max(0, ms / 1000);
 2827 |       } catch {}
 2828 | 
 2829 |       pRef.current.clearPendingSeek();
 2830 |     };
 2831 | 
 2832 |     const handleAutoAdvance = async (): Promise<void> => {
 2833 |       const s = pRef.current;
 2834 |       const cur = s.current;
 2835 |       const nextTrack = getNextTrack();
 2836 | 
 2837 |       if (!cur || !nextTrack) return;
 2838 | 
 2839 |       const key = `${cur.recordingId}:${
 2840 |         telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? cur.muxPlaybackId ?? ""
 2841 |       }`;
 2842 | 
 2843 |       if (autoAdvanceKeyRef.current === key) return;
 2844 |       autoAdvanceKeyRef.current = key;
 2845 | 
 2846 |       const activeAudio = getAudio(activeDeckRef.current);
 2847 |       const currentTimeMs = activeAudio
 2848 |         ? Math.round(Math.max(0, activeAudio.currentTime) * 1000)
 2849 |         : 0;
 2850 |       const liveAssetDurationMs = activeAudio
 2851 |         ? readFiniteMediaDurationMs(activeAudio)
 2852 |         : 0;
 2853 |       const catalogueDurationMs =
 2854 |         s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] ?? cur.durationMs ?? 0;
 2855 |       const cachedAssetDurationMs = cur.muxPlaybackId
 2856 |         ? (s.assetDurationByPlaybackId[cur.muxPlaybackId] ?? 0)
 2857 |         : 0;
 2858 | 
 2859 |       sendAudioDebug({
 2860 |         event: "auto-next-from-ended",
 2861 |         albumId: s.queueContextId ?? null,
 2862 |         recordingId: cur.recordingId,
 2863 |         playbackId: cur.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 2864 |         source: "AudioEngine",
 2865 |         detail: JSON.stringify({
 2866 |           nextRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
 2867 |           currentTimeMs,
 2868 |           liveAssetDurationMs,
 2869 |           cachedAssetDurationMs,
 2870 |           catalogueDurationMs,
 2871 |         }),
 2872 |       });
 2873 | 
 2874 |       // The next track has already been authorised by either the full album
 2875 |       // session or the bounded anonymous sample session. Completion telemetry
 2876 |       // must never delay the deck handoff on a locked/background device.
 2877 |       const completionDurationMs =
 2878 |         liveAssetDurationMs || cachedAssetDurationMs || catalogueDurationMs;
 2879 | 
 2880 |       void reportTelemetryComplete({
 2881 |         recordingId: cur.recordingId,
 2882 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 2883 |         progressMs: currentTimeMs,
 2884 |         durationMs: completionDurationMs || null,
 2885 |       });
 2886 | 
 2887 |       const standbyReady = await prepareStandbyForTrack(nextTrack);
 2888 | 
 2889 |       if (!standbyReady) {
 2890 |         sendAudioDebug({
 2891 |           event: "standby-not-ready-fallback-state-advance",
 2892 |           albumId: s.queueContextId ?? null,
 2893 |           recordingId: cur.recordingId,
 2894 |           playbackId: cur.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 2895 |           source: "AudioEngine",
 2896 |           detail: `next=${nextTrack.recordingId}`,
 2897 |         });
 2898 | 
 2899 |         playIntentRef.current = true;
 2900 |         pRef.current.advanceFromEngine();
 2901 |         return;
 2902 |       }
 2903 | 
 2904 |       const activeMeta = metaByDeckRef.current[activeDeckRef.current];
 2905 | 
 2906 |       const shouldUseAppleMobileActiveDeckAdvance =
 2907 |         isAppleMobileWebKit() &&
 2908 |         typeof document !== "undefined" &&
 2909 |         document.visibilityState === "hidden" &&
 2910 |         activeMeta?.hlsPath === "static-m4a";
 2911 | 
 2912 |       if (shouldUseAppleMobileActiveDeckAdvance) {
 2913 |         sendAudioDebug({
 2914 |           event: "apple-mobile-static-active-deck-advance",
 2915 |           albumId: s.queueContextId ?? null,
 2916 |           recordingId: cur.recordingId,
 2917 |           playbackId: cur.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 2918 |           source: `AudioEngine.${activeDeckRef.current}`,
 2919 |           detail: `next=${nextTrack.recordingId}`,
 2920 |         });
 2921 | 
 2922 |         playIntentRef.current = true;
 2923 |         pRef.current.advanceFromEngine();
 2924 |         return;
 2925 |       }
 2926 | 
 2927 |       const promoted = await promoteStandby(nextTrack);
 2928 | 
 2929 |       if (!promoted) {
 2930 |         sendAudioDebug({
 2931 |           event: "standby-promote-failed-fallback-state-advance",
 2932 |           albumId: s.queueContextId ?? null,
 2933 |           recordingId: cur.recordingId,
 2934 |           playbackId: cur.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 2935 |           source: "AudioEngine",
 2936 |           detail: `next=${nextTrack.recordingId}`,
 2937 |         });
 2938 | 
 2939 |         playIntentRef.current = true;
 2940 |         pRef.current.advanceFromEngine();
 2941 |       }
 2942 |     };
 2943 | 
 2944 |     const hasPreparedStandbyForTrack = (nextTrack: PlayerTrack): boolean => {
 2945 |       if (isAppleMobileWebKit()) return false;
 2946 | 
 2947 |       const playbackId = (nextTrack.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2948 |       const prepared = standbyRef.current;
 2949 | 
 2950 |       if (
 2951 |         !playbackId ||
 2952 |         !prepared ||
 2953 |         prepared.recordingId !== nextTrack.recordingId ||
 2954 |         prepared.playbackId !== playbackId
 2955 |       ) {
 2956 |         return false;
 2957 |       }
 2958 | 
 2959 |       const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[prepared.deckId];
 2960 | 
 2961 |       return Boolean(
 2962 |         meta &&
 2963 |         meta.prepared &&
 2964 |         meta.attachKey === prepared.attachKey &&
 2965 |         meta.recordingId === nextTrack.recordingId &&
 2966 |         meta.playbackId === playbackId,
 2967 |       );
 2968 |     };

 2970 |     const armEarlyHandoff = (args: {
 2971 |       deckId: DeckId;
 2972 |       currentTrack: PlayerTrack;
 2973 |       nextTrack: PlayerTrack;
 2974 |     }): void => {
 2975 |       if (
 2976 |         GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_LEAD_MS <= 0 ||
 2977 |         isAppleMobileWebKit() ||
 2978 |         earlyHandoffFrame != null
 2979 |       ) {
 2980 |         return;
 2981 |       }
 2982 | 
 2983 |       const currentPlaybackId = (args.currentTrack.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2984 |       const handoffKey = `${args.currentTrack.recordingId}:${
 2985 |         args.nextTrack.recordingId
 2986 |       }:${telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? currentPlaybackId}`;
 2987 | 
 2988 |       if (earlyHandoffKey === handoffKey) return;
 2989 |       earlyHandoffKey = handoffKey;
 2990 | 
 2991 |       const tick = () => {
 2992 |         earlyHandoffFrame = null;
 2993 | 
 2994 |         const activeAudio = getAudio(args.deckId);
 2995 |         const player = pRef.current;
 2996 |         const currentTrack = player.current;
 2997 |         const playbackId = (currentTrack?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 2998 | 
 2999 |         if (
 3000 |           !activeAudio ||
 3001 |           activeDeckRef.current !== args.deckId ||
 3002 |           !currentTrack ||
 3003 |           currentTrack.recordingId !== args.currentTrack.recordingId ||
 3004 |           playbackId !== currentPlaybackId ||
 3005 |           activeAudio.paused ||
 3006 |           activeAudio.ended
 3007 |         ) {
 3008 |           earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3009 |           return;
 3010 |         }
 3011 | 
 3012 |         const durationMs =
 3013 |           readFiniteMediaDurationMs(activeAudio) ||
 3014 |           (playbackId
 3015 |             ? (player.assetDurationByPlaybackId[playbackId] ?? 0)
 3016 |             : 0) ||
 3017 |           player.durationByRecordingId[currentTrack.recordingId] ||
 3018 |           currentTrack.durationMs ||
 3019 |           0;
 3020 | 
 3021 |         if (durationMs <= 0) {
 3022 |           earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3023 |           return;
 3024 |         }
 3025 | 
 3026 |         const remainingMs =
 3027 |           durationMs - Math.floor(Math.max(0, activeAudio.currentTime) * 1000);
 3028 | 
 3029 |         if (remainingMs > GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_LEAD_MS) {
 3030 |           earlyHandoffFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 3031 |           return;
 3032 |         }
 3033 | 
 3034 |         earlyHandoffKey = null;
 3035 | 
 3036 |         if (remainingMs < 0 || !hasPreparedStandbyForTrack(args.nextTrack)) {
 3037 |           return;
 3038 |         }
 3039 | 
 3040 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3041 |           event: "gapless-early-promotion",
 3042 |           albumId: player.queueContextId ?? null,
 3043 |           recordingId: currentTrack.recordingId,
 3044 |           playbackId,
 3045 |           source: `AudioEngine.${args.deckId}`,
 3046 |           detail: `remaining=${remainingMs};next=${args.nextTrack.recordingId}`,
 3047 |         });
 3048 | 
 3049 |         void handleAutoAdvance();
 3050 |       };
 3051 | 
 3052 |       earlyHandoffFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 3053 |     };
 3054 | 
 3055 |     const onTime = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3056 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3057 | 
 3058 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 3059 |       if (!a) return;
 3060 | 
 3061 |       const ms = Math.floor(a.currentTime * 1000);
 3062 |       mediaSurface.setTime(ms);
 3063 |       pRef.current.setPositionMs(ms);
 3064 | 
 3065 |       const currentTrack = pRef.current.current;
 3066 |       const curId = currentTrack?.recordingId ?? "";
 3067 |       const currentPlaybackId = (currentTrack?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 3068 | 
 3069 |       const catalogueDurationMs =
 3070 |         (curId ? pRef.current.durationByRecordingId[curId] : 0) ||
 3071 |         currentTrack?.durationMs ||
 3072 |         0;
 3073 | 
 3074 |       const cachedAssetDurationMs = currentPlaybackId
 3075 |         ? (pRef.current.assetDurationByPlaybackId[currentPlaybackId] ?? 0)
 3076 |         : 0;
 3077 | 
 3078 |       // The live element is closest to the actual delivered rendition and is
 3079 |       // therefore authoritative for playback, telemetry, and end detection.
 3080 |       const liveAssetDurationMs = readFiniteMediaDurationMs(a);
 3081 | 
 3082 |       const durMs =
 3083 |         liveAssetDurationMs || cachedAssetDurationMs || catalogueDurationMs;
 3084 | 
 3085 |       if (durMs <= 0) return;
 3086 | 
 3087 |       const heartbeatBucket = Math.floor(ms / 60_000);
 3088 |       const heartbeatKey = `${curId}:${heartbeatBucket}`;
 3089 | 
 3090 |       if (
 3091 |         heartbeatBucket > 0 &&
 3092 |         debugProgressHeartbeatRef.current !== heartbeatKey
 3093 |       ) {
 3094 |         debugProgressHeartbeatRef.current = heartbeatKey;
 3095 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3096 |           event: "playback-progress-heartbeat",
 3097 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3098 |           recordingId: curId,
 3099 |           playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 3100 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3101 |           detail: `progress=${ms};duration=${durMs}`,
 3102 |         });
 3103 |       }
 3104 | 
 3105 |       setMediaSessionPositionStateSafe({
 3106 |         durationSec: durMs / 1000,
 3107 |         positionSec: ms / 1000,
 3108 |         playbackRate: 1,
 3109 |       });
 3110 | 
 3111 |       reportTelemetryPlay({
 3112 |         recordingId: curId,
 3113 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3114 |         progressMs: ms,
 3115 |         durationMs: durMs,
 3116 |       });
 3117 | 
 3118 |       reportTelemetryProgress({
 3119 |         recordingId: curId,
 3120 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3121 |         progressMs: ms,
 3122 |         durationMs: durMs,
 3123 |       });
 3124 | 
 3125 |       const deckMeta = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];
 3126 |       const staticM4aProgressProofKey = `${curId}:${
 3127 |         telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? ""
 3128 |       }`;
 3129 | 
 3130 |       if (
 3131 |         deckMeta?.hlsPath === "static-m4a" &&
 3132 |         ms >= 5_000 &&
 3133 |         staticM4aProgressProofRef.current !== staticM4aProgressProofKey
 3134 |       ) {
 3135 |         staticM4aProgressProofRef.current = staticM4aProgressProofKey;
 3136 | 
 3137 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3138 |           event: "static-m4a-progress-confirmed",
 3139 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3140 |           recordingId: curId,
 3141 |           playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 3142 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3143 |           detail: JSON.stringify({
 3144 |             visibility:
 3145 |               typeof document === "undefined" ? null : document.visibilityState,
 3146 |             currentTimeSec: Number(a.currentTime.toFixed(3)),
 3147 |             bufferedAheadSec: Number(bufferedAheadSeconds(a).toFixed(3)),
 3148 |           }),
 3149 |         });
 3150 |       }
 3151 | 
 3152 |       const remainingMs = durMs - ms;
 3153 |       const nextTrack = getNextTrack();
 3154 | 
 3155 |       if (
 3156 |         nextTrack &&
 3157 |         remainingMs > 0 &&
 3158 |         remainingMs <= STANDBY_PREPARE_WINDOW_MS
 3159 |       ) {
 3160 |         const warmKey = `${curId}:${nextTrack.recordingId}:${
 3161 |           telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? ""
 3162 |         }`;
 3163 | 
 3164 |         if (nearEndWarmKeyRef.current !== warmKey) {
 3165 |           nearEndWarmKeyRef.current = warmKey;
 3166 |           void prefetchCurrentQueueAlbumSession();
 3167 |           void prepareStandbyForTrack(nextTrack);
 3168 |         }
 3169 |       }
 3170 | 
 3171 |       if (
 3172 |         currentTrack &&
 3173 |         nextTrack &&
 3174 |         remainingMs > GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_LEAD_MS &&
 3175 |         remainingMs <= GAPLESS_EARLY_PROMOTION_ARM_WINDOW_MS
 3176 |       ) {
 3177 |         armEarlyHandoff({
 3178 |           deckId,
 3179 |           currentTrack,
 3180 |           nextTrack,
 3181 |         });
 3182 |       }
 3183 | 
 3184 |       const pct = ms / durMs;
 3185 | 
 3186 |       if (pct >= 0.9) {
 3187 |         void reportTelemetryComplete({
 3188 |           recordingId: curId,
 3189 |           playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3190 |           progressMs: ms,
 3191 |           durationMs: durMs,
 3192 |         });
 3193 |       }
 3194 |     };
 3195 | 
 3196 |     const onLoadedMeta = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3197 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 3198 |       const meta = metaByDeckRef.current[deckId];
 3199 | 
 3200 |       if (!a || !meta) return;
 3201 | 
 3202 |       const assetDurationMs = readFiniteMediaDurationMs(a);
 3203 | 
 3204 |       if (assetDurationMs > 0) {
 3205 |         pRef.current.setAssetDurationMs(meta.playbackId, assetDurationMs);
 3206 |       }

 3209 |     const markPlaying = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3210 |       debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-playing");
 3211 | 
 3212 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3213 | 
 3214 |       if (engineBlockedRef.current) {
 3215 |         hardStopAll();
 3216 |         mediaSurface.setStatus("blocked");
 3217 |         return;
 3218 |       }
 3219 | 
 3220 |       mediaSurface.setStatus("playing");
 3221 | 
 3222 |       if (hasMediaSession()) {
 3223 |         try {
 3224 |           navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = "playing";
 3225 |         } catch {}
 3226 |       }
 3227 | 
 3228 |       pRef.current.setStatusExternal("playing");
 3229 |       pRef.current.setLoadingReasonExternal(undefined);
 3230 |       pRef.current.clearIntent();
 3231 | 
 3232 |       applyPendingSeek(deckId);
 3233 | 
 3234 |       const curId = pRef.current.current?.recordingId;
 3235 |       if (curId) pRef.current.resolvePendingTrack(curId);
 3236 |     };
 3237 | 
 3238 |     const markPaused = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3239 |       debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-paused");
 3240 | 
 3241 |       if (suppressPauseDeckRef.current === deckId) {
 3242 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3243 |           event: "media-paused-during-deck-promotion-ignored",
 3244 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3245 |           recordingId: metaByDeckRef.current[deckId]?.recordingId ?? null,
 3246 |           playbackId: metaByDeckRef.current[deckId]?.playbackId ?? null,
 3247 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3248 |         });
 3249 |         return;
 3250 |       }
 3251 | 
 3252 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3253 | 
 3254 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 3255 |       if (!a) return;
 3256 | 
 3257 |       const effectivelyEnded =
 3258 |         a.ended ||
 3259 |         (Number.isFinite(a.duration) &&
 3260 |           a.duration > 0 &&
 3261 |           a.currentTime >= a.duration - 0.25);
 3262 | 
 3263 |       if (effectivelyEnded) {
 3264 |         sendAudioDebug({
 3265 |           event: "media-paused-at-ended-ignored",
 3266 |           albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3267 |           recordingId: pRef.current.current?.recordingId ?? null,
 3268 |           playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 3269 |           source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3270 |         });
 3271 |         return;
 3272 |       }
 3273 | 
 3274 |       if (engineBlockedRef.current) return;
 3275 | 
 3276 |       mediaSurface.setStatus("paused");
 3277 | 
 3278 |       if (hasMediaSession()) {
 3279 |         try {
 3280 |           navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = "paused";
 3281 |         } catch {}
 3282 |       }
 3283 | 
 3284 |       pRef.current.setStatusExternal("paused");
 3285 |       pRef.current.setLoadingReasonExternal(undefined);
 3286 |       pRef.current.clearIntent();
 3287 |     };

 3289 |     const markBuffering = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3290 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3291 | 
 3292 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 3293 |       if (!a) return;
 3294 | 
 3295 |       const falsePositiveWhilePlaying =
 3296 |         !a.paused &&
 3297 |         !a.ended &&
 3298 |         a.readyState >= HTMLMediaElement.HAVE_FUTURE_DATA;
 3299 | 
 3300 |       if (falsePositiveWhilePlaying) return;
 3301 | 
 3302 |       debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-buffering");
 3303 | 
 3304 |       if (engineBlockedRef.current) return;
 3305 | 
 3306 |       const s = pRef.current;
 3307 |       const shouldBePlaying =
 3308 |         s.intent === "play" || s.status === "playing" || s.status === "loading";
 3309 | 
 3310 |       if (!shouldBePlaying) return;
 3311 | 
 3312 |       mediaSurface.setStatus("loading");
 3313 |       s.setStatusExternal("loading");
 3314 |       s.setLoadingReasonExternal("buffering");
 3315 |     };
 3316 | 
 3317 |     const clearBuffering = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3318 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3319 | 
 3320 |       debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-buffering-cleared");
 3321 |

 3325 |       applyPendingSeek(deckId);
 3326 |     };

 3328 |     const onEnded = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3329 |       if (deckId !== activeDeckRef.current) return;
 3330 | 
 3331 |       debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-ended");
 3332 | 
 3333 |       const nextTrack = getNextTrack();
 3334 | 
 3335 |       sendAudioDebug({
 3336 |         event: "ended-fired",
 3337 |         albumId: pRef.current.queueContextId ?? null,
 3338 |         recordingId: pRef.current.current?.recordingId ?? null,
 3339 |         playbackId: pRef.current.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null,
 3340 |         source: `AudioEngine.${deckId}`,
 3341 |         detail: nextTrack
 3342 |           ? `next=${nextTrack.recordingId}`
 3343 |           : `next=null;queue=${pRef.current.queue.length};repeat=${pRef.current.repeat}`,
 3344 |       });
 3345 | 
 3346 |       const currentTrack = pRef.current.current;
 3347 |       const recordingId = currentTrack?.recordingId ?? "";
 3348 |       const currentPlaybackId = (currentTrack?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
 3349 |       const activeAudio = getAudio(deckId);
 3350 | 
 3351 |       const liveDurationMs = activeAudio
 3352 |         ? readFiniteMediaDurationMs(activeAudio)
 3353 |         : 0;
 3354 | 
 3355 |       const fallbackDurationMs =
 3356 |         (currentPlaybackId
 3357 |           ? (pRef.current.assetDurationByPlaybackId[currentPlaybackId] ?? 0)
 3358 |           : 0) ||
 3359 |         pRef.current.durationByRecordingId[recordingId] ||
 3360 |         currentTrack?.durationMs ||
 3361 |         0;
 3362 | 
 3363 |       const progressMs = activeAudio
 3364 |         ? Math.floor(Math.max(0, activeAudio.currentTime) * 1000)
 3365 |         : fallbackDurationMs;
 3366 | 
 3367 |       void reportTelemetryComplete({
 3368 |         recordingId,
 3369 |         playbackId: telemetrySessionIdRef.current ?? "",
 3370 |         progressMs,
 3371 |         durationMs: liveDurationMs || fallbackDurationMs || null,
 3372 |       });
 3373 | 
 3374 |       if (nextTrack) {
 3375 |         void handleAutoAdvance();
 3376 |       } else {
 3377 |         pRef.current.advanceFromEngine();
 3378 |       }
 3379 |     };
 3380 | 
 3381 |     const makeHandlers = (deckId: DeckId) => {
 3382 |       const a = getAudio(deckId);
 3383 |       if (!a) return null;
 3384 | 
 3385 |       const handlers = {
 3386 |         timeupdate: () => onTime(deckId),
 3387 |         loadedmetadata: () => onLoadedMeta(deckId),
 3388 |         playing: () => markPlaying(deckId),
 3389 |         pause: () => markPaused(deckId),
 3390 |         waiting: () => markBuffering(deckId),
 3391 |         stalled: () => markBuffering(deckId),
 3392 |         canplay: () => clearBuffering(deckId),
 3393 |         canplaythrough: () => clearBuffering(deckId),
 3394 |         ended: () => onEnded(deckId),
 3395 |         error: () => {
 3396 |           const err = a.error;
 3397 |           debugMediaEvent(
 3398 |             deckId,
 3399 |             "media-error",
 3400 |             err ? `code=${err.code};message=${err.message}` : "unknown",
 3401 |           );
 3402 |         },
 3403 |         suspend: () => debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-suspend"),
 3404 |         abort: () => debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-abort"),
 3405 |         emptied: () => debugMediaEvent(deckId, "media-emptied"),
 3406 |       };
 3407 | 
 3408 |       a.addEventListener("timeupdate", handlers.timeupdate);
 3409 |       a.addEventListener("loadedmetadata", handlers.loadedmetadata);
 3410 |       a.addEventListener("playing", handlers.playing);
 3411 |       a.addEventListener("pause", handlers.pause);
 3412 |       a.addEventListener("waiting", handlers.waiting);
 3413 |       a.addEventListener("stalled", handlers.stalled);
 3414 |       a.addEventListener("canplay", handlers.canplay);
 3415 |       a.addEventListener("canplaythrough", handlers.canplaythrough);
 3416 |       a.addEventListener("ended", handlers.ended);
 3417 |       a.addEventListener("error", handlers.error);
 3418 |       a.addEventListener("suspend", handlers.suspend);
 3419 |       a.addEventListener("abort", handlers.abort);
 3420 |       a.addEventListener("emptied", handlers.emptied);
 3421 | 
 3422 |       return () => {
 3423 |         a.removeEventListener("timeupdate", handlers.timeupdate);
 3424 |         a.removeEventListener("loadedmetadata", handlers.loadedmetadata);
 3425 |         a.removeEventListener("playing", handlers.playing);
 3426 |         a.removeEventListener("pause", handlers.pause);
 3427 |         a.removeEventListener("waiting", handlers.waiting);
 3428 |         a.removeEventListener("stalled", handlers.stalled);
 3429 |         a.removeEventListener("canplay", handlers.canplay);
 3430 |         a.removeEventListener("canplaythrough", handlers.canplaythrough);
 3431 |         a.removeEventListener("ended", handlers.ended);
 3432 |         a.removeEventListener("error", handlers.error);
 3433 |         a.removeEventListener("suspend", handlers.suspend);
 3434 |         a.removeEventListener("abort", handlers.abort);
 3435 |         a.removeEventListener("emptied", handlers.emptied);
 3436 |       };
 3437 |     };
 3438 | 
 3439 |     const cleanupA = makeHandlers("a");
 3440 |     const cleanupB = makeHandlers("b");
 3441 | 
 3442 |     return () => {
 3443 |       if (earlyHandoffFrame != null) {
 3444 |         window.cancelAnimationFrame(earlyHandoffFrame);
 3445 |       }
 3446 | 
 3447 |       cleanupA?.();
 3448 |       cleanupB?.();
 3449 |     };
 3450 |   }, [
 3451 |     announceBadges,
 3452 |     getAudio,

  259 | function isStreamingPlatform(v: unknown): v is StreamingPlatform {
  260 |   return (
  261 |     v === "spotify" ||
  262 |     v === "appleMusic" ||

  270 | function isPlatformLink(v: unknown): v is PlatformLink {
  271 |   if (typeof v !== "object" || v === null) return false;
  272 | 
  273 |   const maybe = v as Record<string, unknown>;
  274 | 
  275 |   return (
  276 |     isStreamingPlatform(maybe.platform) &&
  277 |     typeof maybe.url === "string" &&
  278 |     /^https:\/\//i.test(maybe.url)
  279 |   );
  280 | }

  285 |     | null
  286 |     | undefined,
  287 | ): PlatformLink[] {
  288 |   if (!Array.isArray(links)) return [];
  289 |   return links.filter(isPlatformLink);

  376 | 
  377 | function AvailableOnRibbon({
  378 |   links,
  379 | }: Readonly<{ links: readonly PlatformLink[] }>) {
  380 |   if (!links.length) return null;
  381 | 
  382 |   const order: StreamingPlatform[] = [
  383 |     "spotify",
  384 |     "appleMusic",
  385 |     "youtubeMusic",
  386 |     "amazonMusic",
  387 |     "tidal",
  388 |     "deezer",
  389 |   ];
  390 | 
  391 |   const byPlatform = new Map<StreamingPlatform, string>();
  392 |   for (const l of links) byPlatform.set(l.platform, l.url);
  393 | 
  394 |   const ordered = order
  395 |     .map((p) => ({ platform: p, url: byPlatform.get(p) }))
  396 |     .filter((x): x is { platform: StreamingPlatform; url: string } =>
  397 |       Boolean(x.url),
  398 |     );
  399 | 
  400 |   if (!ordered.length) return null;
  401 | 
  402 |   return (
  403 |     <div className="afAvailWrap" aria-label="Available on">
  404 |       <div className="afAvailLine" aria-hidden="true" />
  405 |       <div className="afAvailLabel">AVAILABLE ON</div>
  406 | 
  407 |       <div className="afAvailIcons">
  408 |         {ordered.map(({ platform, url }) => (
  409 |           <a
  410 |             key={platform}
  411 |             href={url}
  412 |             target="_blank"
  413 |             rel="noopener noreferrer external nofollow"
  414 |             className="afAvailIcon"
  415 |             aria-label={`Open on ${platform}`}
  416 |             title={`Open on ${platform}`}
  417 |           >
  418 |             <PlatformIcon platform={platform} />
  419 |           </a>
  420 |         ))}
  421 |       </div>
  422 |     </div>
  423 |   );
  424 | }

  428 | type FullPlayerProps = Readonly<{
  429 |   bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
  430 |   albums: AlbumNavItem[];
  431 |   onSelectAlbum?: (slug: string) => void;
  432 |   isBrowsingAlbum?: boolean;
  433 |   tier?: Tier;

  436 | type EffectivePlayerView = Readonly<{
  437 |   albumSlug: string;
  438 |   album: AlbumPlayerBundle["album"];
  439 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[];
  440 | }>;
  441 | 
  442 | function resolveAccessIdentityKey(
  443 |   params: Readonly<{
  444 |     clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
  445 |     isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
  446 |     userId: string | null | undefined;
  447 |     sessionId: string | null | undefined;
  448 |   }>,
  449 | ): string {
  450 |   if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
  451 |   if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
  452 | 
  453 |   return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
  454 | }
  455 | 
  456 | function resolveEffectivePlayerView(
  457 |   params: Readonly<{
  458 |     bundle: AlbumPlayerBundle;
  459 |     stableView: StableView | null;
  460 |     isBrowsingAlbum: boolean;
  461 |   }>,
  462 | ): EffectivePlayerView {
  463 |   const { bundle, stableView, isBrowsingAlbum } = params;
  464 |   const shouldUseStableView = Boolean(
  465 |     isBrowsingAlbum &&
  466 |     stableView?.album &&
  467 |     (!bundle.album || bundle.tracks.length === 0),
  468 |   );
  469 | 
  470 |   if (shouldUseStableView && stableView) {
  471 |     return {
  472 |       albumSlug: stableView.albumSlug,
  473 |       album: stableView.album,
  474 |       tracks: stableView.tracks,
  475 |     };
  476 |   }
  477 | 
  478 |   return {
  479 |     albumSlug: bundle.albumSlug,
  480 |     album: bundle.album,
  481 |     tracks: bundle.tracks,
  482 |   };
  483 | }
  484 | 
  485 | function accessStatesEqual(
  486 |   left: AccessState | null,
  487 |   right: AccessState | null,
  488 | ): boolean {
  489 |   if (!left || !right) return left === right;
  490 |   return JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right);
  491 | }
  492 | 
  493 | function mergeAccessState(
  494 |   previous: AccessState | null,
  495 |   next: AccessState | null,
  496 | ): AccessState | null {
  497 |   return accessStatesEqual(previous, next) ? previous : next;
  498 | }
  499 | 
  500 | function isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code: string): boolean {
  501 |   return (
  502 |     code === "AUTH_REQUIRED" ||
  503 |     code === "CAP_REACHED" ||
  504 |     code === "ANON_CAP_REACHED"
  505 |   );
  506 | }
  507 | 
  508 | function hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(
  509 |   player: PlayerApi,
  510 |   pendingInAlbum: boolean,
  511 | ): boolean {
  512 |   return (
  513 |     player.playRequestPending ||
  514 |     player.intent === "play" ||
  515 |     player.status === "loading" ||
  516 |     player.status === "playing" ||
  517 |     player.status === "paused" ||
  518 |     pendingInAlbum
  519 |   );
  520 | }
  521 | 
  522 | function reconcilePlayerWithAccessDecision(
  523 |   params: Readonly<{
  524 |     player: PlayerApi;
  525 |     decision: AccessState;
  526 |     recordingIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  527 |     albumKey: string | null;
  528 |   }>,
  529 | ): void {
  530 |   const { player, decision, recordingIds, albumKey } = params;
  531 | 
  532 |   if (decision.allowed) {
  533 |     if (player.lastError) player.clearError();
  534 |     return;
  535 |   }
  536 | 
  537 |   const currentInAlbum = Boolean(
  538 |     player.current?.recordingId && recordingIds.has(player.current.recordingId),
  539 |   );
  540 |   const pendingInAlbum = Boolean(
  541 |     player.pendingRecordingId && recordingIds.has(player.pendingRecordingId),
  542 |   );
  543 |   const queueIsAlbum = Boolean(albumKey && player.queueContextId === albumKey);
  544 |   const hasIntent = hasPlaybackIntentForAlbum(player, pendingInAlbum);
  545 |   const shouldBlockAlbum =
  546 |     currentInAlbum || pendingInAlbum || (queueIsAlbum && hasIntent);
  547 | 
  548 |   const code = typeof decision.code === "string" ? decision.code.trim() : "";
  549 |   if (!shouldBlockAlbum || (isAuthOrLegacyCapCode(code) && !hasIntent)) return;
  550 | 
  551 |   try {
  552 |     player.pause();
  553 |   } catch {
  554 |     // ignore
  555 |   }
  556 | 
  557 |   if (player.status === "loading") {
  558 |     player.setStatusExternal("idle");
  559 |   }
  560 | }
  561 | 
  562 | function accessFailureFallback(catalogueId: string): AccessState {
  563 |   return {
  564 |     forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
  565 |     allowed: true,
  566 |     embargoed: false,
  567 |     releaseAt: null,
  568 |     code: "ACCESS_CHECK_ERROR",
  569 |     reason: "Access check failed (client).",
  570 |     corr: null,
  571 |     shareTokenAccess: null,
  572 |     sharePlaybackContext: null,
  573 |     sharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  574 |   };
  575 | }
  576 | 
  577 | function resolveAccessForAlbum(
  578 |   catalogueId: string | null,
  579 |   access: AccessState | null,
  580 | ): AccessState | null {
  581 |   if (!catalogueId) return null;
  582 |   if (access?.forCatalogueId !== catalogueId) return null;
  583 |   return access;
  584 | }
  585 | 
  586 | function isPlayerPlayingish(player: PlayerApi): boolean {
  587 |   return (
  588 |     player.status === "playing" ||
  589 |     player.status === "loading" ||
  590 |     player.playRequestPending
  591 |   );
  592 | }

  594 | type PlaybackAccessUiState = Readonly<{
  595 |   accessKnown: boolean;
  596 |   isPlaybackGate: boolean;
  597 |   canPlay: boolean;
  598 |   canAttemptPlay: boolean;
  599 | }>;
  600 | 
  601 | function derivePlaybackAccessUiState(
  602 |   tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[],
  603 |   accessForAlbum: AccessState | null,
  604 | ): PlaybackAccessUiState {
  605 |   const accessKnown = accessForAlbum !== null;
  606 |   const hasPlayableTracks = tracks.length > 0;
  607 |   const isPlaybackGate = Boolean(
  608 |     hasPlayableTracks &&
  609 |     accessKnown &&
  610 |     accessForAlbum?.allowed === false &&
  611 |     isPlaybackGateCode(accessForAlbum.code),
  612 |   );
  613 |   const canPlay = Boolean(
  614 |     hasPlayableTracks && accessKnown && accessForAlbum?.allowed !== false,
  615 |   );
  616 | 
  617 |   return {
  618 |     accessKnown,
  619 |     isPlaybackGate,
  620 |     canPlay,
  621 |     canAttemptPlay: canPlay || isPlaybackGate,
  622 |   };
  623 | }
  624 | 
  625 | type EmbargoUiState = Readonly<{
  626 |   releaseAtMs: number;
  627 |   showEmbargo: boolean;
  628 |   showShareTokenEmbargoAccess: boolean;
  629 | }>;
  630 | 
  631 | function deriveEmbargoUiState(
  632 |   params: Readonly<{
  633 |     embargo: NonNullable<AlbumPlayerBundle["album"]>["embargo"];
  634 |     accessForAlbum: AccessState | null;
  635 |     shareToken: string | null;
  636 |   }>,
  637 | ): EmbargoUiState {
  638 |   const releaseAtMs = params.embargo?.releaseAt
  639 |     ? Date.parse(params.embargo.releaseAt)
  640 |     : Number.NaN;
  641 |   const accessResolved = params.accessForAlbum !== null;
  642 |   const accessAllowsEmbargoBypass = params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true;
  643 |   const showEmbargo = Boolean(
  644 |     params.embargo?.embargoed &&
  645 |     Number.isFinite(releaseAtMs) &&
  646 |     !accessAllowsEmbargoBypass &&
  647 |     (!params.shareToken || accessResolved),
  648 |   );
  649 |   const showShareTokenEmbargoAccess = Boolean(
  650 |     params.embargo?.embargoed &&
  651 |     params.accessForAlbum?.allowed === true &&
  652 |     params.accessForAlbum.shareTokenAccess,
  653 |   );
  654 | 
  655 |   return { releaseAtMs, showEmbargo, showShareTokenEmbargoAccess };
  656 | }
  657 | 
  658 | function isAlbumPlaybackActive(
  659 |   albumKey: string | null,
  660 |   queueContextId: string | undefined,
  661 | ): boolean {
  662 |   return Boolean(albumKey && queueContextId === albumKey);
  663 | }
  664 | 
  665 | type AlbumTransportState = Readonly<{
  666 |   albumIdx: number;
  667 |   prevDisabled: boolean;
  668 |   nextDisabled: boolean;
  669 | }>;
  670 | 
  671 | function deriveAlbumTransportState(
  672 |   params: Readonly<{
  673 |     currentRecordingId: string;
  674 |     tracks: readonly PlayerTrack[];
  675 |     transportLock: boolean;
  676 |   }>,
  677 | ): AlbumTransportState {
  678 |   const albumIdx = params.currentRecordingId
  679 |     ? params.tracks.findIndex(
  680 |         (track) => track.recordingId === params.currentRecordingId,
  681 |       )
  682 |     : -1;
  683 | 
  684 |   if (albumIdx < 0) {
  685 |     return { albumIdx, prevDisabled: true, nextDisabled: true };
  686 |   }
  687 | 
  688 |   return {
  689 |     albumIdx,
  690 |     prevDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === 0,
  691 |     nextDisabled: params.transportLock || albumIdx === params.tracks.length - 1,
  692 |   };
  693 | }
  694 | 
  695 | function isCoarsePointerDevice(): boolean {
  696 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
  697 | 
  698 |   try {
  699 |     return window.matchMedia?.("(pointer: coarse)").matches ?? false;
  700 |   } catch {
  701 |     return "ontouchstart" in window;
  702 |   }
  703 | }

  705 | function trackRowColors(
  706 |   canPlay: boolean,
  707 |   isCurrent: boolean,
  708 | ): Readonly<{ titleColor: string; subColor: string }> {
  709 |   if (!canPlay) {
  710 |     return {
  711 |       titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.38)",
  712 |       subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.32)",
  713 |     };
  714 |   }
  715 | 
  716 |   if (isCurrent) {
  717 |     return {
  718 |       titleColor: "var(--accent)",
  719 |       subColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 70%, rgba(255,255,255,0.70))",
  720 |     };
  721 |   }
  722 | 
  723 |   return {
  724 |     titleColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
  725 |     subColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.70)",
  726 |   };
  727 | }
  728 | 
  729 | function trackRowRadius(index: number, trackCount: number): string {
  730 |   if (index === 0) return "14px 14px 0 0";
  731 |   if (index === trackCount - 1) return "0 0 14px 14px";
  732 |   return "0";
  733 | }

  748 | function deriveTrackRowView(
  749 |   params: Readonly<{
  750 |     track: PlayerTrack;
  751 |     index: number;
  752 |     trackCount: number;
  753 |     currentRecordingId: string | undefined;

  761 |   }>,
  762 | ): TrackRowView {
  763 |   const isCurrent = params.currentRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
  764 |   const isPending = params.pendingRecordingId === params.track.recordingId;
  765 |   const shimmerTitle =
  766 |     params.playerStatus === "loading" && isPending && !isCurrent;
  767 |   const isNowPlaying = Boolean(
  768 |     isCurrent &&
  769 |     (params.playerStatus === "playing" ||
  770 |       params.playerStatus === "loading" ||
  771 |       params.playerIntent === "play"),
  772 |   );
  773 |   const { titleColor, subColor } = trackRowColors(params.canPlay, isCurrent);
  774 |   const baseBackground = params.isSelected
  775 |     ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)"
  776 |     : "transparent";
  777 |   const restingBackground =
  778 |     isCurrent && !params.isSelected ? "transparent" : baseBackground;
  779 |   const dataReason =
  780 |     isCurrent && params.playerStatus === "loading"
  781 |       ? (params.loadingReason ?? "")
  782 |       : "";
  783 | 
  784 |   return {
  785 |     isCurrent,
  786 |     shimmerTitle,
  787 |     isNowPlaying,
  788 |     titleColor,
  789 |     subColor,
  790 |     restingBackground,
  791 |     rowRadius: trackRowRadius(params.index, params.trackCount),
  792 |     dataReason,
  793 |     cursor: params.canAttemptPlay ? "pointer" : "default",
  794 |     opacity: params.canPlay ? 1 : 0.75,
  795 |   };
  796 | }
  797 | 
  798 | function trackPlayLabel(track: PlayerTrack, index: number): string {
  799 |   const title = track.title?.trim();
  800 |   const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;
  801 |   return `Play ${subject}`;
  802 | }

  805 |   track: PlayerTrack,
  806 |   index: number,
  807 |   isCoarsePointer: boolean,
  808 | ): string {
  809 |   const title = track.title?.trim();
  810 |   const subject = title || `track ${index + 1}`;
  811 |   const verb = isCoarsePointer ? "Play" : "Select";
  812 |   return `${verb} ${subject}`;
  813 | }

 1119 |           title="Play"

 1152 | 
 1153 | function parsePublicAlbumPath(pathname: string | null): {
 1154 |   albumSlug: string | null;
 1155 |   displayId: string | null;
 1156 | } {
 1157 |   const p = (pathname ?? "").trim();
 1158 | 
 1159 |   // canonical music surfaces:
 1160 |   // /:slug
 1161 |   // /:slug/:displayId
 1162 |   //
 1163 |   // exclude obvious non-music reserved roots
 1164 |   const reserved = new Set([
 1165 |     "",
 1166 |     "player",
 1167 |     "journal",
 1168 |     "portal",
 1169 |     "posts",
 1170 |     "extras",
 1171 |     "download",
 1172 |     "gift",
 1173 |     "unsubscribe",
 1174 |     "studio",
 1175 |     "admin",
 1176 |     "api",
 1177 |   ]);
 1178 | 
 1179 |   const parts = p
 1180 |     .split("?")[0]
 1181 |     .split("#")[0]
 1182 |     .split("/")
 1183 |     .filter(Boolean)
 1184 |     .map((x) => decodeURIComponent(x).trim())
 1185 |     .filter(Boolean);
 1186 | 
 1187 |   if (parts.length === 0) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1188 |   if (parts.length > 2) return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1189 | 
 1190 |   const albumSlug = parts[0] ?? null;
 1191 |   if (!albumSlug || reserved.has(albumSlug)) {
 1192 |     return { albumSlug: null, displayId: null };
 1193 |   }
 1194 | 
 1195 |   const displayId = parts[1] ?? null;
 1196 |   return { albumSlug, displayId };
 1197 | }
 1198 | 
 1199 | function canonicalCarryQuery(): string {
 1200 |   // Only carry the params that are allowed to exist on canonical public routes.
 1201 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
 1202 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
 1203 |   const out = new URLSearchParams();
 1204 | 
 1205 |   const st = (sp.get("st") ?? "").trim();
 1206 |   const share = (sp.get("share") ?? "").trim();
 1207 |   const autoplay = (sp.get("autoplay") ?? "").trim();
 1208 | 
 1209 |   if (st) out.set("st", st);
 1210 |   else if (share) out.set("share", share);
 1211 | 
 1212 |   if (autoplay) out.set("autoplay", autoplay);
 1213 | 
 1214 |   // keep utm_* if present
 1215 |   for (const [k, v] of sp.entries()) {
 1216 |     if (k.startsWith("utm_") && v.trim()) out.set(k, v.trim());
 1217 |   }
 1218 | 
 1219 |   const q = out.toString();
 1220 |   return q ? `?${q}` : "";
 1221 | }

 1223 | export default function FullPlayer(props: FullPlayerProps) {
 1224 |   const {
 1225 |     bundle,
 1226 |     albums,
 1227 |     onSelectAlbum,
 1228 |     isBrowsingAlbum = false,
 1229 |     tier = "none",
 1230 |   } = props;
 1231 | 
 1232 |   const { album, tracks, albumLyrics } = bundle;
 1233 |   const p = usePlayer();
 1234 |   const { reportGate } = useGateBroker();
 1235 |   const {
 1236 |     isLoaded: clerkAuthLoaded,
 1237 |     isSignedIn,
 1238 |     userId,
 1239 |     sessionId,
 1240 |   } = useAuth();
 1241 | 
 1242 |   const accessIdentityKey = resolveAccessIdentityKey({
 1243 |     clerkAuthLoaded,
 1244 |     isSignedIn,
 1245 |     userId,
 1246 |     sessionId,
 1247 |   });
 1248 | 
 1249 |   // near your other hooks
 1250 |   const searchParams = useSearchParams();
 1251 |   const stParam =
 1252 |     (searchParams?.get("st") ?? searchParams?.get("share") ?? "").trim() ||
 1253 |     null;
 1254 | 
 1255 |   const pRef = React.useRef(p);
 1256 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1257 |     pRef.current = p;
 1258 |   }, [p]);
 1259 | 
 1260 |   // ---- optimistic UI cache (state, not ref) to satisfy react-hooks/refs lint ----
 1261 |   const [stableView, setStableView] = React.useState<StableView | null>(null);
 1262 | 
 1263 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1264 |     if (album && tracks?.length) {
 1265 |       setStableView(bundle);
 1266 |     }
 1267 |     primeLyricsFromAlbumBundle(albumLyrics ?? null);
 1268 |   }, [bundle, album, tracks, albumLyrics]);
 1269 | 
 1270 |   const {
 1271 |     albumSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1272 |     album: effAlbum,
 1273 |     tracks: effTracks,
 1274 |   } = resolveEffectivePlayerView({
 1275 |     bundle,
 1276 |     stableView,
 1277 |     isBrowsingAlbum,
 1278 |   });
 1279 |   // Track membership ref (lets access-check logic test membership without re-triggering fetch)
 1280 |   const effRecordingIdSetRef = React.useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
 1281 | 
 1282 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1283 |     effRecordingIdSetRef.current = new Set(effTracks.map((t) => t.recordingId));
 1284 |   }, [effTracks]);
 1285 | 
 1286 |   const router = useRouter();
 1287 |   const pathname = usePathname();
 1288 | 
 1289 |   const route = React.useMemo(() => parsePublicAlbumPath(pathname), [pathname]);
 1290 |   const isPublicAlbumRoute = Boolean(route.albumSlug);
 1291 | 
 1292 |   const goCanonicalTrack = React.useCallback(
 1293 |     (displayId: string | null, mode: "push" | "replace" = "push") => {
 1294 |       if (!isPublicAlbumRoute) return;
 1295 | 
 1296 |       const qs = canonicalCarryQuery();
 1297 |       const base = `/${encodeURIComponent(effAlbumSlug)}`;
 1298 |       const href = displayId
 1299 |         ? `${base}/${encodeURIComponent(displayId)}${qs}`
 1300 |         : `${base}${qs}`;
 1301 | 
 1302 |       if (mode === "replace") router.replace(href, { scroll: false });
 1303 |       else router.push(href, { scroll: false });
 1304 |     },
 1305 |     [isPublicAlbumRoute, router, effAlbumSlug],
 1306 |   );
 1307 | 
 1308 |   const [pendingAlbumSlug, setPendingAlbumSlug] = React.useState<string | null>(
 1309 |     null,
 1310 |   );
 1311 |   const fullPlayerTopRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
 1312 |   const mobileScrollTargetAlbumSlugRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
 1313 | 
 1314 |   const scrollMobilePlayerToTop = React.useCallback(() => {
 1315 |     if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
 1316 |     if (!window.matchMedia("(max-width: 640px)").matches) return;
 1317 | 
 1318 |     const target = fullPlayerTopRef.current;
 1319 |     if (!target) return;
 1320 | 
 1321 |     let ancestor: HTMLElement | null = target.parentElement;
 1322 | 
 1323 |     while (ancestor) {
 1324 |       const overflowY = window.getComputedStyle(ancestor).overflowY;
 1325 |       const canScroll =
 1326 |         (overflowY === "auto" ||
 1327 |           overflowY === "scroll" ||
 1328 |           overflowY === "overlay") &&
 1329 |         ancestor.scrollHeight > ancestor.clientHeight;
 1330 | 
 1331 |       if (canScroll) {
 1332 |         const ancestorTop = ancestor.getBoundingClientRect().top;
 1333 |         const targetTop = target.getBoundingClientRect().top;
 1334 |         const nextTop = Math.max(
 1335 |           0,
 1336 |           ancestor.scrollTop + targetTop - ancestorTop,
 1337 |         );
 1338 | 
 1339 |         ancestor.scrollTo({
 1340 |           top: nextTop,
 1341 |           behavior: "auto",
 1342 |         });
 1343 | 
 1344 |         return;
 1345 |       }
 1346 | 
 1347 |       ancestor = ancestor.parentElement;
 1348 |     }
 1349 | 
 1350 |     const targetTop = window.scrollY + target.getBoundingClientRect().top;
 1351 | 
 1352 |     window.scrollTo({
 1353 |       top: Math.max(0, targetTop),
 1354 |       behavior: "auto",
 1355 |     });
 1356 |   }, []);
 1357 | 
 1358 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
 1359 |     const targetAlbumSlug = mobileScrollTargetAlbumSlugRef.current;
 1360 | 
 1361 |     const hasResolvedSelectedAlbum =
 1362 |       targetAlbumSlug === effAlbumSlug &&
 1363 |       Boolean(effAlbum) &&
 1364 |       effTracks.length > 0;
 1365 | 
 1366 |     if (!hasResolvedSelectedAlbum) return;
 1367 | 
 1368 |     // This fires after React has committed the incoming album's DOM, but before
 1369 |     // the browser paints it. The user therefore sees the new album and the
 1370 |     // restored top position as a single visual change.
 1371 |     scrollMobilePlayerToTop();
 1372 | 
 1373 |     mobileScrollTargetAlbumSlugRef.current = null;
 1374 |   }, [effAlbum, effAlbumSlug, effTracks.length, scrollMobilePlayerToTop]);
 1375 | 
 1376 |   // Clear when the pending selection has actually become the effective album view.
 1377 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1378 |     if (!pendingAlbumSlug) return;
 1379 | 
 1380 |     const resolved =
 1381 |       effAlbumSlug === pendingAlbumSlug &&
 1382 |       Boolean(effAlbum) &&
 1383 |       effTracks.length > 0;
 1384 | 
 1385 |     if (resolved) setPendingAlbumSlug(null);
 1386 |   }, [pendingAlbumSlug, effAlbumSlug, effAlbum, effTracks.length]);
 1387 | 
 1388 |   // Safety: auto-clear after 8s so it can't stick forever if something goes sideways.
 1389 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1390 |     if (!pendingAlbumSlug) return;
 1391 |     const t = window.setTimeout(() => setPendingAlbumSlug(null), 8000);
 1392 |     return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
 1393 |   }, [pendingAlbumSlug]);
 1394 | 
 1395 |   const albumTitle = effAlbum?.title ?? "—";
 1396 |   const albumDesc =
 1397 |     effAlbum?.description ??
 1398 |     "This is placeholder copy. Soon: pull album description from Sanity.";
 1399 | 
 1400 |   // ---- browser tab title (client-side) ----
 1401 |   // We set this here because in portal surfaces we may not actually navigate routes
 1402 |   // (and therefore server metadata won't rerun).
 1403 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1404 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
 1405 | 
 1406 |     const prev = document.title;
 1407 | 
 1408 |     const cur = p.current;
 1409 |     const curInThisAlbum = Boolean(
 1410 |       cur?.recordingId &&
 1411 |       effTracks.some((t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId),
 1412 |     );

 1422 |       ? [trackTitle, aTitle, artist].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")
 1423 |       : [aTitle, artist].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");

 1426 |

 1428 |       // Don't leave a stale player title behind if the component unmounts
 1429 |       document.title = prev;
 1430 |     };
 1431 |     // Intentionally depend on the minimal fields that affect the title.
 1432 |   }, [
 1433 |     p.current?.recordingId,

 1450 |   const [activeListeners, setActiveListeners] = React.useState<number>(0);

 1452 |   React.useEffect(() => {

 1454 |       setActiveListeners(0);

 1458 |     let cancelled = false;
 1459 | 
 1460 |     const isVisible = () => document.visibilityState === "visible";
 1461 | 
 1462 |     async function refreshActiveListeners(): Promise<void> {
 1463 |       if (!isVisible()) return;
 1464 | 
 1465 |       try {
 1466 |         const res = await fetch("/api/playback/live?windowSeconds=30", {
 1467 |           method: "GET",
 1468 |         });
 1469 | 
 1470 |         if (!res.ok) return;
 1471 | 
 1472 |         const body = (await res.json()) as {
 1473 |           ok?: boolean;
 1474 |           activeListeners?: number;
 1475 |         };
 1476 | 
 1477 |         if (cancelled) return;
 1478 | 
 1479 |         const next =
 1480 |           typeof body.activeListeners === "number" &&
 1481 |           Number.isFinite(body.activeListeners) &&
 1482 |           body.activeListeners >= 0
 1483 |             ? Math.floor(body.activeListeners)
 1484 |             : 0;
 1485 | 
 1486 |         setActiveListeners(next);
 1487 |       } catch {
 1488 |         // A live-count failure must never affect playback.
 1489 |       }
 1490 |     }
 1491 | 
 1492 |     const onVisibilityChange = () => {
 1493 |       if (isVisible()) {
 1494 |         void refreshActiveListeners();
 1495 |       }
 1496 |     };
 1497 | 
 1498 |     void refreshActiveListeners();
 1499 | 
 1500 |     const id = window.setInterval(() => {
 1501 |       void refreshActiveListeners();
 1502 |     }, 30_000);
 1503 | 
 1504 |     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibilityChange);
 1505 | 
 1506 |     return () => {
 1507 |       cancelled = true;
 1508 |       window.clearInterval(id);
 1509 |       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibilityChange);
 1510 |     };

 1524 |   React.useEffect(() => {

 1529 |       catalogueId,
 1530 |       shareToken: stParam,
 1531 |       accessIdentityKey,
 1532 |     };
 1533 |

 1636 |   function openPlaybackGate() {
 1637 |     reportGate({
 1638 |       code: isPlaybackGateCode(accessForAlbum?.code)
 1639 |         ? accessForAlbum.code
 1640 |         : "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
 1641 |       action: toGateAction(accessForAlbum?.action),

 1649 |   const playTrackWithIntent = (
 1650 |     track: PlayerTrack,
 1651 |     options: Readonly<{
 1652 |       ensureQueue: boolean;
 1653 |       navigationMode: "push" | "replace";
 1654 |     }>,
 1655 |   ) => {
 1656 |     prefetchAlbumSession(track);
 1657 |     if (options.ensureQueue) ensureAlbumQueue();
 1658 |     goCanonicalTrack(track.displayId, options.navigationMode);
 1659 |     p.play(track);
 1660 |     window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
 1661 |   };

 1663 |   const onTogglePlay = () => {
 1664 |     lockPlayFor(120);
 1665 | 
 1666 |     if (isPlaybackGate) {
 1667 |       openPlaybackGate();
 1668 |       return;
 1669 |     }
 1670 |     if (!canPlay) return;
 1671 | 
 1672 |     const currentRecordingId = p.current?.recordingId ?? "";
 1673 |     const currentTrackInThisAlbum = isThisAlbumActive
 1674 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.recordingId === currentRecordingId) ??
 1675 |         null)
 1676 |       : null;
 1677 |     const routeTargetsThisAlbum =
 1678 |       isPublicAlbumRoute && route.albumSlug === effAlbumSlug;
 1679 |     const directRouteTrack = routeTargetsThisAlbum
 1680 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.displayId === route.displayId) ?? null)
 1681 |       : null;
 1682 |     const directRouteTargetsAnotherTrack = Boolean(
 1683 |       directRouteTrack &&
 1684 |       directRouteTrack.recordingId !== currentTrackInThisAlbum?.recordingId,
 1685 |     );
 1686 | 
 1687 |     // A generic album page controls its existing same-album transport natively.
 1688 |     // A direct track URL is more specific: it deliberately selects that track.
 1689 |     if (playingThisAlbum && !directRouteTargetsAnotherTrack) {
 1690 |       window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:pause-intent"));
 1691 |       p.pause();
 1692 |       return;
 1693 |     }
 1694 | 
 1695 |     if (directRouteTrack && directRouteTargetsAnotherTrack) {
 1696 |       playTrackWithIntent(directRouteTrack, {
 1697 |         ensureQueue: true,
 1698 |         navigationMode: "replace",
 1699 |       });
 1700 |       return;
 1701 |     }
 1702 | 
 1703 |     // Same active album, no differently targeted direct-track URL:
 1704 |     // retain position and resume rather than resetting to track one.
 1705 |     if (currentTrackInThisAlbum) {
 1706 |       playTrackWithIntent(currentTrackInThisAlbum, {
 1707 |         ensureQueue: false,
 1708 |         navigationMode: "replace",
 1709 |       });
 1710 |       return;
 1711 |     }
 1712 | 
 1713 |     // A different album, or an album with no recoverable current track:
 1714 |     // intentionally establish its queue and begin at its first track.
 1715 |     const firstTrack = effTracks[0];
 1716 |     if (!firstTrack) return;
 1717 | 
 1718 |     playTrackWithIntent(firstTrack, {
 1719 |       ensureQueue: true,
 1720 |       navigationMode: "replace",
 1721 |     });
 1722 |   };

 1754 |   function ensureAlbumQueue() {
 1755 |     // Explicit nulls prevent an earlier private-link attribution context from
 1756 |     // surviving when ordinary album playback replaces the queue.
 1757 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1758 |       contextId: albumKey ?? undefined,
 1759 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1760 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1761 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1762 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1763 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1764 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1765 |     });
 1766 |   }
 1767 | 
 1768 |   const preparedInitialQueueRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
 1769 | 
 1770 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1771 |     // Wait for the access check so a private-link queue receives its resolved
 1772 |     // attribution context before any listener can press play.
 1773 |     if (!accessKnown) return;
 1774 | 
 1775 |     if (!isPublicAlbumRoute || route.albumSlug !== effAlbumSlug || !albumKey) {
 1776 |       return;
 1777 |     }
 1778 | 
 1779 |     const directTrack = route.displayId
 1780 |       ? (effTracks.find((track) => track.displayId === route.displayId) ?? null)
 1781 |       : null;
 1782 | 
 1783 |     const initialTrack = directTrack ?? effTracks[0] ?? null;
 1784 | 
 1785 |     if (!initialTrack) return;
 1786 | 
 1787 |     // Prepare a fresh player around the route's target track—or track one on a
 1788 |     // generic album route—without beginning playback. Never overwrite an
 1789 |     // existing session, including a paused or playing different album.
 1790 |     const hasExistingPlayerSession = Boolean(
 1791 |       p.current || p.queue.length > 0 || p.pendingRecordingId,
 1792 |     );
 1793 | 
 1794 |     if (hasExistingPlayerSession) return;
 1795 | 
 1796 |     const queueKey = `${albumKey}:${initialTrack.recordingId}`;
 1797 | 
 1798 |     if (preparedInitialQueueRef.current === queueKey) return;
 1799 | 
 1800 |     preparedInitialQueueRef.current = queueKey;
 1801 | 
 1802 |     p.setQueue(effTracks, {
 1803 |       contextId: albumKey,
 1804 |       artworkUrl: effAlbum?.artworkUrl ?? null,
 1805 |       contextSlug: effAlbumSlug,
 1806 |       contextTitle: effAlbum?.title ?? undefined,
 1807 |       contextArtist: effAlbum?.artist ?? undefined,
 1808 |       sharePlaybackContext: queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1809 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1810 |       initialCurrentRecordingId: initialTrack.recordingId,
 1811 |     });
 1812 |   }, [
 1813 |     accessKnown,
 1814 |     albumKey,
 1815 |     effAlbum?.artworkUrl,
 1816 |     effAlbum?.artist,
 1817 |     effAlbum?.title,
 1818 |     effAlbumSlug,
 1819 |     effTracks,
 1820 |     isPublicAlbumRoute,
 1821 |     p,
 1822 |     queueSharePlaybackContext,
 1823 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId,
 1824 |     route.albumSlug,
 1825 |     route.displayId,

 1828 |   function playAlbumIndex(i: number) {

 1837 |     }

 1839 |     p.play(t);
 1840 |     window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
 1841 |   }

   17 | function fmtTimeSec(sec: number) {
   18 |   const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec));
   19 |   const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
   20 |   const r = s % 60;
   21 |   return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   22 | }

  224 |     } else {
  225 |       // Fallback: go to featured album directly (no more /player alias hop)

  238 |     if (!mounted) return;
  239 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  240 | 
  241 |     const el = document.getElementById("af-mini-player");
  242 |     if (!el) return;
  243 | 
  244 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  245 |     let ro: ResizeObserver | null = null;
  246 | 
  247 |     const applyNow = () => {
  248 |       // don’t force layout reads while backgrounded
  249 |       if (document.hidden) return;

  251 |       document.documentElement.style.setProperty(

  262 |       });
  263 |     };
  264 | 
  265 |     // prime once
