  267 | 
  268 |     ro = new ResizeObserver(() => schedule());
  269 |     ro.observe(el);
  270 | 
  271 |     const onResize = () => schedule();
  272 |     window.addEventListener("resize", onResize, { passive: true });
  273 | 
  274 |     const onVis = () => {
  275 |       if (!document.hidden) schedule();
  276 |     };
  277 |     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis, { passive: true });
  278 | 
  279 |     return () => {
  280 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  281 |       raf = null;
  282 |       ro?.disconnect();
  283 |       ro = null;
  284 |       window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
  285 |       document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
  286 |     };
  287 |   }, [mounted]);
  288 | 
  289 |   const playingish =

  298 |

  300 |

   51 |   queueSharePlaybackScopeId?: string | null;

   53 |   intent: Intent;
   54 |   intentAtMs?: number;
   55 |   selectedRecordingId?: string;
   56 |   pendingRecordingId?: string;
   57 | 
   58 |   pendingSeekMs?: number;
   59 |   seeking: boolean;
   60 | 
   61 |   loadingReason?: LoadingReason;
   62 |   reloadNonce: number;
   63 | 
   64 |   positionMs: number;
   65 |   seekNonce: number;
   66 | 
   67 |   volume: number;
   68 |   muted: boolean;
   69 |   repeat: RepeatMode;
   70 | 
   71 |   // Catalogue duration supplied by Sanity. This remains stable metadata and a
   72 |   // pre-load fallback, but is not assumed to be the decoded asset's exact end.
   73 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>;
   74 | 
   75 |   // Runtime duration reported by the browser for the actual delivered Mux
   76 |   // rendition. Keyed by playback ID so a future asset replacement cannot reuse
   77 |   // a stale duration merely because it shares a recording ID.
   78 |   assetDurationByPlaybackId: Record<string, number>;
   79 | };
   80 |

   82 |   play: (track?: PlayerTrack) => void;

   89 |   enqueue: (track: PlayerTrack) => void;
   90 |   clearQueue: () => void;
   91 | 
   92 |   setPositionMs: (ms: number) => void;
   93 |   setDurationMs: (ms: number) => void;
   94 |   setAssetDurationMs: (playbackId: string, ms: number) => void;
   95 | 
   96 |   setStatusExternal: (s: PlayerStatus) => void;
   97 |   setLoadingReasonExternal: (r?: LoadingReason) => void;
   98 |

  100 |   clearIntent: () => void;
  101 |   selectTrack: (id?: string) => void;
  102 |   setPendingRecordingId: (id?: string) => void;
  103 |   resolvePendingTrack: (id: string) => void;

  107 | 
  108 |   setVolume: (v: number) => void;
  109 |   toggleMute: () => void;
  110 |

  112 |   tick: (deltaMs: number) => void;
  113 |

  115 |   bumpReload: () => void;

  118 | type PlayerVisualState = {
  119 |   status: PlayerStatus;
  120 |   intent: Intent;
  121 |   playRequestPending: boolean;
  122 |   current?: PlayerTrack;
  123 |   firstQueuedTrack?: PlayerTrack;

  126 | const PlayerCtx = React.createContext<(PlayerState & PlayerActions) | null>(
  127 |   null,
  128 | );
  129 | const PlayerActionsCtx = React.createContext<PlayerActions | null>(null);
  130 | const PlayerVisualCtx = React.createContext<PlayerVisualState | null>(null);
  131 | 
  132 | function clamp(n: number, lo: number, hi: number) {
  133 |   return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n));
  134 | }
  135 | 
  136 | function nextRepeat(r: RepeatMode): RepeatMode {
  137 |   if (r === "off") return "all";
  138 |   if (r === "all") return "one";
  139 |   return "off";
  140 | }
  141 | 
  142 | function positiveDurationMs(value: number | undefined): number {
  143 |   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0
  144 |     ? Math.round(value)
  145 |     : 0;
  146 | }
  147 | 
  148 | function getAssetDurationMs(
  149 |   assetDurationByPlaybackId: Record<string, number>,
  150 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  151 | ): number {
  152 |   const playbackId = (track?.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  153 | 
  154 |   return playbackId
  155 |     ? positiveDurationMs(assetDurationByPlaybackId[playbackId])
  156 |     : 0;
  157 | }
  158 | 
  159 | function getEffectiveDurationMs(
  160 |   state: Pick<
  161 |     PlayerState,
  162 |     "durationByRecordingId" | "assetDurationByPlaybackId"
  163 |   >,
  164 |   track: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  165 | ): number {
  166 |   const assetDurationMs = getAssetDurationMs(
  167 |     state.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  168 |     track,
  169 |   );
  170 | 
  171 |   if (assetDurationMs > 0) return assetDurationMs;
  172 | 
  173 |   const recordingId = (track?.recordingId ?? "").trim();
  174 |   const catalogueDurationMs = recordingId
  175 |     ? positiveDurationMs(state.durationByRecordingId[recordingId])
  176 |     : 0;
  177 | 
  178 |   return catalogueDurationMs || positiveDurationMs(track?.durationMs);
  179 | }
  180 | 
  181 | function retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  182 |   previous: Record<string, number>,
  183 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  184 | ): Record<string, number> {
  185 |   const activePlaybackIds = new Set<string>();
  186 | 
  187 |   for (const track of tracks) {
  188 |     const playbackId = (track.muxPlaybackId ?? "").trim();
  189 |     if (playbackId) activePlaybackIds.add(playbackId);
  190 |   }
  191 | 
  192 |   let next = previous;
  193 | 
  194 |   for (const playbackId of Object.keys(previous)) {
  195 |     if (activePlaybackIds.has(playbackId)) continue;
  196 | 
  197 |     if (next === previous) next = { ...previous };
  198 |     delete next[playbackId];
  199 |   }
  200 | 
  201 |   return next;
  202 | }
  203 | 
  204 | function hydrateTrack(
  205 |   t: PlayerTrack,
  206 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  207 | ): PlayerTrack {
  208 |   const cached = durationByRecordingId[t.recordingId];
  209 |   if (!cached || cached <= 0) return t;
  210 |   if (t.durationMs === cached) return t;
  211 |   return { ...t, durationMs: cached };
  212 | }
  213 | 
  214 | function hydrateTracks(
  215 |   ts: PlayerTrack[],
  216 |   durationByRecordingId: Record<string, number>,
  217 | ) {
  218 |   let changed = false;
  219 |   const next = ts.map((t) => {
  220 |     const ht = hydrateTrack(t, durationByRecordingId);
  221 |     if (ht !== t) changed = true;
  222 |     return ht;
  223 |   });
  224 |   return changed ? next : ts;
  225 | }
  226 | 
  227 | function blockedPlaybackMessage(access: PlaybackAccessDecision): string {
  228 |   if (access.embargoed) {
  229 |     return "Playback is disabled pre-release.";
  230 |   }
  231 | 
  232 |   if (access.reason) return access.reason;
  233 | 
  234 |   return "Playback is not available for this release.";
  235 | }
  236 | 
  237 | type TargetedPlayIntentDetail = {
  238 |   track: PlayerTrack;
  239 |   albumId: string | null;
  240 | };
  241 | 
  242 | function dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  243 |   track: PlayerTrack,
  244 |   state: Pick<PlayerState, "queueContextId">,
  245 | ): void {
  246 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  247 | 
  248 |   const albumId = (state.queueContextId ?? "").trim() || null;
  249 | 
  250 |   window.dispatchEvent(
  251 |     new CustomEvent<TargetedPlayIntentDetail>("af:play-target-intent", {
  252 |       detail: {
  253 |         track,
  254 |         albumId,
  255 |       },
  256 |     }),
  257 |   );
  258 | }
  259 | 
  260 | function getNextTransportTrack(state: PlayerState): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  261 |   const current = state.current;
  262 | 
  263 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  264 |   if (state.repeat === "one") return current;
  265 | 
  266 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  267 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  268 |   );
  269 |   const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
  270 | 
  271 |   if (currentIndex + 1 < state.queue.length) {
  272 |     return state.queue[currentIndex + 1];
  273 |   }
  274 | 
  275 |   return state.repeat === "all" ? state.queue[0] : undefined;
  276 | }
  277 | 
  278 | function getPreviousTransportTrack(
  279 |   state: PlayerState,
  280 | ): PlayerTrack | undefined {
  281 |   const current = state.current;
  282 | 
  283 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return undefined;
  284 | 
  285 |   if (state.status === "playing" && state.positionMs > 3000) {
  286 |     return current;
  287 |   }
  288 | 
  289 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  290 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  291 |   );
  292 |   const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
  293 | 
  294 |   if (currentIndex > 0) {
  295 |     return state.queue[currentIndex - 1];
  296 |   }
  297 | 
  298 |   if (state.repeat === "all") {
  299 |     return state.queue.at(-1);
  300 |   }
  301 | 
  302 |   return current;
  303 | }
  304 | 
  305 | function primeDurationByRecordingId(
  306 |   prev: Record<string, number>,
  307 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  308 | ): Record<string, number> {
  309 |   let next = prev;
  310 |   for (const t of tracks) {
  311 |     if (!t?.recordingId) continue;
  312 |     const ms = t.durationMs;
  313 |     if (typeof ms !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) continue;
  314 |     if (typeof next[t.recordingId] === "number" && next[t.recordingId] > 0)
  315 |       continue;
  316 |     if (next === prev) next = { ...prev };
  317 |     next[t.recordingId] = ms;
  318 |   }
  319 |   return next;
  320 | }
  321 | 
  322 | function resolvePlaybackAccessIdentityKey(
  323 |   params: Readonly<{
  324 |     clerkAuthLoaded: boolean;
  325 |     isSignedIn: boolean | undefined;
  326 |     userId: string | null | undefined;
  327 |     sessionId: string | null | undefined;
  328 |   }>,
  329 | ): string {
  330 |   if (!params.clerkAuthLoaded) return "clerk:loading";
  331 |   if (!params.isSignedIn) return "clerk:anonymous";
  332 | 
  333 |   return `clerk:user:${params.userId ?? ""}:session:${params.sessionId ?? ""}`;
  334 | }
  335 | 
  336 | function markCurrentTrackLoading(
  337 |   state: PlayerState,
  338 |   loadingReason: "attach",
  339 | ): PlayerState {
  340 |   const current = state.current;
  341 |   if (!current) return state;
  342 | 
  343 |   return {
  344 |     ...state,
  345 |     status: "loading",
  346 |     loadingReason,
  347 |     positionMs: 0,
  348 |     intent: "play",
  349 |     intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  350 |     pendingRecordingId: current.recordingId,
  351 |     selectedRecordingId: current.recordingId,
  352 |   };
  353 | }
  354 | 
  355 | function markNextTrackLoading(
  356 |   state: PlayerState,
  357 |   track: PlayerTrack,
  358 |   loadingReason: "token" | "attach",
  359 | ): PlayerState {
  360 |   const hydratedTrack = hydrateTrack(track, state.durationByRecordingId);
  361 | 
  362 |   return {
  363 |     ...state,
  364 |     current: hydratedTrack,
  365 |     status: "loading",
  366 |     loadingReason,
  367 |     positionMs: 0,
  368 |     intent: "play",
  369 |     intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  370 |     pendingRecordingId: hydratedTrack.recordingId,
  371 |     selectedRecordingId: hydratedTrack.recordingId,
  372 |   };
  373 | }
  374 | 
  375 | function transitionForwardState(
  376 |   state: PlayerState,
  377 |   nextTrackLoadingReason: "token" | "attach",
  378 |   terminalIntent: "pause" | null,
  379 | ): PlayerState {
  380 |   const current = state.current;
  381 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return state;
  382 | 
  383 |   if (state.repeat === "one") {
  384 |     return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
  385 |   }
  386 | 
  387 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  388 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  389 |   );
  390 |   const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
  391 |   const nextTrack = state.queue[currentIndex + 1];
  392 | 
  393 |   if (nextTrack) {
  394 |     return markNextTrackLoading(state, nextTrack, nextTrackLoadingReason);
  395 |   }
  396 | 
  397 |   if (state.repeat === "all") {
  398 |     const firstTrack = state.queue[0];
  399 |     if (firstTrack) {
  400 |       return markNextTrackLoading(state, firstTrack, nextTrackLoadingReason);
  401 |     }
  402 |   }
  403 | 
  404 |   return {
  405 |     ...state,
  406 |     status: "paused",
  407 |     intent: terminalIntent,
  408 |     intentAtMs: terminalIntent === "pause" ? Date.now() : undefined,
  409 |     positionMs: 0,
  410 |     pendingSeekMs: 0,
  411 |     seeking: true,
  412 |     seekNonce: state.seekNonce + 1,
  413 |     loadingReason: undefined,
  414 |     pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  415 |   };
  416 | }
  417 | 
  418 | function transitionPreviousState(state: PlayerState): PlayerState {
  419 |   const current = state.current;
  420 |   if (!current || state.queue.length === 0) return state;
  421 | 
  422 |   if (state.status === "playing" && state.positionMs > 3000) {
  423 |     return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
  424 |   }
  425 | 
  426 |   const index = state.queue.findIndex(
  427 |     (track) => track.recordingId === current.recordingId,
  428 |   );
  429 |   const currentIndex = Math.max(index, 0);
  430 |   const previousTrack = state.queue[currentIndex - 1];
  431 | 
  432 |   if (previousTrack) {
  433 |     return markNextTrackLoading(state, previousTrack, "token");
  434 |   }
  435 | 
  436 |   if (state.repeat === "all") {
  437 |     const lastTrack = state.queue.at(-1);
  438 |     if (lastTrack) {
  439 |       return markNextTrackLoading(state, lastTrack, "token");
  440 |     }
  441 |   }
  442 | 
  443 |   return markCurrentTrackLoading(state, "attach");
  444 | }
  445 | 
  446 | type QueueContextStatePatch = Partial<
  447 |   Pick<
  448 |     PlayerState,
  449 |     | "queueContextId"
  450 |     | "queueContextSlug"
  451 |     | "queueContextTitle"
  452 |     | "queueContextArtist"
  453 |     | "queueContextArtworkUrl"
  454 |     | "queueSharePlaybackContext"
  455 |     | "queueSharePlaybackScopeId"
  456 |   >
  457 | >;
  458 | 
  459 | function normalizedQueueShareValue(
  460 |   value: string | null | undefined,
  461 | ): string | null {
  462 |   if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  463 |   return value.trim() || null;
  464 | }
  465 | 
  466 | function buildQueueContextStatePatch(
  467 |   state: PlayerState,
  468 |   opts: QueueContext | undefined,
  469 | ): QueueContextStatePatch {
  470 |   const patch: QueueContextStatePatch = {};
  471 |   const contextId = typeof opts?.contextId === "string" ? opts.contextId : "";
  472 |   const contextSlug =
  473 |     typeof opts?.contextSlug === "string" ? opts.contextSlug.trim() : "";
  474 |   const contextTitle =
  475 |     typeof opts?.contextTitle === "string" ? opts.contextTitle.trim() : "";
  476 |   const contextArtist =
  477 |     typeof opts?.contextArtist === "string" ? opts.contextArtist.trim() : "";
  478 | 
  479 |   if (contextId.length > 0) patch.queueContextId = contextId;
  480 |   if (contextSlug) patch.queueContextSlug = contextSlug;
  481 |   if (contextTitle) patch.queueContextTitle = contextTitle;
  482 |   if (contextArtist) patch.queueContextArtist = contextArtist;
  483 | 
  484 |   patch.queueContextArtworkUrl =
  485 |     opts?.artworkUrl !== undefined
  486 |       ? (opts.artworkUrl ?? null)
  487 |       : (state.queueContextArtworkUrl ?? null);
  488 | 
  489 |   const optionBag = opts ?? {};
  490 | 
  491 |   if (Object.hasOwn(optionBag, "sharePlaybackContext")) {
  492 |     patch.queueSharePlaybackContext = normalizedQueueShareValue(
  493 |       opts?.sharePlaybackContext,
  494 |     );
  495 |   }
  496 | 
  497 |   if (Object.hasOwn(optionBag, "sharePlaybackScopeId")) {
  498 |     patch.queueSharePlaybackScopeId = normalizedQueueShareValue(
  499 |       opts?.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  500 |     );
  501 |   }
  502 | 
  503 |   return patch;
  504 | }
  505 | 
  506 | function findRequestedInitialCurrent(
  507 |   queue: readonly PlayerTrack[],
  508 |   opts: QueueContext | undefined,
  509 | ): PlayerTrack | null {
  510 |   const recordingId =
  511 |     typeof opts?.initialCurrentRecordingId === "string"
  512 |       ? opts.initialCurrentRecordingId.trim()
  513 |       : "";
  514 | 
  515 |   if (!recordingId) return null;
  516 | 
  517 |   return (
  518 |     queue.find((track) => track.recordingId === recordingId) ?? null
  519 |   );
  520 | }
  521 | 
  522 | type QueueSelectionState = Pick<
  523 |   PlayerState,
  524 |   "positionMs" | "selectedRecordingId" | "pendingRecordingId"
  525 | >;
  526 | 
  527 | function resolveQueueSelectionState(
  528 |   state: PlayerState,
  529 |   nextCurrent: PlayerTrack | undefined,
  530 |   requestedInitialCurrent: PlayerTrack | null,
  531 | ): QueueSelectionState {
  532 |   if (requestedInitialCurrent) {
  533 |     return {
  534 |       positionMs: 0,
  535 |       selectedRecordingId: requestedInitialCurrent.recordingId,
  536 |       pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  537 |     };
  538 |   }
  539 | 
  540 |   return {
  541 |     positionMs: state.current ? state.positionMs : 0,
  542 |     selectedRecordingId:
  543 |       state.selectedRecordingId ?? nextCurrent?.recordingId,
  544 |     pendingRecordingId: state.pendingRecordingId,
  545 |   };
  546 | }
  547 | 
  548 | function applyQueueState(
  549 |   state: PlayerState,
  550 |   tracks: PlayerTrack[],
  551 |   opts: QueueContext | undefined,
  552 | ): PlayerState {
  553 |   const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  554 |     state.durationByRecordingId,
  555 |     tracks,
  556 |   );
  557 |   const nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId = retainAssetDurationsByPlaybackId(
  558 |     state.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  559 |     tracks,
  560 |   );
  561 |   const hydratedQueue = hydrateTracks(tracks, nextDurationByRecordingId);
  562 |   const requestedInitialCurrent = findRequestedInitialCurrent(
  563 |     hydratedQueue,
  564 |     opts,
  565 |   );
  566 |   const nextCurrentRaw =
  567 |     requestedInitialCurrent ?? state.current ?? hydratedQueue[0];
  568 |   const nextCurrent = nextCurrentRaw
  569 |     ? hydrateTrack(nextCurrentRaw, nextDurationByRecordingId)
  570 |     : undefined;
  571 | 
  572 |   return {
  573 |     ...state,
  574 |     ...buildQueueContextStatePatch(state, opts),
  575 |     durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  576 |     assetDurationByPlaybackId: nextAssetDurationByPlaybackId,
  577 |     queue: hydratedQueue,
  578 |     current: nextCurrent,
  579 |     ...resolveQueueSelectionState(
  580 |       state,
  581 |       nextCurrent,
  582 |       requestedInitialCurrent,
  583 |     ),
  584 |   };
  585 | }
  586 | 
  587 | export function PlayerStateProvider(
  588 |   props: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>,
  589 | ) {
  590 |   const {
  591 |     isLoaded: clerkAuthLoaded,
  592 |     isSignedIn,
  593 |     userId,
  594 |     sessionId,
  595 |   } = useAuth();
  596 | 
  597 |   const playbackAccessIdentityKey = resolvePlaybackAccessIdentityKey({
  598 |     clerkAuthLoaded,
  599 |     isSignedIn,
  600 |     userId,
  601 |     sessionId,
  602 |   });
  603 | 
  604 |   const [state, setState] = React.useState<PlayerState>({
  605 |     status: "idle",
  606 |     current: undefined,
  607 |     queue: [],
  608 |     lastError: undefined,
  609 |     lastPlayAttemptAtMs: undefined,
  610 |     playRequestPending: false,
  611 | 
  612 |     queueContextId: undefined,
  613 |     queueContextSlug: undefined,
  614 |     queueContextTitle: undefined,
  615 |     queueContextArtist: undefined,
  616 |     queueContextArtworkUrl: null,
  617 |     queueSharePlaybackContext: null,
  618 |     queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  619 | 
  620 |     intent: null,
  621 |     intentAtMs: undefined,
  622 |     selectedRecordingId: undefined,
  623 |     pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  624 | 
  625 |     pendingSeekMs: undefined,
  626 |     seeking: false,
  627 | 
  628 |     loadingReason: undefined,
  629 |     reloadNonce: 0,
  630 | 
  631 |     positionMs: 0,
  632 |     seekNonce: 0,
  633 | 
  634 |     volume: 0.9,
  635 |     muted: false,
  636 |     repeat: "off",
  637 | 
  638 |     durationByRecordingId: {},
  639 |     assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  640 |   });
  641 | 
  642 |   const stateRef = React.useRef(state);
  643 |   const playRequestSequenceRef = React.useRef(0);
  644 | 
  645 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  646 |     stateRef.current = state;
  647 |   }, [state]);
  648 | 
  649 |   const blockPlayback = React.useCallback((message: string) => {
  650 |     setState((s) => ({
  651 |       ...s,
  652 |       status: s.status === "loading" ? "idle" : s.status,
  653 |       intent: "pause",
  654 |       intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  655 |       playRequestPending: false,
  656 |       loadingReason: undefined,
  657 |       pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  658 |       lastError: message,
  659 |     }));
  660 |   }, []);
  661 | 
  662 |   const guardPlayback = React.useCallback(
  663 |     async (onAllowed: () => void): Promise<void> => {
  664 |       const catalogueId = (stateRef.current.queueContextId ?? "").trim();
  665 | 
  666 |       if (!catalogueId) {
  667 |         onAllowed();
  668 |         return;
  669 |       }
  670 | 
  671 |       try {
  672 |         const access = await fetchPlaybackAccessDecision({
  673 |           catalogueId,
  674 |           shareToken: readPlaybackShareTokenFromLocation(),
  675 |           accessIdentityKey: playbackAccessIdentityKey,
  676 |         });
  677 | 
  678 |         if (!access.allowed) {
  679 |           blockPlayback(blockedPlaybackMessage(access));
  680 |           return;
  681 |         }
  682 | 
  683 |         const expectedSharePlaybackScopeId = `alb:${catalogueId.replace(
  684 |           /^alb:/i,
  685 |           "",
  686 |         )}`;
  687 | 
  688 |         if (
  689 |           access.sharePlaybackContext &&
  690 |           access.sharePlaybackScopeId === expectedSharePlaybackScopeId
  691 |         ) {
  692 |           setState((current) => ({
  693 |             ...current,
  694 |             queueSharePlaybackContext: access.sharePlaybackContext,
  695 |             queueSharePlaybackScopeId: access.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  696 |           }));
  697 |         }
  698 | 
  699 |         onAllowed();
  700 |       } catch {
  701 |         blockPlayback("Playback access could not be verified.");
  702 |       }
  703 |     },
  704 |     [blockPlayback, playbackAccessIdentityKey],
  705 |   );
  706 |   const actions: PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
  707 |     return {
  708 |       setIntent: (i: Intent) =>
  709 |         setState((s) => ({
  710 |           ...s,
  711 |           intent: i,
  712 |           intentAtMs: i ? Date.now() : undefined,
  713 |         })),
  714 | 
  715 |       clearIntent: () =>
  716 |         setState((s) =>
  717 |           s.intent ? { ...s, intent: null, intentAtMs: undefined } : s,
  718 |         ),
  719 | 
  720 |       selectTrack: (id?: string) =>
  721 |         setState((s) => {
  722 |           if (s.selectedRecordingId === id) return s;
  723 |           return {
  724 |             ...s,
  725 |             selectedRecordingId: id,
  726 |           };
  727 |         }),
  728 | 
  729 |       setPendingRecordingId: (id?: string) =>
  730 |         setState((s) => {
  731 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId === id) return s;
  732 |           return {
  733 |             ...s,
  734 |             pendingRecordingId: id,
  735 |           };
  736 |         }),
  737 | 
  738 |       resolvePendingTrack: (id: string) =>
  739 |         setState((s) => {
  740 |           if (s.pendingRecordingId !== id) return s;
  741 |           return { ...s, pendingRecordingId: undefined };
  742 |         }),
  743 | 
  744 |       play: (track?: PlayerTrack) => {
  745 |         const requestSequence = ++playRequestSequenceRef.current;
  746 |         const requestStartedAtMs = Date.now();
  747 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  748 |         const requestedTrack = track ?? snapshot.current ?? snapshot.queue[0];
  749 | 
  750 |         if (requestedTrack) {
  751 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  752 |             hydrateTrack(requestedTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  753 |             snapshot,
  754 |           );
  755 |         }
  756 | 
  757 |         setState((s) => ({
  758 |           ...s,
  759 |           playRequestPending: true,
  760 |           lastPlayAttemptAtMs: requestStartedAtMs,
  761 |           lastError: undefined,
  762 |         }));
  763 | 
  764 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  765 |           if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  766 |             return;
  767 |           }
  768 | 
  769 |           setState((s) => {
  770 |             if (requestSequence !== playRequestSequenceRef.current) {
  771 |               return s;
  772 |             }
  773 | 
  774 |             const now = Date.now();
  775 |             const rawNext = track ?? s.current ?? s.queue[0];
  776 | 
  777 |             if (!rawNext) {
  778 |               return {
  779 |                 ...s,
  780 |                 status: "idle",
  781 |                 current: undefined,
  782 |                 positionMs: 0,
  783 |                 intent: "play",
  784 |                 intentAtMs: now,
  785 |                 lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  786 |                 playRequestPending: false,
  787 |                 lastError: undefined,
  788 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  789 |                 pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  790 |               };
  791 |             }
  792 | 
  793 |             const nextTrack = hydrateTrack(rawNext, s.durationByRecordingId);
  794 |             const sameTrack =
  795 |               s.current?.recordingId === nextTrack.recordingId;
  796 | 
  797 |             const base = {
  798 |               ...s,
  799 |               intent: "play" as const,
  800 |               intentAtMs: now,
  801 |               lastPlayAttemptAtMs: now,
  802 |               playRequestPending: false,
  803 |               lastError: undefined,
  804 |               selectedRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  805 |               pendingRecordingId: nextTrack.recordingId,
  806 |             };
  807 | 
  808 |             if (sameTrack && s.status === "paused") {
  809 |               return {
  810 |                 ...base,
  811 |                 current: hydrateTrack(s.current!, s.durationByRecordingId),
  812 |                 status: "paused",
  813 |                 loadingReason: undefined,
  814 |               };
  815 |             }
  816 | 
  817 |             if (
  818 |               sameTrack &&
  819 |               (s.status === "playing" || s.status === "loading")
  820 |             ) {
  821 |               return { ...base, loadingReason: s.loadingReason };
  822 |             }
  823 | 
  824 |             return {
  825 |               ...base,
  826 |               current: nextTrack,
  827 |               status: "loading",
  828 |               loadingReason: "token",
  829 |               positionMs: 0,
  830 |             };
  831 |           });
  832 |         });
  833 |       },
  834 | 
  835 |       pause: () => {
  836 |         playRequestSequenceRef.current += 1;
  837 | 
  838 |         setState((s) => ({
  839 |           ...s,
  840 |           intent: "pause",
  841 |           intentAtMs: Date.now(),
  842 |           playRequestPending: false,
  843 |         }));
  844 |       },
  845 | 
  846 |       next: () => {
  847 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  848 |         const targetTrack = getNextTransportTrack(snapshot);
  849 | 
  850 |         if (targetTrack) {
  851 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  852 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  853 |             snapshot,
  854 |           );
  855 |         }
  856 | 
  857 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  858 |           setState((s) => transitionForwardState(s, "token", "pause"));
  859 |         });
  860 |       },
  861 | 
  862 |       advanceFromEngine: () => {
  863 |         setState((s) => transitionForwardState(s, "attach", null));
  864 |       },
  865 | 
  866 |       prev: () => {
  867 |         const snapshot = stateRef.current;
  868 |         const targetTrack = getPreviousTransportTrack(snapshot);
  869 | 
  870 |         if (targetTrack) {
  871 |           dispatchTargetedPlayIntent(
  872 |             hydrateTrack(targetTrack, snapshot.durationByRecordingId),
  873 |             snapshot,
  874 |           );
  875 |         }
  876 | 
  877 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
  878 |           setState(transitionPreviousState);
  879 |         });
  880 |       },
  881 | 
  882 |       setQueue: (tracks: PlayerTrack[], opts?: QueueContext) =>
  883 |         setState((s) => applyQueueState(s, tracks, opts)),
  884 | 
  885 |       enqueue: (track: PlayerTrack) =>
  886 |         setState((s) => {
  887 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = primeDurationByRecordingId(
  888 |             s.durationByRecordingId,
  889 |             [track],
  890 |           );
  891 |           const t = hydrateTrack(track, nextDurationByRecordingId);
  892 |           return {
  893 |             ...s,
  894 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  895 |             queue: [...s.queue, t],
  896 |             current: s.current ?? t,
  897 |             selectedRecordingId: s.selectedRecordingId ?? t.recordingId,
  898 |           };
  899 |         }),
  900 | 
  901 |       clearQueue: () =>
  902 |         setState((s) => ({
  903 |           ...s,
  904 |           queue: [],
  905 |           current: undefined,
  906 |           selectedRecordingId: undefined,
  907 |           pendingRecordingId: undefined,
  908 | 
  909 |           positionMs: 0,
  910 |           pendingSeekMs: undefined,
  911 |           seeking: false,
  912 |           seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
  913 |           loadingReason: undefined,
  914 |           queueSharePlaybackContext: null,
  915 |           queueSharePlaybackScopeId: null,
  916 |           assetDurationByPlaybackId: {},
  917 |         })),
  918 | 
  919 |       setPositionMs: (ms: number) =>
  920 |         setState((s) => ({
  921 |           ...s,
  922 |           positionMs: Math.max(0, ms),
  923 |         })),
  924 | 
  925 |       setDurationMs: (ms: number) =>
  926 |         setState((s) => {
  927 |           const cur = s.current;
  928 |           if (!cur) return s;
  929 |           if (!Number.isFinite(ms) || ms <= 0) return s;
  930 | 
  931 |           const alreadyCached =
  932 |             typeof s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] === "number" &&
  933 |             s.durationByRecordingId[cur.recordingId] > 0;
  934 |           const alreadyOnTrack =
  935 |             typeof cur.durationMs === "number" && cur.durationMs > 0;
  936 |           if (alreadyCached || alreadyOnTrack) return s;
  937 | 
  938 |           const nextDurationByRecordingId = {
  939 |             ...s.durationByRecordingId,
  940 |             [cur.recordingId]: ms,
  941 |           };
  942 | 
  943 |           const nextCurrent = { ...cur, durationMs: ms };
  944 |           let changed = false;
  945 |           const nextQueue = s.queue.map((t) => {
  946 |             if (t.recordingId !== cur.recordingId) return t;
  947 |             if (typeof t.durationMs === "number" && t.durationMs > 0) return t;
  948 |             changed = true;
  949 |             return { ...t, durationMs: ms };
  950 |           });
  951 | 
  952 |           return {
  953 |             ...s,
  954 |             durationByRecordingId: nextDurationByRecordingId,
  955 |             current: nextCurrent,
  956 |             queue: changed ? nextQueue : s.queue,
  957 |           };
  958 |         }),
  959 | 
  960 |       setAssetDurationMs: (playbackId: string, ms: number) => {
  961 |         const normalizedPlaybackId = playbackId.trim();
  962 |         const normalizedDurationMs = positiveDurationMs(ms);
  963 | 
  964 |         if (!normalizedPlaybackId || normalizedDurationMs <= 0) return;
  965 | 
  966 |         setState((s) => {
  967 |           if (
  968 |             s.assetDurationByPlaybackId[normalizedPlaybackId] ===
  969 |             normalizedDurationMs
  970 |           ) {
  971 |             return s;
  972 |           }
  973 | 
  974 |           return {
  975 |             ...s,
  976 |             assetDurationByPlaybackId: {
  977 |               ...s.assetDurationByPlaybackId,
  978 |               [normalizedPlaybackId]: normalizedDurationMs,
  979 |             },
  980 |           };
  981 |         });
  982 |       },
  983 | 
  984 |       setStatusExternal: (st: PlayerStatus) =>
  985 |         setState((s) => (s.status === st ? s : { ...s, status: st })),
  986 | 
  987 |       setLoadingReasonExternal: (r?: LoadingReason) =>
  988 |         setState((s) =>
  989 |           s.loadingReason === r ? s : { ...s, loadingReason: r },
  990 |         ),
  991 | 
  992 |       seek: (ms: number) => {
  993 |         setState((s) => {
  994 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
  995 |           const next = dur > 0 ? clamp(ms, 0, dur) : Math.max(0, ms);
  996 |           return {
  997 |             ...s,
  998 |             positionMs: next,
  999 |             pendingSeekMs: next,
 1000 |             seeking: true,
 1001 |             seekNonce: s.seekNonce + 1,
 1002 |           };
 1003 |         });
 1004 |       },
 1005 | 
 1006 |       clearPendingSeek: () =>
 1007 |         setState((s) => {
 1008 |           if (!s.seeking && s.pendingSeekMs == null) return s;
 1009 |           return { ...s, seeking: false, pendingSeekMs: undefined };
 1010 |         }),
 1011 | 
 1012 |       setVolume: (v: number) =>
 1013 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, volume: clamp(v, 0, 1) })),
 1014 |       toggleMute: () => setState((s) => ({ ...s, muted: !s.muted })),
 1015 | 
 1016 |       cycleRepeat: () =>
 1017 |         setState((s) => ({ ...s, repeat: nextRepeat(s.repeat) })),
 1018 | 
 1019 |       tick: (deltaMs: number) => {
 1020 |         setState((s) => {
 1021 |           if (s.status !== "playing") return s;
 1022 | 
 1023 |           const dur = getEffectiveDurationMs(s, s.current);
 1024 |           const nextPos = Math.max(0, s.positionMs + Math.max(0, deltaMs));
 1025 | 
 1026 |           if (dur <= 0) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1027 |           if (nextPos < dur) return { ...s, positionMs: nextPos };
 1028 | 
 1029 |           if (s.repeat === "one") return { ...s, positionMs: 0 };
 1030 | 
 1031 |           const cur = s.current;
 1032 |           const idx = cur
 1033 |             ? s.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === cur.recordingId)
 1034 |             : -1;
 1035 |           const at = Math.max(idx, 0);
 1036 |           const nextIdx = at + 1;
 1037 | 
 1038 |           if (nextIdx < s.queue.length) {
 1039 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[nextIdx], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1040 |             return {
 1041 |               ...s,
 1042 |               current: t,
 1043 |               positionMs: 0,
 1044 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1045 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1046 |             };
 1047 |           }
 1048 | 
 1049 |           if (s.repeat === "all" && s.queue.length > 0) {
 1050 |             const t = hydrateTrack(s.queue[0], s.durationByRecordingId);
 1051 |             return {
 1052 |               ...s,
 1053 |               current: t,
 1054 |               positionMs: 0,
 1055 |               selectedRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1056 |               pendingRecordingId: t.recordingId,
 1057 |             };
 1058 |           }
 1059 | 
 1060 |           return { ...s, status: "paused", positionMs: dur };
 1061 |         });
 1062 |       },
 1063 | 
 1064 |       clearError: () =>
 1065 |         setState((s) => (s.lastError ? { ...s, lastError: undefined } : s)),
 1066 | 
 1067 |       bumpReload: () => {
 1068 |         void guardPlayback(() => {
 1069 |           setState((s) => {
 1070 |             if (!s.current?.muxPlaybackId) {
 1071 |               return { ...s, reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1 };
 1072 |             }
 1073 | 
 1074 |             return {
 1075 |               ...s,
 1076 |               reloadNonce: s.reloadNonce + 1,
 1077 |               status: "loading",
 1078 |               loadingReason: "token",
 1079 |               lastError: undefined,
 1080 |               intent: "play",
 1081 |               intentAtMs: Date.now(),
 1082 |               pendingRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1083 |               selectedRecordingId: s.current.recordingId,
 1084 |             };
 1085 |           });
 1086 |         });
 1087 |       },
 1088 |     };
 1089 |   }, [guardPlayback]);
 1090 | 
 1091 |   const api: PlayerState & PlayerActions = React.useMemo(() => {
 1092 |     return {
 1093 |       ...state,
 1094 |       ...actions,
 1095 |     };
 1096 |   }, [state, actions]);
 1097 | 
 1098 |   const {
 1099 |     status: visualStatus,
 1100 |     intent: visualIntent,
 1101 |     playRequestPending: visualPlayRequestPending,
 1102 |     current: visualCurrent,
 1103 |     queue: visualQueue,
 1104 |   } = state;
 1105 | 
 1106 |   const firstQueuedTrack = visualQueue[0];
 1107 | 
 1108 |   const visualState: PlayerVisualState = React.useMemo(
 1109 |     () => ({
 1110 |       status: visualStatus,
 1111 |       intent: visualIntent,
 1112 |       playRequestPending: visualPlayRequestPending,
 1113 |       current: visualCurrent,
 1114 |       firstQueuedTrack,
 1115 |     }),
 1116 |     [
 1117 |       visualStatus,
 1118 |       visualIntent,
 1119 |       visualPlayRequestPending,
 1120 |       visualCurrent,
 1121 |       firstQueuedTrack,
 1122 |     ],
 1123 |   );
 1124 | 
 1125 |   const currentId = state.current?.recordingId ?? null;
 1126 |   const pendingId = state.pendingRecordingId ?? null;
 1127 | 
 1128 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1129 |     const id = (currentId ?? "").trim();
 1130 |     if (!id) return;
 1131 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(id);
 1132 |   }, [currentId]);
 1133 | 
 1134 |   React.useEffect(() => {
 1135 |     const id = (pendingId ?? "").trim();
 1136 |     if (!id) return;
 1137 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(id);
 1138 |   }, [pendingId]);
 1139 | 
 1140 |   return (
 1141 |     <PlayerActionsCtx.Provider value={actions}>
 1142 |       <PlayerVisualCtx.Provider value={visualState}>
 1143 |         <PlayerCtx.Provider value={api}>{props.children}</PlayerCtx.Provider>
 1144 |       </PlayerVisualCtx.Provider>
 1145 |     </PlayerActionsCtx.Provider>
 1146 |   );
 1147 | }
 1148 | 
 1149 | export function usePlayer() {
 1150 |   const ctx = React.useContext(PlayerCtx);
 1151 |   if (!ctx)
 1152 |     throw new Error("usePlayer must be used within PlayerStateProvider");
 1153 |   return ctx;
 1154 | }
 1155 | 
 1156 | export function usePlayerActions() {
 1157 |   const ctx = React.useContext(PlayerActionsCtx);
 1158 |   if (!ctx)
 1159 |     throw new Error("usePlayerActions must be used within PlayerStateProvider");
 1160 |   return ctx;
 1161 | }
 1162 | 
 1163 | export function usePlayerVisual() {
 1164 |   const ctx = React.useContext(PlayerVisualCtx);
 1165 |   if (!ctx)
 1166 |     throw new Error("usePlayerVisual must be used within PlayerStateProvider");
 1167 |   return ctx;
 1168 | }

  111 | export default function StageTransportBar() {
  112 |   const p = usePlayer();
  113 | 
  114 |   const playingish =
  115 |     p.status === "playing" ||
  116 |     p.status === "loading" ||
  117 |     p.intent === "play" ||
  118 |     p.playRequestPending;
  119 | 
  120 |   const curId = p.current?.recordingId ?? "";
  121 |   const idx = curId ? p.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === curId) : -1;
  122 |   const atStart = idx <= 0;
  123 |   const atEnd = idx >= 0 && idx === p.queue.length - 1;
  124 | 
  125 |   const [transportLock, setTransportLock] = React.useState(false);
  126 |   const lockFor = (ms: number) => {
  127 |     setTransportLock(true);
  128 |     window.setTimeout(() => setTransportLock(false), ms);
  129 |   };
  130 | 
  131 |   const prevDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atStart;
  132 |   const nextDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atEnd;
  133 |   const playDisabled = (!p.current && p.queue.length === 0) || transportLock;
  134 | 
  135 |   return (
  136 |     <div
  137 |       data-af-stage-transport
  138 |       style={{
  139 |         position: "absolute",
  140 |         left: 0,
  141 |         right: 0,
  142 |         bottom: 0,
  143 |         zIndex: 8,
  144 | 
  145 |         // This is the “claimed footer zone” the lyrics should not occupy.
  146 |         height: `calc(${STAGE_TRANSPORT_FOOTER_PX}px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`,
  147 | 
  148 |         paddingLeft: `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-left, 0px))`,
  149 |         paddingRight: `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-right, 0px))`,
  150 |         paddingBottom: `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`,
  151 |         paddingTop: 12,
  152 | 
  153 |         pointerEvents: "none",
  154 |       }}
  155 |     >
  156 |       {/* smooth fade so the footer reads, but it’s not a “floating overlay” anymore */}
  157 |       <div
  158 |         aria-hidden
  159 |         style={{
  160 |           position: "absolute",
  161 |           left: 0,
  162 |           right: 0,
  163 |           bottom: 0,
  164 |           top: 0,
  165 |           background:
  166 |             "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.00), rgba(0,0,0,0.60))",
  167 |           pointerEvents: "none",
  168 |         }}
  169 |       />
  170 | 
  171 |       <div
  172 |         style={{
  173 |           position: "relative",
  174 |           pointerEvents: "auto",
  175 |           height: "100%",
  176 |           display: "flex",
  177 |           alignItems: "flex-end",
  178 |           justifyContent: "center",
  179 |           gap: 18,
  180 |         }}
  181 |       >
  182 |         <IconBtn
  183 |           label="Previous"
  184 |           onClick={() => {
  185 |             lockFor(320);
  186 |             p.prev();
  187 |             window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
  188 |           }}
  189 |           disabled={prevDisabled}
  190 |         >
  191 |           <PrevIcon />
  192 |         </IconBtn>
  193 | 
  194 |         <IconBtn
  195 |           label={playingish ? "Pause" : "Play"}
  196 |           onClick={() => {
  197 |             lockFor(180);
  198 | 
  199 |             if (playingish) {
  200 |               window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:pause-intent"));
  201 |               p.pause();
  202 |               return;
  203 |             }
  204 | 
  205 |             const t = p.current ?? p.queue[0];
  206 |             if (!t) return;
  207 | 
  208 |             p.play(t);
  209 |             window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
  210 |           }}
  211 |           disabled={playDisabled}
  212 |         >
  213 |           <PlayPauseIcon playing={playingish} />
  214 |         </IconBtn>
  215 | 
  216 |         <IconBtn
  217 |           label="Next"
  218 |           onClick={() => {
  219 |             lockFor(320);
  220 |             p.next();
  221 |             window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
  222 |           }}
  223 |           disabled={nextDisabled}

  225 |           <NextIcon />
  226 |         </IconBtn>

   19 | export default function VisualizerCanvas(props: { variant: StageVariant }) {
   20 |   const { variant } = props;
   21 |   const player = usePlayerVisual();
   22 | 
   23 |   const canvasRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
   24 |   const engineRef = React.useRef<VisualizerEngine | null>(null);
   25 | 
   26 |   const [activeStage, setActiveStage] = React.useState<StageVariant | null>(
   27 |     () => mediaSurface.getStageVariant(),
   28 |   );
   29 | 
   30 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   31 |     return mediaSurface.subscribe((e) => {
   32 |       if (e.type === "stage") setActiveStage(e.variant);
   33 |     });
   34 |   }, []);
   35 | 
   36 |   const themeName: ThemeName = canonicalThemeName(player.current?.visualTheme);
   37 |   const shouldLoadTrackTheme =
   38 |     player.status === "loading" ||
   39 |     player.status === "playing" ||
   40 |     player.status === "paused" ||
   41 |     player.intent === "play";
   42 | 
   43 |   const themeNameRef = React.useRef<ThemeName>(themeName);
   44 | 
   45 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   46 |     themeNameRef.current = themeName;
   47 |   }, [themeName]);
   48 | 
   49 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   50 |     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
   51 |     if (!canvas) return;
   52 | 
   53 |     const prefersReduced =
   54 |       window.matchMedia?.("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)")?.matches ?? false;
   55 | 
   56 |     const getAudio = () => {
   57 |       const a = audioSurface.get();
   58 |       return prefersReduced ? { ...a, energy: 0.12 } : a;
   59 |     };
   60 | 
   61 |     const engine = new VisualizerEngine({
   62 |       canvas,
   63 |       getAudio,
   64 |       theme: createBlankTheme(),
   65 |       performanceProfile: variant === "fullscreen" ? "fullscreen" : "inline",
   66 |       stageVariant: variant,
   67 |       initialThemeName: themeNameRef.current,
   68 |     });
   69 | 
   70 |     engine.setIdleTheme(createIdleMistTheme());
   71 |     engineRef.current = engine;
   72 | 
   73 |     return () => {
   74 |       try {
   75 |         engine.stop();
   76 |         engine.dispose();
   77 |       } finally {
   78 |         engineRef.current = null;
   79 |       }
   80 |     };
   81 |   }, [variant]);
   82 | 
   83 |   const unregRef = React.useRef<null | (() => void)>(null);
   84 | 
   85 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   86 |     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
   87 |     if (!canvas) return;
   88 | 
   89 |     try {
   90 |       unregRef.current?.();
   91 |     } catch {}
   92 | 
   93 |     unregRef.current = null;
   94 | 
   95 |     if (activeStage === variant) {
   96 |       const snapshotCanvas =
   97 |         engineRef.current?.getStableSnapshotCanvas?.() ?? null;
   98 | 
   99 |       unregRef.current = visualSurface.registerCanvas(
  100 |         variant,
  101 |         canvas,
  102 |         snapshotCanvas,
  103 |       );
  104 |     }
  105 | 
  106 |     return () => {
  107 |       try {
  108 |         unregRef.current?.();
  109 |       } catch {}
  110 | 
  111 |       unregRef.current = null;
  112 |     };

  114 |

  116 |     const engine = engineRef.current;
  117 |     if (!engine) return;
  118 | 
  119 |     if (activeStage === variant) engine.start();
  120 |     else engine.stop();
  121 |   }, [activeStage, variant]);
  122 | 
  123 |   const wantPlaying =
  124 |     player.status === "playing" ||
  125 |     player.status === "loading" ||
  126 |     player.status === "paused" ||
  127 |     player.intent === "play" ||
  128 |     player.playRequestPending;
  129 | 
  130 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  131 |     const engine = engineRef.current;
  132 |     if (!engine) return;
  133 | 
  134 |     engine.setWantPlaying(wantPlaying, { toIdleTransition: true });
  135 |   }, [wantPlaying]);
  136 | 
  137 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  138 |     const engine = engineRef.current;
  139 |     if (!engine || !shouldLoadTrackTheme) return;
  140 | 
  141 |     engine.setThemeDebugName(themeName);
  142 | 
  143 |     let cancelled = false;
  144 | 
  145 |     (async () => {
  146 |       const factory = await loadThemeFactory(themeName);
  147 | 
  148 |       if (cancelled || engineRef.current !== engine) return;
  149 | 
  150 |       engine.setTargetTheme(factory());
  151 |     })().catch(() => {});
  152 | 
  153 |     return () => {
  154 |       cancelled = true;
  155 |     };
  156 |   }, [shouldLoadTrackTheme, themeName]);
  157 |

  159 |     <canvas
  160 |       ref={canvasRef}
  161 |       aria-hidden="true"
  162 |       style={{
  163 |         position: "absolute",
  164 |         inset: 0,
  165 |         width: "100%",
  166 |         height: "100%",

  168 |         pointerEvents: "none",

  170 |       }}

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/VisualizerPattern.tsx =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/VisualizerPattern.tsx
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import { visualSurface } from "./visualSurface";
    6 | 
    7 | type SourceRect =
    8 |   | { mode: "full" }
    9 |   | { mode: "center"; scale?: number }
   10 |   | { mode: "random"; seed: number; scale?: number };
   11 | 
   12 | function clamp(n: number, a: number, b: number) {
   13 |   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   14 | }
   15 | 
   16 | function pickRect(
   17 |   srcW: number,
   18 |   srcH: number,
   19 |   rect: SourceRect,
   20 | ): { sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } {
   21 |   if (srcW <= 1 || srcH <= 1) return { sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: srcW, sh: srcH };
   22 |   if (rect.mode === "full") return { sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: srcW, sh: srcH };
   23 | 
   24 |   const scale = clamp(rect.scale ?? 0.55, 0.15, 1);
   25 |   const sw = Math.max(1, Math.floor(srcW * scale));
   26 |   const sh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(srcH * scale));
   27 | 
   28 |   if (rect.mode === "center") {
   29 |     const sx = Math.floor((srcW - sw) / 2);
   30 |     const sy = Math.floor((srcH - sh) / 2);
   31 |     return { sx, sy, sw, sh };
   32 |   }
   33 | 
   34 |   // deterministic “random”
   35 |   let x = Math.trunc(rect.seed);
   36 |   const rand = () => {
   37 |     // xorshift32
   38 |     x ^= x << 13;
   39 |     x ^= x >>> 17;
   40 |     x ^= x << 5;
   41 |     return ((x >>> 0) % 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;
   42 |   };
   43 | 
   44 |   const sx = Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, srcW - sw));
   45 |   const sy = Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, srcH - sh));
   46 |   return { sx, sy, sw, sh };
   47 | }
   48 | 
   49 | function cssBoxSize(el: HTMLCanvasElement): { cssW: number; cssH: number } {
   50 |   const cssW = Math.max(
   51 |     1,
   52 |     el.clientWidth || Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().width),
   53 |   );
   54 |   const cssH = Math.max(
   55 |     1,
   56 |     el.clientHeight || Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().height),
   57 |   );
   58 |   return { cssW, cssH };
   59 | }
   60 | 
   61 | function getSrcSize(src: HTMLCanvasElement): { srcW: number; srcH: number } {
   62 |   const srcW = src.width || src.clientWidth || 0;
   63 |   const srcH = src.height || src.clientHeight || 0;
   64 |   return { srcW, srcH };
   65 | }

   67 | function useVisualSnapshotSource() {
   68 |   const srcRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
   69 |   const [hasSnapshotSource, setHasSnapshotSource] = React.useState(false);
   70 | 
   71 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   72 |     const apply = (canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null) => {
   73 |       srcRef.current = canvas;
   74 | 
   75 |       const nextHasSnapshotSource = Boolean(canvas);
   76 | 
   77 |       setHasSnapshotSource((previous) =>
   78 |         previous === nextHasSnapshotSource ? previous : nextHasSnapshotSource,
   79 |       );
   80 |     };
   81 | 
   82 |     apply(visualSurface.getSnapshotCanvas());
   83 | 
   84 |     const unsubscribe = visualSurface.subscribe((event) => {
   85 |       if (event.type === "snapshot") {
   86 |         apply(event.canvas);
   87 |       }
   88 |     });
   89 | 
   90 |     return () => {
   91 |       try {
   92 |         unsubscribe();
   93 |       } catch {
   94 |         // VisualSurface subscriptions are best-effort decorative plumbing.
   95 |       }
   96 |     };
   97 |   }, []);
   98 | 
   99 |   return { srcRef, hasSnapshotSource };
  100 | }
  101 | 
  102 | function useCanvasRenderAvailability(
  103 |   canvasRef: React.RefObject<HTMLCanvasElement | null>,
  104 |   requestedActive: boolean,
  105 | ): boolean {
  106 |   const [documentVisible, setDocumentVisible] = React.useState(
  107 |     () => typeof document === "undefined" || !document.hidden,
  108 |   );
  109 |   const [intersecting, setIntersecting] = React.useState(true);
  110 | 
  111 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  112 |     const syncDocumentVisibility = () => {
  113 |       setDocumentVisible(!document.hidden);
  114 |     };
  115 | 
  116 |     syncDocumentVisibility();
  117 | 
  118 |     document.addEventListener(
  119 |       "visibilitychange",
  120 |       syncDocumentVisibility,
  121 |       false,
  122 |     );
  123 | 
  124 |     return () => {
  125 |       document.removeEventListener(
  126 |         "visibilitychange",
  127 |         syncDocumentVisibility,
  128 |         false,
  129 |       );
  130 |     };
  131 |   }, []);
  132 | 
  133 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  134 |     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
  135 | 
  136 |     if (!canvas || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") {
  137 |       setIntersecting(true);
  138 |       return;
  139 |     }
  140 | 
  141 |     const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
  142 |       ([entry]) => {
  143 |         if (!entry) return;
  144 | 
  145 |         const nextIntersecting =
  146 |           entry.isIntersecting &&
  147 |           entry.intersectionRatio > 0 &&
  148 |           entry.boundingClientRect.width > 0 &&
  149 |           entry.boundingClientRect.height > 0;
  150 | 
  151 |         setIntersecting((previous) =>
  152 |           previous === nextIntersecting ? previous : nextIntersecting,
  153 |         );
  154 |       },
  155 |       { threshold: 0 },
  156 |     );
  157 | 
  158 |     observer.observe(canvas);
  159 | 
  160 |     return () => observer.disconnect();
  161 |   }, [canvasRef]);
  162 | 
  163 |   return requestedActive && documentVisible && intersecting;
  164 | }
  165 | 
  166 | /** Cover-fit mapping into destination (fills the canvas, cropping if needed). */
  167 | function coverMap(
  168 |   dstW: number,
  169 |   dstH: number,
  170 |   srcW: number,
  171 |   srcH: number,
  172 | ): { dX: number; dY: number; dW: number; dH: number } {
  173 |   const srcAspect = srcW / Math.max(1, srcH);
  174 |   const dstAspect = dstW / Math.max(1, dstH);
  175 | 
  176 |   let dW = dstW,
  177 |     dH = dstH,
  178 |     dX = 0,
  179 |     dY = 0;
  180 | 
  181 |   if (dstAspect > srcAspect) {
  182 |     dH = Math.round(dstW / srcAspect);
  183 |     dY = Math.round((dstH - dH) / 2);
  184 |   } else {
  185 |     dW = Math.round(dstH * srcAspect);
  186 |     dX = Math.round((dstW - dW) / 2);
  187 |   }
  188 | 
  189 |   return { dX, dY, dW, dH };
  190 | }

  192 | export function VisualizerSnapshotCanvas(props: {
  193 |   className?: string;
  194 |   style?: React.CSSProperties;
  195 |   fps?: number;
  196 |   opacity?: number;
  197 |   sourceRect?: SourceRect;
  198 |   active?: boolean;
  199 |   /** Optional canvas-side filter (avoids CSS filter compositor flicker) */
  200 |   ctxFilter?: string;
  201 | }) {
  202 |   const {
  203 |     className,
  204 |     style,
  205 |     fps = 14,
  206 |     opacity = 0.55,
  207 |     sourceRect = { mode: "center" },
  208 |     active = true,
  209 |     ctxFilter,
  210 |   } = props;
  211 | 
  212 |   const canvasRef = React.useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  213 |   const { srcRef, hasSnapshotSource } = useVisualSnapshotSource();
  214 | 
  215 |   const shouldRender = useCanvasRenderAvailability(
  216 |     canvasRef,
  217 |     active && hasSnapshotSource,
  218 |   );
  219 | 
  220 |   // Keep latest prop values without restarting the visible render loop.
  221 |   const fpsRef = React.useRef(fps);
  222 |   const opacityRef = React.useRef(opacity);
  223 |   const sourceRectRef = React.useRef<SourceRect>(sourceRect);
  224 |   const ctxFilterRef = React.useRef<string | undefined>(ctxFilter);
  225 | 
  226 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  227 |     fpsRef.current = fps;
  228 |     opacityRef.current = opacity;
  229 |     sourceRectRef.current = sourceRect;
  230 |     ctxFilterRef.current = ctxFilter;
  231 |   }, [fps, opacity, sourceRect, ctxFilter]);
  232 | 
  233 |   // Canvas sizing via ResizeObserver (no per-frame layout reads).
  234 |   const sizeRef = React.useRef({ pxW: 1, pxH: 1, dpr: 1 });
  235 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  236 |     const dst = canvasRef.current;
  237 |     if (!dst) return;
  238 | 
  239 |     const apply = () => {
  240 |       const { cssW, cssH } = cssBoxSize(dst);
  241 |       const dpr = Math.max(1, Math.min(2.5, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
  242 |       const pxW = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssW * dpr));
  243 |       const pxH = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssH * dpr));
  244 | 
  245 |       sizeRef.current = { pxW, pxH, dpr };
  246 |       if (dst.width !== pxW) dst.width = pxW;
  247 |       if (dst.height !== pxH) dst.height = pxH;
  248 |     };
  249 | 
  250 |     const ro = new ResizeObserver(apply);
  251 |     ro.observe(dst);
  252 |     apply();
  253 |     return () => ro.disconnect();
  254 |   }, []);
  255 | 
  256 |   // Draw only while the canvas is visible, active, and backed by a snapshot.
  257 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  258 |     if (!shouldRender) return;
  259 | 
  260 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  261 |     let timer: number | null = null;
  262 |     let lastDrawMs = 0;
  263 | 
  264 |     // Cache contexts + a backbuffer to avoid blanking on transient draw failures.
  265 |     const backRef = {
  266 |       canvas: null as HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  267 |       ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null,
  268 |     };
  269 |     const ctxRef = { ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null };
  270 | 
  271 |     const ensure2d = (
  272 |       c: HTMLCanvasElement,
  273 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  274 |       if (ctxRef.ctx && ctxRef.ctx.canvas === c) return ctxRef.ctx;
  275 |       ctxRef.ctx = c.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  276 |       return ctxRef.ctx;
  277 |     };
  278 | 
  279 |     const ensureBack = (
  280 |       w: number,
  281 |       h: number,
  282 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  283 |       let bc = backRef.canvas;
  284 |       if (!bc) {
  285 |         bc = document.createElement("canvas");
  286 |         backRef.canvas = bc;
  287 |       }
  288 |       if (bc.width !== w) bc.width = w;
  289 |       if (bc.height !== h) bc.height = h;
  290 | 
  291 |       if (backRef.ctx && backRef.ctx.canvas === bc) return backRef.ctx;
  292 |       backRef.ctx = bc.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  293 |       return backRef.ctx;
  294 |     };
  295 |     const scheduleNextFrame = () => {
  296 |       const interval = 1000 / Math.max(1, fpsRef.current);
  297 |       const elapsedMs = lastDrawMs ? performance.now() - lastDrawMs : interval;
  298 |       const delayMs = Math.max(0, interval - elapsedMs);
  299 | 
  300 |       const queueFrame = () => {
  301 |         timer = null;
  302 |         raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  303 |       };
  304 | 
  305 |       if (delayMs <= 1) {
  306 |         queueFrame();
  307 |       } else {
  308 |         timer = window.setTimeout(queueFrame, delayMs);
  309 |       }
  310 |     };
  311 | 
  312 |     const tick = (t: number) => {
  313 |       raf = null;
  314 |       lastDrawMs = t;
  315 | 
  316 |       const dst = canvasRef.current;
  317 |       const src = srcRef.current;
  318 | 
  319 |       if (!dst || !src) {
  320 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  321 |         return;
  322 |       }
  323 | 
  324 |       const { pxW, pxH } = sizeRef.current;
  325 |       const ctx = ensure2d(dst);
  326 | 
  327 |       if (!ctx) {
  328 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  329 |         return;
  330 |       }
  331 | 
  332 |       const { srcW, srcH } = getSrcSize(src);
  333 | 
  334 |       if (srcW < 2 || srcH < 2) {
  335 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  336 |         return;
  337 |       }
  338 | 
  339 |       const bctx = ensureBack(pxW, pxH);
  340 | 
  341 |       if (!bctx || !backRef.canvas) {
  342 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  343 |         return;
  344 |       }
  345 | 
  346 |       const { sx, sy, sw, sh } = pickRect(srcW, srcH, sourceRectRef.current);
  347 |       const { dX, dY, dW, dH } = coverMap(pxW, pxH, sw, sh);
  348 | 
  349 |       try {
  350 |         // ---- render onto backbuffer ----
  351 |         bctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  352 |         bctx.clearRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  353 | 
  354 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
  355 |         bctx.globalAlpha = opacityRef.current;
  356 |         bctx.filter = ctxFilterRef.current ?? "none";
  357 |         bctx.drawImage(src, sx, sy, sw, sh, dX, dY, dW, dH);
  358 | 
  359 |         // ---- present once ----
  360 |         ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  361 |         ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  362 |         ctx.filter = "none";
  363 |         ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "copy";
  364 |         ctx.drawImage(backRef.canvas, 0, 0);
  365 |       } catch {
  366 |         // Keep the last good onscreen frame.
  367 |       }
  368 | 
  369 |       scheduleNextFrame();
  370 |     };
  371 | 
  372 |     scheduleNextFrame();
  373 | 
  374 |     return () => {
  375 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  376 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
  377 |     };
  378 |   }, [shouldRender, srcRef]);
  379 | 
  380 |   return (
  381 |     <canvas
  382 |       ref={canvasRef}
  383 |       aria-hidden="true"
  384 |       className={className}
  385 |       style={{
  386 |         width: "100%",
  387 |         height: "100%",
  388 |         display: "block",
  389 |         pointerEvents: "none",
  390 |         background: "transparent",
  391 |         transform: "translateZ(0)",
  392 |         backfaceVisibility: "hidden",
  393 |         willChange: "transform, opacity",
  394 |         contain: "paint",
  395 |         ...style,
  396 |       }}
  397 |     />
  398 |   );
  399 | }

  401 | function VisualizerRingGlowCanvas(props: {

  406 |   opacity: number;

  441 |

  443 |   const sizeRef = React.useRef({ pxW: 1, pxH: 1, dpr: 1 });
  444 |   React.useEffect(() => {

  447 |

  452 |       const pxH = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssH * dpr));
  453 |

  455 |       if (dst.width !== pxW) dst.width = pxW;
  456 |       if (dst.height !== pxH) dst.height = pxH;

  458 | 
  459 |     const ro = new ResizeObserver(apply);
  460 |     ro.observe(dst);
  461 |     apply();

  463 |   }, []);
  464 | 
  465 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  466 |     if (!shouldRender) return;
  467 | 
  468 |     let raf: number | null = null;
  469 |     let timer: number | null = null;
  470 |     let lastDrawMs = 0;
  471 | 
  472 |     const backRef = {
  473 |       canvas: null as HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  474 |       ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null,
  475 |     };
  476 |     const ctxRef = { ctx: null as CanvasRenderingContext2D | null };
  477 | 
  478 |     const ensure2d = (
  479 |       c: HTMLCanvasElement,
  480 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  481 |       if (ctxRef.ctx && ctxRef.ctx.canvas === c) return ctxRef.ctx;
  482 |       ctxRef.ctx = c.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  483 |       return ctxRef.ctx;
  484 |     };
  485 | 
  486 |     const ensureBack = (
  487 |       w: number,
  488 |       h: number,
  489 |     ): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null => {
  490 |       let bc = backRef.canvas;
  491 |       if (!bc) {
  492 |         bc = document.createElement("canvas");
  493 |         backRef.canvas = bc;
  494 |       }
  495 |       if (bc.width !== w) bc.width = w;
  496 |       if (bc.height !== h) bc.height = h;
  497 | 
  498 |       if (backRef.ctx && backRef.ctx.canvas === bc) return backRef.ctx;
  499 |       backRef.ctx = bc.getContext("2d", { alpha: true });
  500 |       return backRef.ctx;
  501 |     };
  502 | 
  503 |     const scheduleNextFrame = () => {
  504 |       const interval = 1000 / Math.max(1, fpsRef.current);
  505 |       const elapsedMs = lastDrawMs ? performance.now() - lastDrawMs : interval;
  506 |       const delayMs = Math.max(0, interval - elapsedMs);
  507 | 
  508 |       const queueFrame = () => {
  509 |         timer = null;
  510 |         raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  511 |       };
  512 | 
  513 |       if (delayMs <= 1) {
  514 |         queueFrame();
  515 |       } else {
  516 |         timer = window.setTimeout(queueFrame, delayMs);
  517 |       }
  518 |     };
  519 | 
  520 |     const tick = (t: number) => {
  521 |       raf = null;
  522 |       lastDrawMs = t;
  523 | 
  524 |       const dst = canvasRef.current;
  525 |       const src = srcRef.current;
  526 | 
  527 |       if (!dst || !src) {
  528 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  529 |         return;
  530 |       }
  531 | 
  532 |       const { pxW, pxH, dpr } = sizeRef.current;
  533 |       const ctx = ensure2d(dst);
  534 | 
  535 |       if (!ctx) {
  536 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  537 |         return;
  538 |       }
  539 | 
  540 |       const { srcW, srcH } = getSrcSize(src);
  541 | 
  542 |       if (srcW < 2 || srcH < 2) {
  543 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  544 |         return;
  545 |       }
  546 | 
  547 |       const ringPxNow = ringRef.current;
  548 |       const glowPxNow = glowRef.current;
  549 |       const blurPxNow = blurRef.current;
  550 |       const sizeNow = sizeParamRef.current;
  551 | 
  552 |       // geometry (CSS px)
  553 |       const pad = ringPxNow + glowPxNow;
  554 |       const bleed = Math.max(2, Math.round(blurPxNow * 2));
  555 |       const outerPad = pad + bleed;
  556 | 
  557 |       const outerR = sizeNow / 2 + outerPad;
  558 |       const fadeWidth = glowPxNow + bleed;
  559 |       const fadeStart = Math.max(0, outerR - fadeWidth);
  560 |       const innerR = Math.max(0, sizeNow / 2 - ringPxNow);
  561 | 
  562 |       const { sx, sy, sw, sh } = pickRect(srcW, srcH, sourceRectRef.current);
  563 | 
  564 |       // cover fill: keep aspect, crop if needed
  565 |       const { dX, dY, dW, dH } = coverMap(pxW, pxH, sw, sh);
  566 | 
  567 |       const bctx = ensureBack(pxW, pxH);
  568 | 
  569 |       if (!bctx || !backRef.canvas) {
  570 |         scheduleNextFrame();
  571 |         return;
  572 |       }
  573 | 
  574 |       try {
  575 |         // ---- render EVERYTHING onto backbuffer ----
  576 |         bctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  577 |         bctx.clearRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  578 | 
  579 |         // texture
  580 |         bctx.save();
  581 |         bctx.globalAlpha = opacityRef.current;
  582 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "screen";
  583 |         bctx.filter = `blur(${Math.max(0, blurPxNow) * dpr}px) contrast(1.55) saturate(1.55) brightness(1.25)`;
  584 |         bctx.drawImage(src, sx, sy, sw, sh, dX, dY, dW, dH);
  585 |         bctx.restore();
  586 | 
  587 |         // donut mask
  588 |         const cx = pxW / 2;
  589 |         const cy = pxH / 2;
  590 |         const outerRp = outerR * dpr;
  591 |         const innerRp = innerR * dpr;
  592 | 
  593 |         bctx.save();
  594 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-in";
  595 |         bctx.beginPath();
  596 |         bctx.arc(cx, cy, outerRp, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  597 |         bctx.closePath();
  598 |         bctx.fill();
  599 |         bctx.restore();
  600 | 
  601 |         if (innerRp > 0) {
  602 |           bctx.save();
  603 |           bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
  604 |           bctx.beginPath();
  605 |           bctx.arc(cx, cy, innerRp, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  606 |           bctx.closePath();
  607 |           bctx.fill();
  608 |           bctx.restore();
  609 |         }
  610 | 
  611 |         // outer fade
  612 |         bctx.save();
  613 |         bctx.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-in";
  614 |         const g = bctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, outerRp);
  615 |         const s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fadeStart / outerR));
  616 |         g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,1)");
  617 |         g.addColorStop(s, "rgba(0,0,0,1)");
  618 |         g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
  619 |         bctx.fillStyle = g;
  620 |         bctx.fillRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
  621 |         bctx.restore();
  622 | 
  623 |         // ---- present once ----
  624 |         ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  625 |         ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  626 |         ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "copy";
  627 |         ctx.drawImage(backRef.canvas, 0, 0);
  628 |       } catch {
  629 |         // Keep the last good onscreen frame.
  630 |       }
  631 | 
  632 |       scheduleNextFrame();
  633 |     };
  634 | 
  635 |     scheduleNextFrame();
  636 | 
  637 |     return () => {
  638 |       if (raf != null) window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  639 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
  640 |     };
  641 |   }, [shouldRender, srcRef]);
  642 | 
  643 |   // CSS size is controlled by parent, but we set explicit dimensions for clarity
  644 |   const pad = ringPx + glowPx;
  645 |   const bleed = Math.max(2, Math.round(blurPx * 2));
  646 |   const outerPad = pad + bleed;
  647 |   const cssSize = size + outerPad * 2;
  648 | 
  649 |   return (
  650 |     <canvas
  651 |       ref={canvasRef}
  652 |       aria-hidden="true"
  653 |       style={{
  654 |         width: cssSize,
  655 |         height: cssSize,
  656 |         display: "block",
  657 |         pointerEvents: "none",
  658 |         background: "transparent",
  659 |       }}
  660 |     />
  661 |   );
  662 | }
  663 |

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import { lyricsSurface } from "./lyricsSurface";
    5 | import { fetchLyricsByrecordingId } from "./fetchLyricsByRecordingId";
    6 | import type { AlbumLyricsBundle } from "@/lib/types";
    7 | 
    8 | // Module-scope in-flight map so *any* caller (any surface) dedupes fetches.
    9 | const inFlight = new Map<string, AbortController>();
   10 | 
   11 | function hasOwn(obj: object, key: string): boolean {
   12 |   return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key);
   13 | }
   14 | 
   15 | /**
   16 |  * Prime lyricsSurface from an album-provided bundle.
   17 |  * - Merges without clobbering existing per-track entries (unless missing).
   18 |  * - Ensures we still preserve "known no lyrics" as [] if already present.
   19 |  * - No network; safe to call repeatedly (idempotent-ish).
   20 |  */
   21 | export function primeLyricsFromAlbumBundle(
   22 |   bundle: AlbumLyricsBundle | null | undefined,
   23 | ) {
   24 |   if (!bundle) return;
   25 | 
   26 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   27 | 
   28 |   const cuesIn = bundle.cuesByRecordingId ?? {};
   29 |   const offIn = bundle.offsetByRecordingId ?? {};
   30 | 
   31 |   // Build merged maps but only add keys that are currently unknown.
   32 |   let cuesOut = snap.cuesByRecordingId;
   33 |   let offOut = snap.offsetByRecordingId;
   34 | 
   35 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, cuesRaw] of Object.entries(cuesIn)) {
   36 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   37 |     if (!id) continue;
   38 | 
   39 |     if (hasOwn(cuesOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing (including [] known-no-lyrics)
   40 | 
   41 |     const cues = Array.isArray(cuesRaw) ? cuesRaw : [];
   42 |     cuesOut = { ...cuesOut, [id]: cues };
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, offRaw] of Object.entries(offIn)) {
   46 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   47 |     if (!id) continue;
   48 | 
   49 |     if (hasOwn(offOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing
   50 | 
   51 |     const n =
   52 |       typeof offRaw === "number" && Number.isFinite(offRaw)
   53 |         ? Math.floor(offRaw)
   54 |         : 0;
   55 | 
   56 |     offOut = { ...offOut, [id]: n };
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   // Only write if something changed (cheap reference check).
   60 |   if (cuesOut === snap.cuesByRecordingId && offOut === snap.offsetByRecordingId) return;
   61 | 
   62 |   lyricsSurface.setMaps({
   63 |     cuesByrecordingId: cuesOut,
   64 |     offsetByrecordingId: offOut,
   65 |     globalOffsetMs: snap.globalOffsetMs,
   66 |   });
   67 | }
   68 | 
   69 | /**
   70 |  * Ensure lyrics (cues + offset) exist in lyricsSurface for recordingId.
   71 |  * - Dedupes concurrent calls (module-level inFlight).
   72 |  * - Caches "no lyrics" as [] to avoid refetch loops.
   73 |  * - Safe to call repeatedly from any surface.
   74 |  */
   75 | export async function ensureLyricsForTrack(
   76 |   recordingId: string,
   77 |   opts?: { signal?: AbortSignal },
   78 | ): Promise<void> {
   79 |   const id = (recordingId ?? "").trim();
   80 |   if (!id) return;
   81 | 
   82 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   83 |   const existing = snap.cuesByRecordingId[id];
   84 | 
   85 |   const knownKey = hasOwn(snap.cuesByRecordingId, id);
   86 |   const hasCues = Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length > 0;
   87 |   const knownNoLyrics =
   88 |     knownKey && Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length === 0;
   89 | 
   90 |   if (hasCues || knownNoLyrics) return;
   91 | 
   92 |   // Already fetching?
   93 |   if (inFlight.has(id)) return;
   94 | 
   95 |   const ac = new AbortController();
   96 |   inFlight.set(id, ac);
   97 | 
   98 |   // Caller abort should NOT abort the shared in-flight request.
   99 |   // It only means "this caller no longer cares". The module-scope fetch may be
  100 |   // servicing other callers (PlayerState, StageInline, etc).
  101 |   const outerSignal = opts?.signal;
  102 |   if (outerSignal?.aborted) {
  103 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  104 |     return;
  105 |   }
  106 | 
  107 |   try {
  108 |     const r = await fetchLyricsByrecordingId(id, ac.signal);
  109 |     if (!r) return;
  110 |     if (r.recordingId !== id) return;
  111 | 
  112 |     const prev = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
  113 | 
  114 |     const nextCuesByrecordingId =
  115 |       Array.isArray(r.cues) && r.cues.length
  116 |         ? { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: r.cues }
  117 |         : hasOwn(prev.cuesByRecordingId, id)
  118 |           ? prev.cuesByRecordingId
  119 |           : { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: [] };
  120 | 
  121 |     const nextOffsetByrecordingId =
  122 |       typeof r.offsetMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(r.offsetMs)
  123 |         ? { ...prev.offsetByRecordingId, [id]: r.offsetMs }
  124 |         : prev.offsetByRecordingId;
  125 | 
  126 |     lyricsSurface.setMaps({
  127 |       cuesByrecordingId: nextCuesByrecordingId,
  128 |       offsetByrecordingId: nextOffsetByrecordingId,
  129 |       // preserve whatever globalOffsetMs currently is (lyricsSurface handles defaulting)
  130 |       globalOffsetMs: prev.globalOffsetMs,
  131 |     });
  132 |   } finally {
  133 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  134 |   }
  135 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/mediaSurface.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/mediaSurface.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | export type MediaStatus = "idle" | "loading" | "playing" | "paused" | "blocked";
    5 | export type StageVariant = "inline" | "fullscreen";
    6 | 
    7 | export type MediaEvent =
    8 |   | { type: "time"; ms: number }
    9 |   | { type: "status"; status: MediaStatus }
   10 |   | { type: "track"; id: string | null }
   11 |   | { type: "stage"; variant: StageVariant | null };
   12 | 
   13 | type Listener = (e: MediaEvent) => void;
   14 | 
   15 | class MediaSurface {
   16 |   private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
   17 |   private lastTimeMs = 0;
   18 |   private lastStatus: MediaStatus = "idle";
   19 |   private lastRecordingId: string | null = null;
   20 | 
   21 |   // Stage “authority”
   22 |   private inlineCount = 0;
   23 |   private fullscreenCount = 0;
   24 |   private activeStage: StageVariant | null = null;
   25 | 
   26 |   subscribe(fn: Listener) {
   27 |     this.listeners.add(fn);
   28 |     fn({ type: "time", ms: this.lastTimeMs });
   29 |     fn({ type: "status", status: this.lastStatus });
   30 |     fn({ type: "track", id: this.lastRecordingId });
   31 |     fn({ type: "stage", variant: this.activeStage });
   32 | 
   33 |     return () => {
   34 |       this.listeners.delete(fn);
   35 |     };
   36 |   }
   37 | 
   38 |   /* ---------------- media state ---------------- */
   39 | 
   40 |   setTime(ms: number) {
   41 |     this.lastTimeMs = ms;
   42 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "time", ms });
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   setStatus(status: MediaStatus) {
   46 |     this.lastStatus = status;
   47 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "status", status });
   48 |   }
   49 | 
   50 |   setTrack(id: string | null) {
   51 |     this.lastRecordingId = id;
   52 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "track", id });
   53 |   }
   54 | 
   55 |   getTimeMs() {
   56 |     return this.lastTimeMs;
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   getStatus() {
   60 |     return this.lastStatus;
   61 |   }
   62 | 
   63 |   getRecordingId() {
   64 |     return this.lastRecordingId;
   65 |   }
   66 | 
   67 |   /* ---------------- stage authority ---------------- */
   68 | 
   69 |   private recomputeStage() {
   70 |     const next: StageVariant | null =
   71 |       this.fullscreenCount > 0
   72 |         ? "fullscreen"
   73 |         : this.inlineCount > 0
   74 |           ? "inline"
   75 |           : null;
   76 |     if (next === this.activeStage) return;
   77 |     this.activeStage = next;
   78 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "stage", variant: next });
   79 |   }
   80 | 
   81 |   registerStage(variant: StageVariant) {
   82 |     if (variant === "fullscreen") this.fullscreenCount++;
   83 |     else this.inlineCount++;
   84 |     this.recomputeStage();
   85 | 
   86 |     return () => {
   87 |       if (variant === "fullscreen")
   88 |         this.fullscreenCount = Math.max(0, this.fullscreenCount - 1);
   89 |       else this.inlineCount = Math.max(0, this.inlineCount - 1);
   90 |       this.recomputeStage();
   91 |     };
   92 |   }
   93 | 
   94 |   getStageVariant(): StageVariant | null {
   95 |     return this.activeStage;
   96 |   }
   97 | }
   98 | 
   99 | export const mediaSurface = new MediaSurface();
