   49 | 
   50 | type CachedPlaybackAccessDecision = {
   51 |   decision: PlaybackAccessDecision;
   52 |   expiresAtMs: number;
   53 | };
   54 | 
   55 | const PLAYBACK_ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60_000;
   56 | 
   57 | const decisionsByKey = new Map<string, CachedPlaybackAccessDecision>();
   58 | const inFlightByKey = new Map<string, Promise<PlaybackAccessDecision>>();
   59 | 
   60 | function getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(
   61 |   key: string,
   62 | ): PlaybackAccessDecision | null {
   63 |   const cached = decisionsByKey.get(key);
   64 | 
   65 |   if (!cached) return null;
   66 | 
   67 |   if (cached.expiresAtMs <= Date.now()) {
   68 |     decisionsByKey.delete(key);
   69 |     return null;
   70 |   }
   71 | 
   72 |   return cached.decision;
   73 | }
   74 | 
   75 | function normalizeCatalogueId(value: string): string {
   76 |   return value.trim();
   77 | }
   78 | 
   79 | function normalizeShareToken(value: string | null): string | null {
   80 |   const token = (value ?? "").trim();
   81 |   return token || null;
   82 | }
   83 | 
   84 | function playbackAccessKey(request: PlaybackAccessRequest): string {
   85 |   const catalogueId = normalizeCatalogueId(request.catalogueId);
   86 |   const shareToken = normalizeShareToken(request.shareToken);
   87 | 
   88 |   return `${catalogueId}::st=${shareToken ?? ""}::identity=${
   89 |     request.accessIdentityKey
   90 |   }`;
   91 | }
   92 | 
   93 | function parseShareTokenAccess(
   94 |   value: unknown,
   95 | ): PlaybackShareTokenAccess | null {
   96 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
   97 | 
   98 |   const raw = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   99 |   const hasExpiresAt = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, "expiresAt");
  100 |   const hasMaxRedemptions = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
  101 |     raw,
  102 |     "maxRedemptions",
  103 |   );
  104 | 
  105 |   if (!hasExpiresAt && !hasMaxRedemptions) return null;
  106 | 
  107 |   const expiresAtRaw = raw.expiresAt;
  108 |   const maxRedemptionsRaw = raw.maxRedemptions;
  109 | 
  110 |   const expiresAt =
  111 |     expiresAtRaw == null
  112 |       ? null
  113 |       : typeof expiresAtRaw === "string" &&
  114 |           Number.isFinite(Date.parse(expiresAtRaw))
  115 |         ? expiresAtRaw
  116 |         : null;
  117 | 
  118 |   const maxRedemptions =
  119 |     maxRedemptionsRaw == null
  120 |       ? null
  121 |       : typeof maxRedemptionsRaw === "number" &&
  122 |           Number.isFinite(maxRedemptionsRaw) &&
  123 |           maxRedemptionsRaw >= 1
  124 |         ? Math.floor(maxRedemptionsRaw)
  125 |         : null;
  126 | 
  127 |   if (expiresAtRaw != null && expiresAt == null) return null;
  128 |   if (maxRedemptionsRaw != null && maxRedemptions == null) return null;
  129 | 
  130 |   return { expiresAt, maxRedemptions };
  131 | }
  132 | 
  133 | function parseSharePlaybackContext(value: unknown): string | null {
  134 |   if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  135 | 
  136 |   const context = value.trim();
  137 | 
  138 |   return context.startsWith("stpc1.") && context.length <= 900 ? context : null;
  139 | }
  140 | 
  141 | function parseSharePlaybackScopeId(value: unknown): string | null {
  142 |   if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  143 | 
  144 |   const scopeId = value.trim();
  145 | 
  146 |   return /^alb:[^\s]+$/i.test(scopeId) ? scopeId : null;
  147 | }
  148 | 
  149 | export function isPlaybackAccessAction(
  150 |   value: unknown,
  151 | ): value is PlaybackAccessAction {
  152 |   return (
  153 |     typeof value === "string" &&
  154 |     (PLAYBACK_ACCESS_ACTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(value)
  155 |   );
  156 | }
  157 | 
  158 | export function readPlaybackShareTokenFromLocation(): string | null {
  159 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  160 | 
  161 |   const search = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  162 |   const token = (search.get("st") ?? search.get("share") ?? "").trim();
  163 | 
  164 |   return token || null;
  165 | }
  166 | 
  167 | export function getCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(
  168 |   request: PlaybackAccessRequest,
  169 | ): PlaybackAccessDecision | null {
  170 |   return getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(playbackAccessKey(request));
  171 | }
  172 | 
  173 | export async function fetchPlaybackAccessDecision(
  174 |   request: PlaybackAccessRequest,
  175 | ): Promise<PlaybackAccessDecision> {
  176 |   const catalogueId = normalizeCatalogueId(request.catalogueId);
  177 | 
  178 |   if (!catalogueId) {
  179 |     throw new Error("Playback access requires a catalogue ID.");
  180 |   }
  181 | 
  182 |   const shareToken = normalizeShareToken(request.shareToken);
  183 |   const key = playbackAccessKey({
  184 |     catalogueId,
  185 |     shareToken,
  186 |     accessIdentityKey: request.accessIdentityKey,
  187 |   });
  188 | 
  189 |   const cached = getFreshCachedPlaybackAccessDecision(key);
  190 |   if (cached) return cached;
  191 | 
  192 |   const existing = inFlightByKey.get(key);
  193 |   if (existing) return existing;
  194 | 
  195 |   const promise = (async () => {
  196 |     const url = new URL("/api/access/check", window.location.origin);
  197 |     url.searchParams.set("albumId", catalogueId);
  198 | 
  199 |     if (shareToken) {
  200 |       url.searchParams.set("st", shareToken);
  201 |     }
  202 | 
  203 |     const response = await fetch(url.toString(), {
  204 |       method: "GET",
  205 |       cache: "no-store",
  206 |     });
  207 | 
  208 |     const body = (await response.json()) as PlaybackAccessResponse;
  209 | 
  210 |     const decision: PlaybackAccessDecision = {
  211 |       forCatalogueId: catalogueId,
  212 |       allowed: body.allowed !== false,
  213 |       embargoed: body.embargoed === true,
  214 |       releaseAt: body.releaseAt ?? null,
  215 |       code:
  216 |         typeof body.code === "string" && body.code.trim()
  217 |           ? body.code
  218 |           : undefined,
  219 |       action: isPlaybackAccessAction(body.action) ? body.action : undefined,
  220 |       reason:
  221 |         typeof body.reason === "string" && body.reason.trim()
  222 |           ? body.reason
  223 |           : undefined,
  224 |       corr: response.headers.get("x-correlation-id") ?? null,
  225 |       shareTokenAccess: parseShareTokenAccess(body.shareTokenAccess),
  226 |       sharePlaybackContext: parseSharePlaybackContext(
  227 |         body.sharePlaybackContext,
  228 |       ),
  229 |       sharePlaybackScopeId: parseSharePlaybackScopeId(
  230 |         body.sharePlaybackScopeId,
  231 |       ),
  232 |     };
  233 | 
  234 |     decisionsByKey.set(key, {
  235 |       decision,
  236 |       expiresAtMs: Date.now() + PLAYBACK_ACCESS_CACHE_TTL_MS,
  237 |     });
  238 | 
  239 |     return decision;
  240 |   })().finally(() => {
  241 |     inFlightByKey.delete(key);
  242 |   });
  243 | 
  244 |   inFlightByKey.set(key, promise);
  245 |   return promise;
  246 | }

    1 | // web/app/home/player/visualSurface.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import type { StageVariant } from "./mediaSurface";
    5 | 
    6 | export type VisualSurfaceEvent =
    7 |   | { type: "canvas"; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null }
    8 |   | { type: "snapshot"; canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null };
    9 | 
   10 | type Listener = (e: VisualSurfaceEvent) => void;
   11 | 
   12 | /** Toggle via: window.__AF_VIS_DEBUG = true */
   13 | function debugEnabled(): boolean {
   14 |   return Boolean((globalThis as { __AF_VIS_DEBUG?: boolean }).__AF_VIS_DEBUG);
   15 | }
   16 | 
   17 | class VisualSurface {
   18 |   private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
   19 | 
   20 |   private inlineCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   21 |   private fullscreenCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   22 |   private active: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   23 | 
   24 |   private inlineSnapshotCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   25 |   private fullscreenSnapshotCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   26 |   private activeSnapshot: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
   27 | 
   28 |   private notify(e: VisualSurfaceEvent) {
   29 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) {
   30 |       try {
   31 |         fn(e);
   32 |       } catch {
   33 |         // ignore listener errors
   34 |       }
   35 |     }
   36 |   }
   37 | 
   38 |   private recompute() {
   39 |     const nextCanvas = this.fullscreenCanvas ?? this.inlineCanvas ?? null;
   40 |     const nextSnapshot =
   41 |       this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas ?? this.inlineSnapshotCanvas ?? null;
   42 | 
   43 |     const canvasChanged = nextCanvas !== this.active;
   44 |     const snapshotChanged = nextSnapshot !== this.activeSnapshot;
   45 | 
   46 |     if (!canvasChanged && !snapshotChanged) return;
   47 | 
   48 |     this.active = nextCanvas;
   49 |     this.activeSnapshot = nextSnapshot;
   50 | 
   51 |     if (canvasChanged) {
   52 |       this.notify({ type: "canvas", canvas: this.active });
   53 |     }
   54 | 
   55 |     if (snapshotChanged) {
   56 |       this.notify({ type: "snapshot", canvas: this.activeSnapshot });
   57 |     }
   58 | 
   59 |     if (debugEnabled()) {
   60 |       console.log("[vis] recompute", {
   61 |         activeVariant: this.fullscreenCanvas
   62 |           ? "fullscreen"
   63 |           : this.inlineCanvas
   64 |             ? "inline"
   65 |             : null,
   66 |         hasCanvas: Boolean(this.active),
   67 |         hasSnapshot: Boolean(this.activeSnapshot),
   68 |       });
   69 |     }
   70 |   }
   71 | 
   72 |   getCanvas(): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
   73 |     return this.active;
   74 |   }
   75 | 
   76 |   getSnapshotCanvas(): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
   77 |     return this.activeSnapshot;
   78 |   }
   79 | 
   80 |   /**
   81 |    * Register live and snapshot canvases for a stage variant. Fullscreen always wins if present.
   82 |    * Returns an unsubscribe cleanup.
   83 |    */
   84 |   registerCanvas(
   85 |     variant: StageVariant,
   86 |     canvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
   87 |     snapshotCanvas?: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
   88 |   ) {
   89 |     if (variant === "fullscreen") {
   90 |       this.fullscreenCanvas = canvas;
   91 |       this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas = snapshotCanvas ?? null;
   92 |     } else {
   93 |       this.inlineCanvas = canvas;
   94 |       this.inlineSnapshotCanvas = snapshotCanvas ?? null;
   95 |     }
   96 | 
   97 |     this.recompute();
   98 | 
   99 |     return () => {
  100 |       if (variant === "fullscreen") {
  101 |         if (this.fullscreenCanvas === canvas) this.fullscreenCanvas = null;
  102 |         if (this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas === (snapshotCanvas ?? null)) {
  103 |           this.fullscreenSnapshotCanvas = null;
  104 |         }
  105 |       } else {
  106 |         if (this.inlineCanvas === canvas) this.inlineCanvas = null;
  107 |         if (this.inlineSnapshotCanvas === (snapshotCanvas ?? null)) {
  108 |           this.inlineSnapshotCanvas = null;
  109 |         }
  110 |       }
  111 |       this.recompute();
  112 |     };
  113 |   }
  114 | 
  115 |   subscribe(fn: Listener) {
  116 |     this.listeners.add(fn);
  117 | 
  118 |     fn({ type: "canvas", canvas: this.active });
  119 |     fn({ type: "snapshot", canvas: this.activeSnapshot });
  120 | 
  121 |     return () => {
  122 |       this.listeners.delete(fn);
  123 |     };
  124 |   }
  125 | }
  126 | 
  127 | export const visualSurface = new VisualSurface();

===== COMPLETE FILE: lib/nav/preservedQuery.ts =====
    1 | // web/lib/nav/preservedQuery.ts
    2 | export type PageSearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
    3 | 
    4 | type PreservedQueryReader = {
    5 |   get: (key: string) => string;
    6 |   entries: () => Iterable<[string, string]>;
    7 | };
    8 | 
    9 | const DIRECT_PRESERVED_KEYS = [
   10 |   "autoplay",
   11 |   "post",
   12 |   "pt",
   13 |   "gift",
   14 |   "checkout",
   15 |   "purchase",
   16 |   "purchaseAlbum",
   17 | ] as const;
   18 | 
   19 | function first(sp: PageSearchParams | undefined, key: string): string {
   20 |   const value = sp?.[key];
   21 | 
   22 |   return Array.isArray(value)
   23 |     ? (value[0] ?? "").trim()
   24 |     : typeof value === "string"
   25 |       ? value.trim()
   26 |       : "";
   27 | }
   28 | 
   29 | function queryStringFromParams(params: URLSearchParams): string {
   30 |   const qs = params.toString();
   31 |   return qs ? `?${qs}` : "";
   32 | }
   33 | 
   34 | function buildPreservedQuery(reader: PreservedQueryReader): string {
   35 |   const out = new URLSearchParams();
   36 | 
   37 |   const shareToken = reader.get("st") || reader.get("share");
   38 |   if (shareToken) out.set("st", shareToken);
   39 | 
   40 |   for (const key of DIRECT_PRESERVED_KEYS) {
   41 |     const value = reader.get(key);
   42 |     if (value) out.set(key, value);
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   for (const [key, value] of reader.entries()) {
   46 |     if (key.startsWith("utm_") && value.trim()) {
   47 |       out.set(key, value.trim());
   48 |     }
   49 |   }
   50 | 
   51 |   return queryStringFromParams(out);
   52 | }
   53 | 
   54 | /** Server: feed Next.js `searchParams` object form. Returns "" or "?a=b". */
   55 | export function preservedQueryFromSearchParams(
   56 |   sp: PageSearchParams | undefined,
   57 | ): string {
   58 |   return buildPreservedQuery({
   59 |     get: (key) => first(sp, key),
   60 |     entries: () =>
   61 |       Object.entries(sp ?? {}).flatMap(([key, raw]) => {
   62 |         const value = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
   63 |         return typeof value === "string" ? [[key, value] as const] : [];
   64 |       }),
   65 |   });
   66 | }
   67 | 
   68 | /** Client: reads window.location.search. Returns "" or "?a=b". */
   69 | export function preservedQueryFromLocation(): string {
   70 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
   71 | 
   72 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
   73 | 
   74 |   return buildPreservedQuery({
   75 |     get: (key) => (sp.get(key) ?? "").trim(),
   76 |     entries: () => sp.entries(),
   77 |   });
   78 | }

   35 |   let end = s.length;
   36 |   while (end > 0 && s.charCodeAt(end - 1) === 47) {
   37 |     end -= 1;
   38 |   }

   72 | export function buildShareTarget(
   73 |   input:
   74 |     | {
   75 |         type: "album";
   76 |         methodHint?: ShareMethod;
   77 |         origin?: string;
   78 |         st?: string;
   79 |         album: {
   80 |           slug: string;
   81 |           title: string;
   82 |           artistName?: string;
   83 |           id?: string;
   84 |         };
   85 |       }
   86 |     | {
   87 |         type: "track";
   88 |         methodHint?: ShareMethod;
   89 |         origin?: string;
   90 |         st?: string;
   91 |         album: {
   92 |           slug: string;
   93 |           title: string;
   94 |           artistName?: string;
   95 |           id?: string;
   96 |         };
   97 |         track: { recordingId: string; displayId: string; title: string };
   98 |       }
   99 |     | {
  100 |         type: "post";
  101 |         methodHint?: ShareMethod;
  102 |         origin?: string;
  103 |         st?: string;
  104 |         post: { slug: string; title?: string; id?: string };
  105 |         authorName?: string;
  106 |       },
  107 | ): ShareTarget {
  108 |   const origin = getOrigin(input.origin);
  109 |   const method = input.methodHint ?? "copy";
  110 | 
  111 |   if (input.type === "post") {
  112 |     const postTitle = input.post.title?.trim() || "Post";
  113 |     const basePath = `/journal?post=${encodePathSeg(input.post.slug)}`;
  114 |     const baseAbs = origin ? `${origin}${basePath}` : basePath;
  115 | 
  116 |     const abs = origin ? new URL(baseAbs) : null;
  117 |     if (abs) {
  118 |       maybeAddSt(abs, input.st);
  119 |       addUtm(abs, method, "post");
  120 |     }
  121 | 
  122 |     const url = abs ? abs.toString() : baseAbs;
  123 | 
  124 |     const who = input.authorName?.trim();
  125 |     const title = who ? `${postTitle} · ${who}` : postTitle;
  126 |     const text = who ? `Read “${postTitle}” by ${who}` : `Read “${postTitle}”`;
  127 | 
  128 |     return {
  129 |       type: "post",
  130 |       postSlug: input.post.slug,
  131 |       postId: input.post.id,
  132 |       title,
  133 |       text,
  134 |       url,
  135 |     };
  136 |   }
  137 | 
  138 |   const artist = input.album.artistName?.trim();
  139 |   const albumTitleRaw = (input.album.title ?? "").toString().trim();
  140 | 
  141 |   // Never treat slug-ish strings as display titles.
  142 |   // If title is missing, keep it generic rather than leaking `god-defend`.
  143 |   const albumTitle =
  144 |     albumTitleRaw.length > 0 && albumTitleRaw.includes("-") === false
  145 |       ? albumTitleRaw
  146 |       : albumTitleRaw.length > 0
  147 |         ? albumTitleRaw // keep non-empty titles even if they contain hyphens
  148 |         : "Album";
  149 | 
  150 |   // ✅ canonical album base path
  151 |   const basePath = `/${encodePathSeg(input.album.slug)}`;
  152 |   const baseAbs = origin ? `${origin}${basePath}` : basePath;
  153 | 
  154 |   if (input.type === "album") {
  155 |     const url = origin
  156 |       ? addUtm(
  157 |           maybeAddSt(new URL(baseAbs), input.st),
  158 |           method,
  159 |           "album",
  160 |         ).toString()
  161 |       : baseAbs;
  162 | 
  163 |     const title = artist ? `${artist} · ${albumTitle}` : albumTitle;
  164 |     const text = artist
  165 |       ? `Listen to ${albumTitle} by ${artist}`
  166 |       : `Listen to ${albumTitle}`;
  167 | 
  168 |     return {
  169 |       type: "album",
  170 |       albumSlug: input.album.slug,
  171 |       albumId: input.album.id,
  172 |       title,
  173 |       text,
  174 |       url,
  175 |     };
  176 |   }
  177 | 
  178 |   // ✅ canonical track path (no query-based player state)
  179 |   const trackTitle = input.track.title?.trim() || "Track";
  180 |   const trackPath = `/${encodePathSeg(input.album.slug)}/${encodePathSeg(
  181 |     input.track.displayId,
  182 |   )}`;

  184 | 
  185 |   const url = origin

  188 |         method,
  189 |         "track",
  190 |       ).toString()
  191 |     : trackAbs;
  192 | 
  193 |   const title = artist
  194 |     ? `${trackTitle} · ${albumTitle} · ${artist}`
  195 |     : `${trackTitle} · ${albumTitle}`;
  196 |   const text = artist
  197 |     ? `Listen to “${trackTitle}” on ${albumTitle} by ${artist}`
  198 |     : `Listen to “${trackTitle}” on ${albumTitle}`;
  199 | 
  200 |   return {
  201 |     type: "track",
  202 |     albumSlug: input.album.slug,
  203 |     albumId: input.album.id,
  204 |     recordingId: input.track.recordingId,
  205 |     displayId: input.track.displayId,
  206 |     trackTitle,
  207 |     title,
  208 |     text,
  209 |     url,
  210 |   };

  217 | function isAbortError(err: unknown) {

  219 |   const anyErr = err as { name?: unknown; message?: unknown };
  220 |   return anyErr.name === "AbortError";

  223 | export async function performShare(target: ShareTarget): Promise<ShareResult> {
  224 |   const url = target.url;
  225 | 
  226 |   if (typeof navigator !== "undefined") {
  227 |     const nav = navigator as Navigator & {
  228 |       share?: (data: {
  229 |         title?: string;
  230 |         text?: string;
  231 |         url?: string;
  232 |       }) => Promise<void>;
  233 |       canShare?: (data?: {
  234 |         title?: string;

  238 |       }) => boolean;
  239 |     };
  240 | 
  241 |     if (typeof nav.share === "function") {
  242 |       const payload = { title: target.title, text: target.text, url };
  243 |       if (typeof nav.canShare !== "function" || nav.canShare(payload)) {
  244 |         try {
  245 |           await nav.share(payload);
  246 |           return { ok: true, method: "native", url };
  247 |         } catch (err) {
  248 |           if (isAbortError(err)) {
  249 |             return { ok: false, reason: "failed", url };
  250 |           }
  251 |           // fall through to clipboard
  252 |         }
  253 |       }
  254 |     }
  255 |   }
  256 | 
  257 |   try {
  258 |     if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
  259 |       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
  260 |       return { ok: true, method: "copy", url };
  261 |     }
  262 |     return { ok: false, reason: "clipboard_unavailable", url };
  263 |   } catch {
  264 |     return { ok: false, reason: "failed", url };
  265 |   }
  266 | }

  107 | export type TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  108 |   ok: true;
  109 |   recordingId: string;
  110 |   offsetMs: number;
  111 |   cues: LyricCue[];
  112 | 
  113 |   // present in API today, but optional for consumers
  114 |   version?: string;
  115 |   geniusUrl?: string | null;
  116 |   groupMap?: LyricGroupMap;
  117 | 
  118 |   // meta fields the API returns (nullable)
  119 |   trackTitle?: string | null;
  120 |   trackArtist?: string | null;
  121 |   trackCatalogueId?: string | null;
  122 |   albumTitle?: string | null;
  123 |   albumSlug?: string | null;
  124 |   albumCatalogueId?: string | null;
  125 | };
  126 | 
  127 | export function normalizeLyricCues(input: unknown): LyricCue[] {
  128 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  129 |   const out: LyricCue[] = [];
  130 | 
  131 |   for (const c of input) {
  132 |     if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
  133 | 
  134 |     const lineKey = (c as { lineKey?: unknown }).lineKey;
  135 |     const tMs = (c as { tMs?: unknown }).tMs;
  136 |     const text = (c as { text?: unknown }).text;
  137 |     const endMs = (c as { endMs?: unknown }).endMs;
  138 |     const canonicalGroupKey = (c as { canonicalGroupKey?: unknown })
  139 |       .canonicalGroupKey;
  140 | 
  141 |     if (typeof lineKey !== "string" || lineKey.trim().length === 0) continue;
  142 |     if (typeof tMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(tMs) || tMs < 0) continue;
  143 |     if (typeof text !== "string" || text.trim().length === 0) continue;
  144 | 
  145 |     const cue: LyricCue = {
  146 |       lineKey: lineKey.trim(),
  147 |       tMs: Math.floor(tMs),
  148 |       text: text.trim(),
  149 |     };
  150 | 
  151 |     if (typeof endMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(endMs) && endMs >= 0) {
  152 |       cue.endMs = Math.floor(endMs);
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     // optional enrichment
  156 |     if (typeof canonicalGroupKey === "string" && canonicalGroupKey.trim()) {
  157 |       cue.canonicalGroupKey = canonicalGroupKey.trim();
  158 |     }
  159 | 
  160 |     out.push(cue);
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   out.sort((a, b) => a.tMs - b.tMs);
  164 |   return out;
  165 | }
  166 | 
  167 | export type SanityLyricCue = {
  168 |   _key?: string;
  169 |   tMs?: number;
  170 |   text?: string;
  171 |   endMs?: number;
  172 | };
  173 | 
  174 | // Adapts Sanity cue shape into canonical cue normalization.
  175 | // (Sanity uses `_key` for the line key.)
  176 | export function normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity(
  177 |   input: SanityLyricCue[] | undefined | null,
  178 | ): LyricCue[] {
  179 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  180 |   return normalizeLyricCues(
  181 |     input.map((c) => ({
  182 |       lineKey: c?._key,
  183 |       tMs: c?.tMs,
  184 |       text: c?.text,
  185 |       endMs: c?.endMs,
  186 |       // canonicalGroupKey not present in Sanity lyrics docs
  187 |     })),
  188 |   );
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | export function parseTrackLyricsApiOk(raw: unknown): TrackLyricsApiOk | null {
  192 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  193 |   const o = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  194 |   if (o.ok !== true) return null;
  195 | 
  196 |   if (typeof o.recordingId !== "string" || !o.recordingId.trim()) return null;
  197 |   if (typeof o.offsetMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(o.offsetMs))
  198 |     return null;
  199 | 
  200 |   const cues = normalizeLyricCues(o.cues);
  201 | 
  202 |   // groupMap is optional; keep it only if it looks like the right shape
  203 |   const groupMap =
  204 |     o.groupMap && typeof o.groupMap === "object" && !Array.isArray(o.groupMap)
  205 |       ? (o.groupMap as LyricGroupMap)
  206 |       : undefined;
  207 | 
  208 |   const out: TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  209 |     ok: true,
  210 |     recordingId: o.recordingId.trim(),
  211 |     offsetMs: Math.floor(o.offsetMs),
  212 |     cues,
  213 |   };
  214 | 
  215 |   // Optional fields (keep them if present, but don’t over-validate here)
  216 |   if (typeof o.version === "string" && o.version.trim())
  217 |     out.version = o.version.trim();
  218 |   if (typeof o.geniusUrl === "string" || o.geniusUrl === null)
  219 |     out.geniusUrl = o.geniusUrl as string | null;
  220 |   if (groupMap) out.groupMap = groupMap;
  221 | 
  222 |   // Nullable meta fields (type-safe, no `any`)
  223 |   const nullableMetaKeys = [
  224 |     "trackTitle",
  225 |     "trackArtist",
  226 |     "trackCatalogueId",
  227 |     "albumTitle",
  228 |     "albumSlug",
  229 |     "albumCatalogueId",
  230 |   ] as const;
  231 | 
  232 |   type NullableMetaKey = (typeof nullableMetaKeys)[number];
  233 | 
  234 |   for (const k of nullableMetaKeys) {
  235 |     const v = o[k];
  236 |     if (typeof v === "string") {
  237 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = v.trim() || null;
  238 |     } else if (v === null) {
  239 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = null;
  240 |     }
  241 |   }
  242 | 
  243 |   return out;
  244 | }
